`src/cef/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/cef/CefEngine.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.cef;

import java.io.File;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

import org.cef.CefApp;
import org.cef.CefClient;
import org.cef.CefSettings;
import org.cef.SystemBootstrap;
import org.cef.browser.CefBrowser;
import org.cef.browser.CefFrame;
import org.cef.browser.OverlayBrowser;
import org.cef.handler.CefDisplayHandlerAdapter;
import org.cef.handler.CefLifeSpanHandlerAdapter;
import org.cef.handler.CefLoadHandlerAdapter;

import dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl.BrowserEngine;

/**
 * The overlay's browser on top of JCEF: one off-screen Chromium view. Created by name from the
 * overlay once JCEF and its natives are downloaded; see {@link #create}.
 */
public final class CefEngine implements BrowserEngine {
    private static CefApp app;

    private final CefClient client;
    private final OverlayBrowser browser;
    private volatile String url = "";
    private volatile String title = "";
    private volatile boolean loading;

    private CefEngine(CefClient client, String start) {
        this.client = client;
        this.url = start;
        client.addDisplayHandler(new CefDisplayHandlerAdapter() {
            @Override
            public void onAddressChange(CefBrowser b, CefFrame frame, String address) {
                if (frame == null || frame.isMain()) {
                    url = address;
                }
            }

            @Override
            public void onTitleChange(CefBrowser b, String newTitle) {
                title = newTitle == null ? "" : newTitle;
            }
        });
        client.addLoadHandler(new CefLoadHandlerAdapter() {
            @Override
            public void onLoadingStateChange(CefBrowser b, boolean isLoading, boolean canGoBack, boolean canGoForward) {
                loading = isLoading;
            }
        });
        client.addLifeSpanHandler(new CefLifeSpanHandlerAdapter() {
            @Override
            public boolean onBeforePopup(CefBrowser b, CefFrame frame, String target, String name) {
                // No windows to pop up into: open links meant for a new tab in this view instead.
                if (target != null && !target.isEmpty()) {
                    b.loadURL(target);
                }
                return true;
            }
        });
        browser = new OverlayBrowser(client, start, null);
        browser.createImmediately();
    }

    /**
     * Starts CEF from the natives in {@code dir} (once per JVM) and opens a browser at {@code start}.
     * {@code cache} keeps cookies and logins between games. Called reflectively by the overlay.
     */
    public static synchronized BrowserEngine create(File dir, File cache, String language, String start) {
        if (app == null) {
            app = start(dir, cache, language);
        }
        return new CefEngine(app.createClient(), start);
    }

    private static CefApp start(File dir, File cache, String language) {
        String os = System.getProperty("os.name", "").toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        boolean windows = os.startsWith("windows");
        // The first run would show Chromium's EULA dialog, which cannot open off screen (exit code 28).
        List<String> args = new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList("--no-first-run", "--disable-gpu"));
        if (!windows) {
            args.add("--no-sandbox");
        }
        String[] argv = args.toArray(new String[0]);
        // Libraries are loaded below by absolute path; JCEF's own loader would search java.library.path.
        SystemBootstrap.setLoader(name -> { });
        if (windows) {
            try {
                System.loadLibrary("jawt");
            } catch (UnsatisfiedLinkError e) {
                System.err.println("[k0t1k-overlay] browser: no jawt (" + e.getMessage() + "), going on");
            }
            System.load(new File(dir, "chrome_elf.dll").getAbsolutePath());
            System.load(new File(dir, "libcef.dll").getAbsolutePath());
            System.load(new File(dir, "jcef.dll").getAbsolutePath());
        } else {
            System.load(new File(dir, "libjcef.so").getAbsolutePath());
            if (!CefApp.startup(argv)) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("CEF did not start");
            }
            System.load(new File(dir, "libcef.so").getAbsolutePath());
        }
        CefSettings settings = new CefSettings();
        settings.windowless_rendering_enabled = true;
        settings.browser_subprocess_path = new File(dir, windows ? "jcef_helper.exe" : "jcef_helper").getAbsolutePath();
        settings.resources_dir_path = dir.getAbsolutePath();
        settings.locales_dir_path = new File(dir, "locales").getAbsolutePath();
        settings.root_cache_path = cache.getAbsolutePath();
        settings.persist_session_cookies = true;
        settings.locale = language;
        settings.log_file = new File(cache, "cef.log").getAbsolutePath();
        settings.log_severity = CefSettings.LogSeverity.LOGSEVERITY_WARNING;
        CefApp instance = CefApp.getInstance(argv, settings);
        // Off screen, JCEF runs CEF with an external message pump on the AWT thread: CEF asks for work
        // through onScheduleMessagePumpWork, and this first call starts the cycle.
        instance.doMessageLoopWork(0);
        return instance;
    }

    @Override
    public void resize(int width, int height) {
        browser.resize(width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public void load(String address) {
        browser.loadURL(address);
    }

    @Override
    public void back() {
        browser.goBack();
    }

    @Override
    public void forward() {
        browser.goForward();
    }

    @Override
    public void reload() {
        browser.reload();
    }

    @Override
    public String url() {
        return url;
    }

    @Override
    public String title() {
        return title;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean loading() {
        return loading;
    }

    @Override
    public void mouse(int id, int x, int y, int modifiers, int clicks, int button) {
        browser.mouse(id, x, y, modifiers, clicks, button);
    }

    @Override
    public void wheel(int x, int y, int modifiers, int amount) {
        browser.wheel(x, y, modifiers, amount);
    }

    @Override
    public void key(int id, int keyCode, char keyChar, int modifiers) {
        browser.key(id, keyCode, keyChar, modifiers);
    }

    @Override
    public void focus(boolean focused) {
        browser.setFocus(focused);
    }

    @Override
    public long frame(long seen, ByteBuffer out, int[] size) {
        return browser.copyFrame(seen, out, size);
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        browser.close(true);
        client.dispose();
    }
}

```

`src/cef/java/org/cef/browser/OverlayBrowser.java`:

```java
package org.cef.browser;

import java.awt.Component;
import java.awt.Point;
import java.awt.Rectangle;
import java.awt.event.KeyEvent;
import java.awt.event.MouseEvent;
import java.awt.event.MouseWheelEvent;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

import org.cef.CefBrowserSettings;
import org.cef.CefClient;
import org.cef.callback.CefDragData;
import org.cef.handler.CefRenderHandler;
import org.cef.handler.CefScreenInfo;

/**
 * An off-screen browser that keeps its pixels in memory instead of painting into an AWT canvas, so a
 * game can draw them. Lives in JCEF's package because the browser base class is package-private.
 */
public final class OverlayBrowser extends CefBrowser_N implements CefRenderHandler {
    /** Source for the AWT input events JCEF wants; never shown, safe in headless mode. */
    private static final Component SOURCE = new Component() {
    };

    private final Object lock = new Object();
    private int width = 800;
    private int height = 600;
    private ByteBuffer pixels;
    private int pixelsWidth;
    private int pixelsHeight;
    private long version;
    private boolean created;
    private boolean settingFocus;

    public OverlayBrowser(CefClient client, String url, CefRequestContext context) {
        super(client, url, context, null, null, new CefBrowserSettings());
    }

    /** Creates the native browser; call once CEF runs. */
    @Override
    public void createImmediately() {
        if (!created) {
            created = true;
            createBrowser(getClient(), 0, getUrl(), true, false, null, getRequestContext());
        }
    }

    public void resize(int w, int h) {
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0 || (w == width && h == height)) {
            return;
        }
        width = w;
        height = h;
        wasResized(w, h);
    }

    /**
     * Copies the newest frame into {@code out} if it is newer than {@code seen} and returns its version.
     * Always fills {@code size}; returns -1 when {@code out} is too small for it.
     */
    public long copyFrame(long seen, ByteBuffer out, int[] size) {
        synchronized (lock) {
            if (pixels == null || version == seen) {
                return seen;
            }
            size[0] = pixelsWidth;
            size[1] = pixelsHeight;
            if (out.capacity() < pixels.limit()) {
                return -1;
            }
            pixels.rewind();
            out.clear();
            out.put(pixels);
            out.flip();
            pixels.rewind();
            return version;
        }
    }

    public void mouse(int id, int x, int y, int modifiers, int clicks, int button) {
        sendMouseEvent(new MouseEvent(SOURCE, id, System.currentTimeMillis(), modifiers, x, y, clicks, false, button));
    }

    /** {@code lines} positive scrolls down. */
    public void wheel(int x, int y, int modifiers, int lines) {
        // JCEF hands CEF getUnitsToScroll() (scroll amount times rotation) as pixels, where positive
        // scrolls up; one line of a desktop mouse wheel is about PIXELS_PER_LINE.
        sendMouseWheelEvent(new MouseWheelEvent(SOURCE, MouseEvent.MOUSE_WHEEL, System.currentTimeMillis(), modifiers,
                x, y, 0, false, MouseWheelEvent.WHEEL_UNIT_SCROLL, PIXELS_PER_LINE, -lines));
    }

    private static final int PIXELS_PER_LINE = 100;

    public void key(int id, int keyCode, char keyChar, int modifiers) {
        sendKeyEvent(new KeyEvent(SOURCE, id, System.currentTimeMillis(), modifiers, keyCode, keyChar));
    }

    /**
     * CefClient.onGotFocus calls setFocus(true), and off screen CEF answers SetFocus with another
     * OnGotFocus on the same stack, which recurses until StackOverflowError; break the cycle here.
     */
    @Override
    public void setFocus(boolean enable) {
        if (settingFocus) {
            return;
        }
        settingFocus = true;
        try {
            super.setFocus(enable);
        } finally {
            settingFocus = false;
        }
    }

    // ---- CefRenderHandler ----

    @Override
    public Rectangle getViewRect(CefBrowser browser) {
        return new Rectangle(0, 0, width, height);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean getScreenInfo(CefBrowser browser, CefScreenInfo info) {
        info.Set(1.0, 32, 8, false, new Rectangle(0, 0, width, height), new Rectangle(0, 0, width, height));
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public Point getScreenPoint(CefBrowser browser, Point viewPoint) {
        return viewPoint;
    }

    @Override
    public void onPopupShow(CefBrowser browser, boolean show) {
    }

    @Override
    public void onPopupSize(CefBrowser browser, Rectangle size) {
    }

    @Override
    public void onPaint(CefBrowser browser, boolean popup, Rectangle[] dirty, ByteBuffer buffer, int w, int h) {
        if (popup) {
            return;
        }
        synchronized (lock) {
            int bytes = w * h * 4;
            if (pixels == null || pixels.capacity() < bytes) {
                pixels = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(bytes);
            }
            pixels.clear();
            ByteBuffer source = buffer.duplicate();
            source.rewind();
            source.limit(Math.min(source.capacity(), bytes));
            pixels.put(source);
            pixels.flip();
            pixelsWidth = w;
            pixelsHeight = h;
            version++;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void addOnPaintListener(Consumer<CefPaintEvent> listener) {
    }

    @Override
    public void setOnPaintListener(Consumer<CefPaintEvent> listener) {
    }

    @Override
    public void removeOnPaintListener(Consumer<CefPaintEvent> listener) {
    }

    @Override
    public boolean onCursorChange(CefBrowser browser, int cursorType) {
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean startDragging(CefBrowser browser, CefDragData dragData, int mask, int x, int y) {
        return false;
    }

    @Override
    public void updateDragCursor(CefBrowser browser, int operation) {
    }

    // ---- CefBrowser ----

    @Override
    public CefRenderHandler getRenderHandler() {
        return this;
    }

    @Override
    public Component getUIComponent() {
        return SOURCE;
    }

    @Override
    protected CefBrowser_N createDevToolsBrowser(CefClient client, String url, CefRequestContext context,
            CefBrowser_N parent, Point inspectAt) {
        return null;
    }

    @Override
    public CompletableFuture<BufferedImage> createScreenshot(boolean nativeResolution) {
        CompletableFuture<BufferedImage> future = new CompletableFuture<>();
        future.completeExceptionally(new UnsupportedOperationException("not supported off screen"));
        return future;
    }
}

```

`src/lwjgl2/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/LegacyGlRenderer.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.nio.IntBuffer;

import org.lwjgl.opengl.ContextCapabilities;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GLContext;

import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12.GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL13.GL_ACTIVE_TEXTURE;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL13.GL_TEXTURE0;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL13.glActiveTexture;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL14.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL15.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL20.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL21.GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL21.GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER_BINDING;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL30.*;

/**
 * Draws a {@link Batch} with OpenGL 3.0 or newer (the compatibility context LWJGL 2 games run in): rounded
 * rectangles as a signed distance in the fragment shader, glyphs from a single-channel atlas.
 *
 * <p>Minecraft caches GL state in {@code GlStateManager}, so every piece of state touched here is read
 * before drawing and put back afterwards; otherwise the game would render with wrong state.
 */
final class LegacyGlRenderer {
    private static final String VERTEX = ""
            + "uniform vec2 uViewport;\n"
            + "in vec2 aPos; in vec2 aUv; in vec4 aColor; in vec4 aParams;\n"
            + "out vec2 vUv; out vec4 vColor; out vec4 vParams;\n"
            + "void main() {\n"
            + "  vUv = aUv; vColor = aColor; vParams = aParams;\n"
            + "  gl_Position = vec4(aPos.x / uViewport.x * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - aPos.y / uViewport.y * 2.0, 0.0, 1.0);\n"
            + "}\n";
    // vParams = (half width, half height, corner radius, mode); mode 1 samples the glyph atlas and
    // vUv is a texture coordinate, mode 0 is a rounded box and vUv is the offset from its centre.
    private static final String FRAGMENT = ""
            + "uniform sampler2D uAtlas;\n"
            + "in vec2 vUv; in vec4 vColor; in vec4 vParams;\n"
            + "out vec4 fragColor;\n"
            + "void main() {\n"
            + "  if (vParams.w > 0.5) {\n"
            + "    fragColor = vec4(vColor.rgb, vColor.a * texture(uAtlas, vUv).r);\n"
            + "    return;\n"
            + "  }\n"
            + "  vec2 q = abs(vUv) - vParams.xy + vParams.z;\n"
            + "  float d = length(max(q, 0.0)) + min(max(q.x, q.y), 0.0) - vParams.z;\n"
            + "  fragColor = vec4(vColor.rgb, vColor.a * clamp(0.5 - d, 0.0, 1.0));\n"
            + "}\n";

    private static final int STRIDE = Batch.STRIDE;
    private static final int MAX_QUADS = Batch.MAX_QUADS;

    private final int program;
    private final int uViewport;
    private final int vao;
    private final int vbo;
    private final int ebo;
    private final int atlas;
    final ContextCapabilities caps;
    private final boolean samplers;
    private int height;

    private final IntBuffer ints = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(16 * 4).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder()).asIntBuffer();
    private final int[] saved = new int[32];
    private final int[] savedViewport = new int[4];
    private final int[] savedScissor = new int[4];
    private final boolean[] savedColorMask = new boolean[4];
    private final ByteBuffer mask = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(16).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
    /** A compatibility context (Minecraft 1.12.2 and older), where fixed-function alpha testing still applies. */
    private final boolean legacy;

    LegacyGlRenderer() {
        caps = GLContext.getCapabilities();
        if (!caps.OpenGL30) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("the overlay needs OpenGL 3.0 or newer, this context has " + glGetString(GL_VERSION));
        }
        samplers = caps.OpenGL33 || caps.GL_ARB_sampler_objects;
        legacy = !caps.OpenGL32
                || (glGetInteger(org.lwjgl.opengl.GL32.GL_CONTEXT_PROFILE_MASK) & org.lwjgl.opengl.GL32.GL_CONTEXT_CORE_PROFILE_BIT) == 0;
        saveState();
        try {
            program = link(compile(GL_VERTEX_SHADER, VERTEX), compile(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER, FRAGMENT));
            uViewport = glGetUniformLocation(program, "uViewport");

            vao = glGenVertexArrays();
            glBindVertexArray(vao);
            vbo = glGenBuffers();
            glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
            int pos = glGetAttribLocation(program, "aPos");
            int uv = glGetAttribLocation(program, "aUv");
            int params = glGetAttribLocation(program, "aParams");
            int color = glGetAttribLocation(program, "aColor");
            glEnableVertexAttribArray(pos);
            glVertexAttribPointer(pos, 2, GL_FLOAT, false, STRIDE, 0);
            glEnableVertexAttribArray(uv);
            glVertexAttribPointer(uv, 2, GL_FLOAT, false, STRIDE, 8);
            glEnableVertexAttribArray(params);
            glVertexAttribPointer(params, 4, GL_FLOAT, false, STRIDE, 16);
            glEnableVertexAttribArray(color);
            glVertexAttribPointer(color, 4, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, true, STRIDE, 32);

            ByteBuffer indices = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(MAX_QUADS * 6 * 4).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
            for (int i = 0; i < MAX_QUADS; i++) {
                int v = i * 4;
                indices.putInt(v).putInt(v + 1).putInt(v + 2).putInt(v + 2).putInt(v + 3).putInt(v);
            }
            indices.flip();
            ebo = glGenBuffers();
            glBindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, ebo);
            glBufferData(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, indices, GL_STATIC_DRAW);

            atlas = glGenTextures();
            glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
            glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, atlas);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL, 0);
            // The game leaves its own unpack state behind; with a large row length the upload would read
            // past the end of our buffer.
            resetUnpack();
            glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL_R8, Batch.ATLAS, Batch.ATLAS, 0, GL_RED, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE,
                    (ByteBuffer) null);

            glUseProgram(program);
            glUniform1i(glGetUniformLocation(program, "uAtlas"), 0);
        } finally {
            restoreState();
        }
    }

    private int compile(int type, String source) {
        int shader = glCreateShader(type);
        // GLSL 1.30 (OpenGL 3.0) takes the same sources as 1.50, which needs OpenGL 3.2.
        glShaderSource(shader, (caps.OpenGL32 ? "#version 150\n" : "#version 130\n") + source);
        glCompileShader(shader);
        if (glGetShaderi(shader, GL_COMPILE_STATUS) == GL_FALSE) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("shader: " + glGetShaderInfoLog(shader, glGetShaderi(shader, GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH)));
        }
        return shader;
    }

    private static int link(int vertex, int fragment) {
        int program = glCreateProgram();
        glAttachShader(program, vertex);
        glAttachShader(program, fragment);
        // Some compatibility drivers draw nothing unless generic attribute 0 is in use.
        glBindAttribLocation(program, 0, "aPos");
        glLinkProgram(program);
        glDeleteShader(vertex);
        glDeleteShader(fragment);
        if (glGetProgrami(program, GL_LINK_STATUS) == GL_FALSE) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("program: " + glGetProgramInfoLog(program, glGetProgrami(program, GL_INFO_LOG_LENGTH)));
        }
        return program;
    }

    /** Draws the batch over the default framebuffer. */
    void draw(Batch batch) {
        height = batch.height;
        saveState();
        try {
            glBindFramebuffer(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER, 0);
            glViewport(0, 0, batch.width, batch.height);
            glDisable(GL_DEPTH_TEST);
            glDisable(GL_CULL_FACE);
            glDisable(GL_STENCIL_TEST);
            glDisable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
            glDisable(GL_COLOR_LOGIC_OP);
            glDisable(GL_POLYGON_OFFSET_FILL);
            glEnable(GL_BLEND);
            glBlendEquation(GL_FUNC_ADD);
            // Leave destination alpha alone: some compositors use the window's alpha channel.
            glBlendFuncSeparate(GL_SRC_ALPHA, GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA, GL_ZERO, GL_ONE);
            glColorMask(true, true, true, true);
            glPolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK, GL_FILL);
            if (legacy) {
                glDisable(GL_ALPHA_TEST);
            }
            glUseProgram(program);
            glUniform2f(uViewport, batch.width, batch.height);
            glBindVertexArray(vao);
            glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
            glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
            glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, atlas);
            if (samplers) {
                org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.glBindSampler(0, 0);
            }
            resetUnpack();
            if (batch.atlasDirty()) {
                int[] d = batch.dirty;
                glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, Batch.ATLAS);
                ByteBuffer region = batch.atlas.duplicate();
                region.position(d[1] * Batch.ATLAS + d[0]);
                glTexSubImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, d[0], d[1], d[2] - d[0], d[3] - d[1], GL_RED, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, region);
                glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, 0);
                batch.atlasUploaded();
            }
            // A fresh store each time, so the driver does not wait for the previous draw.
            glBufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, batch.vertices, GL_STREAM_DRAW);
            for (Batch.Draw d : batch.draws) {
                if (d.clip == null) {
                    glDisable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
                } else {
                    glEnable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
                    glScissor(d.clip[0], height - d.clip[3], d.clip[2] - d.clip[0], d.clip[3] - d.clip[1]);
                }
                glDrawElements(GL_TRIANGLES, d.quads * 6, GL_UNSIGNED_INT, (long) d.firstQuad * 6 * 4);
            }
        } finally {
            restoreState();
        }
    }

    private static void resetUnpack() {
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 1);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, 0);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS, 0);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS, 0);
        glBindBuffer(GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER, 0);
    }

    private int get(int name) {
        return glGetInteger(name);
    }

    private void saveState() {
        int i = 0;
        saved[i++] = get(GL_CURRENT_PROGRAM);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_VERTEX_ARRAY_BINDING);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER_BINDING);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_ACTIVE_TEXTURE);
        glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_TEXTURE_BINDING_2D);
        saved[i++] = samplers ? get(org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.GL_SAMPLER_BINDING) : 0;
        saved[i++] = get(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER_BINDING);
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_BLEND) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_SRC_RGB);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_DST_RGB);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_SRC_ALPHA);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_DST_ALPHA);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_EQUATION_RGB);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_EQUATION_ALPHA);
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_DEPTH_TEST) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_CULL_FACE) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_STENCIL_TEST) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_SCISSOR_TEST) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_COLOR_LOGIC_OP) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_POLYGON_OFFSET_FILL) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = get(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER_BINDING);
        saved[i++] = legacy && glIsEnabled(GL_ALPHA_TEST) ? 1 : 0;
        ints.clear();
        glGetInteger(GL_POLYGON_MODE, ints);
        saved[i] = ints.get(0);
        ints.clear();
        glGetInteger(GL_VIEWPORT, ints);
        ints.get(savedViewport);
        ints.clear();
        glGetInteger(GL_SCISSOR_BOX, ints);
        ints.get(savedScissor);
        mask.clear();
        glGetBoolean(GL_COLOR_WRITEMASK, mask);
        for (int c = 0; c < 4; c++) {
            savedColorMask[c] = mask.get(c) != 0;
        }
    }

    private void restoreState() {
        int i = 0;
        glUseProgram(saved[i++]);
        glBindVertexArray(saved[i++]);
        glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, saved[i++]);
        int activeTexture = saved[i++];
        glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
        glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, saved[i++]);
        int sampler = saved[i++];
        if (samplers) {
            org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.glBindSampler(0, sampler);
        }
        glActiveTexture(activeTexture);
        glBindFramebuffer(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_BLEND, saved[i++]);
        glBlendFuncSeparate(saved[i], saved[i + 1], saved[i + 2], saved[i + 3]);
        i += 4;
        glBlendEquationSeparate(saved[i], saved[i + 1]);
        i += 2;
        toggle(GL_DEPTH_TEST, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_CULL_FACE, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_STENCIL_TEST, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_SCISSOR_TEST, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_COLOR_LOGIC_OP, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_POLYGON_OFFSET_FILL, saved[i++]);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, saved[i++]);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, saved[i++]);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS, saved[i++]);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS, saved[i++]);
        glBindBuffer(GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER, saved[i++]);
        if (legacy) {
            toggle(GL_ALPHA_TEST, saved[i]);
        }
        i++;
        glPolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK, saved[i]);
        glViewport(savedViewport[0], savedViewport[1], savedViewport[2], savedViewport[3]);
        glScissor(savedScissor[0], savedScissor[1], savedScissor[2], savedScissor[3]);
        glColorMask(savedColorMask[0], savedColorMask[1], savedColorMask[2], savedColorMask[3]);
    }

    private static void toggle(int cap, int enabled) {
        if (enabled != 0) {
            glEnable(cap);
        } else {
            glDisable(cap);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/lwjgl2/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/LegacyPlatform.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.awt.Toolkit;
import java.awt.datatransfer.DataFlavor;
import java.awt.datatransfer.StringSelection;
import java.awt.datatransfer.UnsupportedFlavorException;
import java.io.IOException;

import org.lwjgl.input.Keyboard;
import org.lwjgl.input.Mouse;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.Display;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GLContext;

/**
 * LWJGL 2 backend (Minecraft 1.12.2 and older, Java 8). {@code Display.update(boolean)} swaps the
 * buffers and then polls the devices, so the overlay draws on entry. The game reads input by
 * draining the event queues with {@code Keyboard.next()} and {@code Mouse.next()}: the overlay looks
 * at each event first and skips the ones it takes by reading on. Held keys and buttons are
 * reported as released while the overlay is open, and the mouse movement as zero.
 */
final class LegacyPlatform implements LegacyBackend {
    private static final int SHIFT = 1;
    private static final int CONTROL = 2;
    private static final int ALT = 4;
    private static final int SUPER = 8;

    private final Overlay overlay;
    private LegacyGlRenderer renderer;
    /** Set while the overlay itself reads events or changes the grab, so its hooks stay out of it. */
    private int ownCalls;
    /** Modifier keys currently held, as seen in the key events (the game's key state reads released). */
    private int heldMods;
    private boolean savedGrab;
    /** Whether the game wants the mouse grabbed, as of its last call while the overlay held the cursor. */
    private boolean pendingGrab;
    private boolean savedRepeat;

    LegacyPlatform(Overlay overlay) {
        this.overlay = overlay;
    }

    @Override
    public Object call(Class<?> type, String method, Object[] args, Object result, boolean exit) {
        if (ownCalls > 0) {
            return null;
        }
        switch (method) {
            case "update":
                frame();
                return null;
            case "setGrabbed":
                if (!overlay.isOpen()) {
                    return null;
                }
                pendingGrab = (Boolean) args[0];
                return Boolean.TRUE;
            case "next":
                return type == Keyboard.class ? drain(true, (Boolean) result) : drain(false, (Boolean) result);
            case "isKeyDown":
            case "isButtonDown":
                return overlay.isOpen() ? Boolean.FALSE : null;
            case "getDX":
            case "getDY":
            case "getDWheel":
                return overlay.isOpen() ? Integer.valueOf(0) : null;
            default:
                return null;
        }
    }

    private void frame() {
        if (!overlay.buildFrame(Display.getWidth(), Display.getHeight())) {
            return;
        }
        // A shared context (Forge's loading screen runs on its own) has none of our objects.
        if (renderer == null || renderer.caps != GLContext.getCapabilities()) {
            renderer = new LegacyGlRenderer();
            overlay.setRendererName(GL11.glGetString(GL11.GL_RENDERER) + " · OpenGL " + GL11.glGetString(GL11.GL_VERSION));
            int[] dirty = overlay.batch().dirty;
            dirty[0] = dirty[1] = 0;
            dirty[2] = dirty[3] = Batch.ATLAS;
        }
        renderer.draw(overlay.batch());
    }

    // ---- input ----

    /**
     * After the game's {@code next()} returned {@code more}: while the overlay takes the event, reads
     * the next one in its place.
     */
    private Object drain(boolean keyboard, boolean more) {
        ownCalls++;
        try {
            while (more && (keyboard ? takeKey() : takeMouse())) {
                more = keyboard ? Keyboard.next() : Mouse.next();
            }
        } finally {
            ownCalls--;
        }
        return more;
    }

    private boolean takeKey() {
        int code = Keyboard.getEventKey();
        boolean down = Keyboard.getEventKeyState();
        heldMods = withMod(heldMods, code, down);
        int mods = ((heldMods & 0x03) != 0 ? Keys.MOD_SHIFT : 0)
                | ((heldMods & 0x0c) != 0 ? Keys.MOD_CONTROL : 0)
                | ((heldMods & 0x30) != 0 ? Keys.MOD_ALT : 0)
                | ((heldMods & 0xc0) != 0 ? Keys.MOD_SUPER : 0);
        int action = !down ? Keys.RELEASE : Keyboard.isRepeatEvent() ? Keys.REPEAT : Keys.PRESS;
        boolean taken = overlay.key(glfwKey(code), action, mods);
        char typed = Keyboard.getEventCharacter();
        if (down && typed >= ' ' && typed != 127) {
            taken |= overlay.character(typed);
        }
        return taken;
    }

    /** Sets or clears the bit of a modifier key (left and right keys have a bit each). */
    private static int withMod(int held, int code, boolean down) {
        int bit;
        switch (code) {
            case Keyboard.KEY_LSHIFT: bit = 0x01; break;
            case Keyboard.KEY_RSHIFT: bit = 0x02; break;
            case Keyboard.KEY_LCONTROL: bit = 0x04; break;
            case Keyboard.KEY_RCONTROL: bit = 0x08; break;
            case Keyboard.KEY_LMENU: bit = 0x10; break;
            case Keyboard.KEY_RMENU: bit = 0x20; break;
            case Keyboard.KEY_LMETA: bit = 0x40; break;
            case Keyboard.KEY_RMETA: bit = 0x80; break;
            default: return held;
        }
        return down ? held | bit : held & ~bit;
    }

    /** The GLFW code for the keys the overlay uses; any other key gets a code of its own above 1000. */
    private static int glfwKey(int code) {
        switch (code) {
            case Keyboard.KEY_ESCAPE: return Keys.ESCAPE;
            case Keyboard.KEY_RETURN: return Keys.ENTER;
            case Keyboard.KEY_NUMPADENTER: return Keys.KP_ENTER;
            case Keyboard.KEY_TAB: return Keys.TAB;
            case Keyboard.KEY_BACK: return Keys.BACKSPACE;
            case Keyboard.KEY_DELETE: return Keys.DELETE;
            case Keyboard.KEY_RIGHT: return Keys.RIGHT;
            case Keyboard.KEY_LEFT: return Keys.LEFT;
            case Keyboard.KEY_DOWN: return Keys.DOWN;
            case Keyboard.KEY_UP: return Keys.UP;
            case Keyboard.KEY_HOME: return Keys.HOME;
            case Keyboard.KEY_F2: return Keys.F2;
            case Keyboard.KEY_PRIOR: return Keys.PAGE_UP;
            case Keyboard.KEY_NEXT: return Keys.PAGE_DOWN;
            case Keyboard.KEY_END: return Keys.END;
            case Keyboard.KEY_A: return Keys.A;
            case Keyboard.KEY_C: return Keys.C;
            case Keyboard.KEY_V: return Keys.V;
            case Keyboard.KEY_X: return Keys.X;
            default: return 1000 + code;
        }
    }

    private boolean takeMouse() {
        // LWJGL 2 counts y from the bottom of the window.
        boolean moved = overlay.cursor(Mouse.getEventX(), Display.getHeight() - 1 - Mouse.getEventY());
        int button = Mouse.getEventButton();
        if (button >= 0) {
            return overlay.button(button, Mouse.getEventButtonState() ? Keys.PRESS : Keys.RELEASE);
        }
        int wheel = Mouse.getEventDWheel();
        if (wheel != 0) {
            return overlay.scroll(wheel / 120.0);
        }
        return moved;
    }

    // ---- Platform ----

    @Override
    public boolean hasWindow() {
        return Display.isCreated();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean hasGlContext() {
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void sizes(int[] out) {
        out[0] = out[2] = Display.getWidth();
        out[1] = out[3] = Display.getHeight();
    }

    @Override
    public float contentScale() {
        return 1;
    }

    @Override
    public String clipboard() {
        try {
            return (String) Toolkit.getDefaultToolkit().getSystemClipboard().getData(DataFlavor.stringFlavor);
        } catch (UnsupportedFlavorException e) {
            return ""; // the clipboard holds something other than text
        } catch (IOException | IllegalStateException e) {
            overlay.report(e);
            return "";
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        try {
            StringSelection selection = new StringSelection(text);
            Toolkit.getDefaultToolkit().getSystemClipboard().setContents(selection, selection);
        } catch (IllegalStateException e) {
            overlay.report(e);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void showCursor() {
        ownCalls++;
        try {
            savedGrab = Mouse.isGrabbed();
            pendingGrab = savedGrab;
            if (savedGrab) {
                Mouse.setGrabbed(false);
                Mouse.setCursorPosition(Display.getWidth() / 2, Display.getHeight() / 2);
            }
            // The game turns key repeat on only for its own screens; the overlay's text fields want it.
            savedRepeat = Keyboard.areRepeatEventsEnabled();
            Keyboard.enableRepeatEvents(true);
        } finally {
            ownCalls--;
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void restoreCursor() {
        ownCalls++;
        try {
            Keyboard.enableRepeatEvents(savedRepeat);
            if (Mouse.isGrabbed() != pendingGrab) {
                Mouse.setGrabbed(pendingGrab);
            }
            // Movement made while the overlay was open must not turn the camera.
            Mouse.getDX();
            Mouse.getDY();
            Mouse.getDWheel();
        } finally {
            ownCalls--;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/Agent.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay;

import java.lang.instrument.Instrumentation;
import java.net.URL;
import java.net.URLClassLoader;
import java.util.function.Function;

import net.bytebuddy.agent.builder.AgentBuilder;
import net.bytebuddy.asm.Advice;
import net.bytebuddy.description.method.MethodDescription;
import net.bytebuddy.implementation.bytecode.assign.Assigner;
import net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatcher;

import static net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.named;
import static net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.namedOneOf;
import static net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.takesArguments;
import static net.bytebuddy.matcher.ElementMatchers.not;

/**
 * Entry point of {@code -javaagent:k0t1k-overlay.jar[=key=value,...]}.
 *
 * <p>The agent hooks LWJGL, never Minecraft itself, so it works the same on vanilla, Fabric, Forge
 * and NeoForge: the window library (GLFW, or SDL from 26.3 on, or LWJGL 2's Display, Keyboard and
 * Mouse up to 1.12.2) for frames and input, and Vulkan's swapchain functions for games that render
 * with Vulkan (Minecraft's own backend or VulkanMod).
 *
 * <p>Every hooked method calls one function stored in the system properties with
 * {@code {class, method name, arguments}} (plus the return value on exit). JDK types are visible from
 * every class loader, while the agent's own classes may not be (module layers of Forge/NeoForge). The
 * overlay itself is loaded lazily by {@link ImplLoader} as a child of whichever loader defined the
 * hooked LWJGL class, so it links against the game's own copy of LWJGL.
 */
public final class Agent {
    static final String CALL = "dev.k0t1k.overlay.call";

    private static final String[] TYPES = {
        "org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW",
        "org.lwjgl.sdl.SDLVideo",
        "org.lwjgl.sdl.SDLEvents",
        "org.lwjgl.sdl.SDLKeyboard",
        "org.lwjgl.sdl.SDLMouse",
        "org.lwjgl.vulkan.KHRSwapchain",
        "org.lwjgl.vulkan.VK10",
        "org.lwjgl.vulkan.VK11",
        "org.lwjgl.opengl.Display",
        "org.lwjgl.input.Keyboard",
        "org.lwjgl.input.Mouse",
    };

    /** Hooks that run before the method and may replace its arguments or skip it. */
    private static final ElementMatcher<MethodDescription> ENTER = namedOneOf(
            // GLFW (Minecraft 1.13 to 26.2)
            "glfwSwapBuffers",
            "glfwSetKeyCallback", "glfwSetCharCallback", "glfwSetCharModsCallback",
            "glfwSetMouseButtonCallback", "glfwSetCursorPosCallback", "glfwSetScrollCallback",
            "glfwSetInputMode",
            // SDL (Minecraft 26.3+)
            "SDL_GL_SwapWindow", "SDL_SetWindowRelativeMouseMode", "SDL_WarpMouseInWindow",
            "SDL_StartTextInput", "SDL_StopTextInput",
            // Vulkan
            "vkQueuePresentKHR", "vkCreateSwapchainKHR", "vkDestroySwapchainKHR", "vkDestroyDevice",
            // LWJGL 2 (Minecraft 1.12.2 and older): Mouse.setGrabbed
            "setGrabbed")
            // Display.update(boolean) swaps the buffers; update() only calls it.
            .or(named("update").and(takesArguments(boolean.class)));

    /** Hooks that run after the method and may replace its return value. */
    private static final ElementMatcher<MethodDescription> EXIT = namedOneOf(
            "glfwGetKey", "glfwGetMouseButton",
            "SDL_PollEvent",
            // A held-back call reports success, as SDL would have.
            "SDL_SetWindowRelativeMouseMode", "SDL_StartTextInput", "SDL_StopTextInput",
            "vkQueuePresentKHR",
            "vkCreateSwapchainKHR", "vkGetDeviceQueue", "vkGetDeviceQueue2",
            // LWJGL 2: Keyboard and Mouse event queues and state
            "next", "isKeyDown", "isButtonDown", "getDX", "getDY", "getDWheel");

    private Agent() {
    }

    public static void premain(String args, Instrumentation instrumentation) {
        URL jar = Agent.class.getProtectionDomain().getCodeSource().getLocation();
        System.getProperties().put(CALL, new Dispatcher(jar, args == null ? "" : args));

        new AgentBuilder.Default()
                .disableClassFormatChanges()
                .ignore(not(namedOneOf(TYPES)))
                .with(AgentBuilder.Listener.StreamWriting.toSystemError().withErrorsOnly())
                .type(namedOneOf(TYPES))
                .transform((builder, type, loader, module, domain) -> builder
                        .visit(Advice.to(Enter.class).on(ENTER))
                        .visit(Advice.to(Exit.class).on(EXIT)))
                .installOn(instrumentation);
    }

    /**
     * Calls the overlay before the method. The overlay answers null to go on unchanged, an array to
     * replace the arguments, or {@code Boolean.TRUE} to skip the method (it then returns 0/null).
     */
    static final class Enter {
        @Advice.OnMethodEnter(skipOn = Advice.OnNonDefaultValue.class, suppress = Throwable.class)
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        static boolean enter(
                @Advice.Origin Class<?> type,
                @Advice.Origin("#m") String method,
                @Advice.AllArguments(readOnly = false, typing = Assigner.Typing.DYNAMIC) Object[] args) {
            Object hook = System.getProperties().get(CALL);
            if (hook == null) {
                return false;
            }
            Object result = ((Function<Object[], Object>) hook).apply(new Object[] {type, method, args});
            if (result instanceof Object[]) {
                args = (Object[]) result;
                return false;
            }
            return Boolean.TRUE.equals(result);
        }
    }

    /** Calls the overlay after the method; a non-null answer replaces the return value. */
    static final class Exit {
        @Advice.OnMethodExit(suppress = Throwable.class)
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        static void exit(
                @Advice.Origin Class<?> type,
                @Advice.Origin("#m") String method,
                @Advice.AllArguments Object[] args,
                @Advice.Return(readOnly = false, typing = Assigner.Typing.DYNAMIC) Object value) {
            Object hook = System.getProperties().get(CALL);
            if (hook == null) {
                return;
            }
            Object result = ((Function<Object[], Object>) hook).apply(new Object[] {type, method, args, value});
            if (result != null) {
                value = result;
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Creates the overlay on the first hook call, once the class loader of LWJGL is known, and
     * forwards every call to it. Only JDK types cross the class loader boundary.
     */
    static final class Dispatcher implements Function<Object[], Object> {
        private final URL jar;
        private final String args;
        private volatile Function<Object[], Object> impl;
        private boolean failed;

        Dispatcher(URL jar, String args) {
            this.jar = jar;
            this.args = args;
        }

        @Override
        public Object apply(Object[] call) {
            Function<Object[], Object> overlay = impl;
            if (overlay == null) {
                overlay = init((Class<?>) call[0]);
                if (overlay == null) {
                    return null;
                }
            }
            return overlay.apply(call);
        }

        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        private synchronized Function<Object[], Object> init(Class<?> lwjgl) {
            if (impl != null || failed) {
                return impl;
            }
            try {
                ClassLoader loader = new ImplLoader(jar, lwjgl.getClassLoader());
                impl = (Function<Object[], Object>) loader.loadClass("dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl.Overlay")
                        .getConstructor(String.class)
                        .newInstance(args);
                return impl;
            } catch (ReflectiveOperationException | RuntimeException | LinkageError e) {
                failed = true;
                System.err.println("[k0t1k-overlay] disabled, could not start: " + e);
                e.printStackTrace();
                return null;
            }
        }
    }

    /** Child-first for the overlay's own classes, parent (the game's LWJGL loader) for the rest. */
    static final class ImplLoader extends URLClassLoader {
        private static final String IMPL = "dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl.";

        static {
            registerAsParallelCapable();
        }

        ImplLoader(URL jar, ClassLoader parent) {
            super(new URL[] {jar}, parent);
        }

        @Override
        protected Class<?> loadClass(String name, boolean resolve) throws ClassNotFoundException {
            if (!name.startsWith(IMPL)) {
                return super.loadClass(name, resolve);
            }
            synchronized (getClassLoadingLock(name)) {
                Class<?> loaded = findLoadedClass(name);
                if (loaded == null) {
                    loaded = findClass(name);
                }
                if (resolve) {
                    resolveClass(loaded);
                }
                return loaded;
            }
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/CrashDialog.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay;

import java.awt.BorderLayout;
import java.awt.Color;
import java.awt.Desktop;
import java.awt.Dimension;
import java.awt.FlowLayout;
import java.awt.Font;
import java.awt.Toolkit;
import java.awt.datatransfer.StringSelection;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.URL;
import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

import javax.swing.BorderFactory;
import javax.swing.JButton;
import javax.swing.JFrame;
import javax.swing.JLabel;
import javax.swing.JPanel;
import javax.swing.JScrollPane;
import javax.swing.JTextArea;
import javax.swing.SwingUtilities;
import javax.swing.SwingWorker;
import javax.swing.WindowConstants;

/**
 * The crash window. The overlay starts it in a JVM of its own from the game's shutdown hook when the
 * game wrote a crash report, so it stays up after the game is gone. It shows the reason and offers to
 * upload the report (plus the overlay's notes from the dying game: a thread dump and uncaught
 * exceptions) to mclo.gs for support. Nothing leaves the computer unless the player clicks.
 *
 * <p>Arguments: {@code lang report bundle supportUrl}.
 */
public final class CrashDialog {
    private static final Color BG = new Color(0x13101c);
    private static final Color RAISED = new Color(0x1b1728);
    private static final Color FG = new Color(0xece8f6);
    private static final Color MUTED = new Color(0xa199ba);
    private static final Color ACCENT = new Color(0xa259f7);

    private CrashDialog() {
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) throws IOException {
        boolean ru = args.length > 0 && args[0].startsWith("ru");
        Path report = Paths.get(args[1]);
        Path bundle = args.length > 2 && !args[2].isEmpty() ? Paths.get(args[2]) : null;
        String support = args.length > 3 ? args[3] : "";
        String text = new String(Files.readAllBytes(report), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        String extra = bundle != null && Files.exists(bundle) ? new String(Files.readAllBytes(bundle), StandardCharsets.UTF_8) : "";
        SwingUtilities.invokeLater(() -> show(ru, report, text, extra, support));
    }

    private static void show(boolean ru, Path report, String text, String extra, String support) {
        JFrame frame = new JFrame(ru ? "Minecraft вылетел — _k0t1k" : "Minecraft crashed — _k0t1k");
        frame.setDefaultCloseOperation(WindowConstants.DISPOSE_ON_CLOSE);
        JPanel root = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 12));
        root.setBackground(BG);
        root.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(18, 18, 18, 18));

        JPanel head = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 6));
        head.setOpaque(false);
        JLabel title = new JLabel(ru ? "Игра вылетела" : "The game crashed");
        title.setForeground(FG);
        title.setFont(title.getFont().deriveFont(Font.BOLD, 20f));
        JLabel reason = new JLabel("<html>" + escape(reason(text)) + "</html>");
        reason.setForeground(MUTED);
        head.add(title, BorderLayout.NORTH);
        head.add(reason, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        root.add(head, BorderLayout.NORTH);

        JTextArea area = new JTextArea(text + (extra.isEmpty() ? "" : "\n\n" + extra));
        area.setEditable(false);
        area.setFont(new Font(Font.MONOSPACED, Font.PLAIN, 12));
        area.setBackground(RAISED);
        area.setForeground(FG);
        area.setCaretPosition(0);
        JScrollPane scroll = new JScrollPane(area);
        scroll.setBorder(BorderFactory.createLineBorder(new Color(0x2b2542)));
        scroll.setPreferredSize(new Dimension(820, 420));
        root.add(scroll, BorderLayout.CENTER);

        JPanel buttons = new JPanel(new FlowLayout(FlowLayout.RIGHT, 8, 0));
        buttons.setOpaque(false);
        JLabel status = new JLabel(" ");
        status.setForeground(MUTED);
        JButton upload = button(ru ? "Отправить в сапорт" : "Send to support", true);
        upload.addActionListener(e -> {
            upload.setEnabled(false);
            status.setText(ru ? "Загружаю отчёт на mclo.gs…" : "Uploading the report to mclo.gs…");
            new SwingWorker<String, Void>() {
                @Override
                protected String doInBackground() throws IOException {
                    return uploadToMclogs(text + (extra.isEmpty() ? "" : "\n\n" + extra));
                }

                @Override
                protected void done() {
                    try {
                        String url = get();
                        Toolkit.getDefaultToolkit().getSystemClipboard().setContents(new StringSelection(url), null);
                        status.setText((ru ? "Ссылка скопирована: " : "Link copied: ") + url);
                        if (!support.isEmpty()) {
                            browse(support);
                        }
                    } catch (Exception ex) {
                        status.setText((ru ? "Не удалось загрузить: " : "Upload failed: ") + ex.getMessage());
                        upload.setEnabled(true);
                    }
                }
            }.execute();
        });
        JButton copy = button(ru ? "Скопировать отчёт" : "Copy report", false);
        copy.addActionListener(e -> {
            Toolkit.getDefaultToolkit().getSystemClipboard().setContents(new StringSelection(text), null);
            status.setText(ru ? "Отчёт скопирован" : "Report copied");
        });
        JButton folder = button(ru ? "Открыть папку" : "Open folder", false);
        folder.addActionListener(e -> {
            try {
                Desktop.getDesktop().open(report.getParent().toFile());
            } catch (IOException | RuntimeException ex) {
                status.setText(ex.getMessage());
            }
        });
        JButton close = button(ru ? "Закрыть" : "Close", false);
        close.addActionListener(e -> frame.dispose());
        buttons.add(folder);
        buttons.add(copy);
        buttons.add(upload);
        buttons.add(close);

        JPanel bottom = new JPanel(new BorderLayout(0, 8));
        bottom.setOpaque(false);
        bottom.add(status, BorderLayout.NORTH);
        bottom.add(buttons, BorderLayout.SOUTH);
        JLabel privacy = new JLabel(ru ? "Отчёт уходит на mclo.gs только по кнопке «Отправить в сапорт»."
                : "The report goes to mclo.gs only when you click Send to support.");
        privacy.setForeground(new Color(0x6d6588));
        bottom.add(privacy, BorderLayout.CENTER);
        root.add(bottom, BorderLayout.SOUTH);

        frame.setContentPane(root);
        frame.pack();
        frame.setLocationRelativeTo(null);
        frame.setAlwaysOnTop(true);
        frame.setVisible(true);
        frame.toFront();
        frame.setAlwaysOnTop(false);
    }

    private static JButton button(String label, boolean primary) {
        JButton button = new JButton(label);
        button.setFocusPainted(false);
        button.setBackground(primary ? ACCENT : RAISED);
        button.setForeground(primary ? Color.WHITE : FG);
        button.setBorder(BorderFactory.createEmptyBorder(8, 14, 8, 14));
        return button;
    }

    /** "Description: ..." and the first exception line of a Minecraft crash report. */
    static String reason(String report) {
        String description = "";
        String exception = "";
        for (String line : report.split("\n")) {
            String trimmed = line.trim();
            if (description.isEmpty() && trimmed.startsWith("Description:")) {
                description = trimmed.substring("Description:".length()).trim();
            } else if (!description.isEmpty() && exception.isEmpty() && !trimmed.isEmpty()) {
                exception = trimmed;
                break;
            }
        }
        String reason = description.isEmpty() ? "" : description + (exception.isEmpty() ? "" : " — " + exception);
        return reason.length() > 300 ? reason.substring(0, 300) + "…" : reason;
    }

    private static String escape(String text) {
        return text.replace("&", "&amp;").replace("<", "&lt;").replace(">", "&gt;");
    }

    /** Uploads to mclo.gs and returns the page address. */
    static String uploadToMclogs(String content) throws IOException {
        HttpURLConnection connection = (HttpURLConnection) new URL("https://api.mclo.gs/1/log").openConnection();
        connection.setRequestMethod("POST");
        connection.setDoOutput(true);
        connection.setConnectTimeout(15000);
        connection.setReadTimeout(30000);
        connection.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded");
        byte[] body = ("content=" + URLEncoder.encode(content, "UTF-8")).getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        try (OutputStream out = connection.getOutputStream()) {
            out.write(body);
        }
        String response;
        try (InputStream in = connection.getResponseCode() < 400 ? connection.getInputStream() : connection.getErrorStream()) {
            ByteArrayOutputStream bytes = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            byte[] buffer = new byte[4096];
            for (int n; in != null && (n = in.read(buffer)) > 0; ) {
                bytes.write(buffer, 0, n);
            }
            response = new String(bytes.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        }
        Matcher url = Pattern.compile("\"url\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]+)\"").matcher(response);
        if (!url.find()) {
            throw new IOException("mclo.gs answered " + connection.getResponseCode() + ": " + response);
        }
        return url.group(1).replace("\\/", "/");
    }

    private static void browse(String url) {
        try {
            if (url.startsWith("https://") && Desktop.isDesktopSupported()) {
                Desktop.getDesktop().browse(URI.create(url));
            }
        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
            System.err.println("[k0t1k-overlay] could not open " + url + ": " + e);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/AviWriter.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.BufferedOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Writes JPEG frames as a Motion-JPEG AVI, a video every player opens (VLC, the Windows player, mpv)
 * without an encoder: the frames are stored as they are, with a RIFF header and an index.
 */
final class AviWriter {
    private AviWriter() {
    }

    static void write(Path file, int width, int height, int fps, List<byte[]> frames) throws IOException {
        int moviSize = 4;
        int largest = 0;
        for (byte[] frame : frames) {
            moviSize += 8 + padded(frame.length);
            largest = Math.max(largest, frame.length);
        }
        int hdrlSize = 4 + (8 + 56) + (8 + 4 + (8 + 56) + (8 + 40));
        int idxSize = frames.size() * 16;
        int riffSize = 4 + (8 + hdrlSize) + (8 + moviSize) + (8 + idxSize);

        try (OutputStream out = new BufferedOutputStream(Files.newOutputStream(file))) {
            ByteBuffer b = ByteBuffer.allocate(8 + 12 + hdrlSize + 12).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
            fourcc(b, "RIFF");
            b.putInt(riffSize);
            fourcc(b, "AVI ");
            fourcc(b, "LIST");
            b.putInt(hdrlSize);
            fourcc(b, "hdrl");
            // Main AVI header.
            fourcc(b, "avih");
            b.putInt(56);
            b.putInt(1_000_000 / fps); // microseconds per frame
            b.putInt(largest * fps); // max bytes per second
            b.putInt(0); // padding granularity
            b.putInt(0x10); // AVIF_HASINDEX
            b.putInt(frames.size());
            b.putInt(0); // initial frames
            b.putInt(1); // streams
            b.putInt(largest);
            b.putInt(width);
            b.putInt(height);
            b.putInt(0).putInt(0).putInt(0).putInt(0);
            // The one video stream.
            fourcc(b, "LIST");
            b.putInt(4 + (8 + 56) + (8 + 40));
            fourcc(b, "strl");
            fourcc(b, "strh");
            b.putInt(56);
            fourcc(b, "vids");
            fourcc(b, "MJPG");
            b.putInt(0); // flags
            b.putShort((short) 0).putShort((short) 0); // priority, language
            b.putInt(0); // initial frames
            b.putInt(1); // scale
            b.putInt(fps); // rate: fps frames per second
            b.putInt(0); // start
            b.putInt(frames.size()); // length
            b.putInt(largest);
            b.putInt(-1); // quality
            b.putInt(0); // sample size
            b.putShort((short) 0).putShort((short) 0).putShort((short) width).putShort((short) height);
            fourcc(b, "strf");
            b.putInt(40); // BITMAPINFOHEADER
            b.putInt(40);
            b.putInt(width);
            b.putInt(height);
            b.putShort((short) 1);
            b.putShort((short) 24);
            fourcc(b, "MJPG");
            b.putInt(width * height * 3);
            b.putInt(0).putInt(0).putInt(0).putInt(0);
            fourcc(b, "LIST");
            b.putInt(moviSize);
            fourcc(b, "movi");
            out.write(b.array(), 0, b.position());

            ByteBuffer chunk = ByteBuffer.allocate(8).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
            for (byte[] frame : frames) {
                chunk.clear();
                fourcc(chunk, "00dc");
                chunk.putInt(frame.length);
                out.write(chunk.array());
                out.write(frame);
                if ((frame.length & 1) != 0) {
                    out.write(0);
                }
            }

            ByteBuffer index = ByteBuffer.allocate(8 + idxSize).order(ByteOrder.LITTLE_ENDIAN);
            fourcc(index, "idx1");
            index.putInt(idxSize);
            int offset = 4; // offsets count from the "movi" fourcc
            for (byte[] frame : frames) {
                fourcc(index, "00dc");
                index.putInt(0x10); // AVIIF_KEYFRAME
                index.putInt(offset);
                index.putInt(frame.length);
                offset += 8 + padded(frame.length);
            }
            out.write(index.array());
        }
    }

    private static int padded(int length) {
        return length + (length & 1);
    }

    private static void fourcc(ByteBuffer b, String code) {
        b.put(code.getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/AwtFont.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.awt.Color;
import java.awt.FontFormatException;
import java.awt.Graphics2D;
import java.awt.RenderingHints;
import java.awt.font.FontRenderContext;
import java.awt.font.GlyphVector;
import java.awt.font.LineMetrics;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.awt.image.DataBufferByte;
import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;

/**
 * A font rasterised with java.awt, for Minecraft versions on LWJGL 2, which has no stb_truetype.
 * Sizes follow {@link StbFont}: a pixel size is the height from descent to ascent, not the em size.
 */
final class AwtFont extends Font {
    private static final float REFERENCE = 1000;
    private static final FontRenderContext CONTEXT = new FontRenderContext(null, true, true);

    private final java.awt.Font base;
    /** Ascent and line height per unit of the pixel size. */
    private final float ascent;
    private final float lineHeight;
    /** Em size per unit of the pixel size. */
    private final float em;

    AwtFont(String resource, Atlas atlas) throws IOException {
        super(atlas);
        try {
            base = java.awt.Font.createFont(java.awt.Font.TRUETYPE_FONT, new ByteArrayInputStream(Resources.read(resource)));
        } catch (FontFormatException e) {
            throw new IOException("unreadable font " + resource, e);
        }
        LineMetrics metrics = base.deriveFont(REFERENCE).getLineMetrics("", CONTEXT);
        float height = metrics.getAscent() + metrics.getDescent();
        ascent = metrics.getAscent() / height;
        lineHeight = (height + metrics.getLeading()) / height;
        em = REFERENCE / height;
    }

    @Override
    float ascent(float px) {
        return ascent * px;
    }

    @Override
    float lineHeight(float px) {
        return lineHeight * px;
    }

    @Override
    boolean has(int codepoint) {
        return base.canDisplay(codepoint);
    }

    /** No kerning table is read; pairs are spaced by their advances alone. */
    @Override
    float kern(int left, int right, float px) {
        return 0;
    }

    @Override
    Glyph rasterise(int codepoint, float px) {
        java.awt.Font sized = base.deriveFont(px * em);
        GlyphVector vector = sized.createGlyphVector(CONTEXT, new String(Character.toChars(codepoint)));
        float advance = vector.getGlyphMetrics(0).getAdvanceX();
        java.awt.Rectangle box = vector.getGlyphPixelBounds(0, CONTEXT, 0, 0);
        int w = box.width;
        int h = box.height;
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) {
            return new Glyph(advance, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
        }
        BufferedImage image = new BufferedImage(w, h, BufferedImage.TYPE_BYTE_GRAY);
        Graphics2D g = image.createGraphics();
        try {
            g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_ANTIALIASING, RenderingHints.VALUE_ANTIALIAS_ON);
            g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_FRACTIONALMETRICS, RenderingHints.VALUE_FRACTIONALMETRICS_ON);
            g.setColor(Color.WHITE);
            g.drawGlyphVector(vector, -box.x, -box.y);
        } finally {
            g.dispose();
        }
        ByteBuffer pixels = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(w * h);
        pixels.put(((DataBufferByte) image.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).getData(), 0, w * h).flip();
        return place(advance, box.x, box.y, w, h, pixels);
    }
}
```
