`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Batch.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * One frame of overlay geometry, built on the CPU and handed to whichever graphics API the game
 * uses: rounded rectangles and glyph quads in framebuffer pixels, split into draws by clip
 * rectangle, plus the glyph atlas pixels with the region changed since the last upload.
 *
 * <p>Vertex layout ({@link #STRIDE} bytes): position (2 floats), uv (2 floats), params (4 floats:
 * half width, half height, corner radius, mode) and colour (RGBA bytes). Mode 1 samples the atlas and
 * uv is a texture coordinate; mode 2 samples the {@link #image} (the browser's page, BGRA) the same way;
 * mode 0 is a rounded box and uv is the offset from its centre. Quads are drawn as the index pattern
 * 0 1 2 2 3 0.
 */
final class Batch {
    static final int STRIDE = (2 + 2 + 4) * 4 + 4;
    static final int MAX_QUADS = 32768;
    static final int ATLAS = 2048;

    /** A run of quads sharing one clip; the clip is {x0, y0, x1, y1} in pixels, or null for none. */
    static final class Draw {
        final int firstQuad;
        int quads;
        final int[] clip;

        Draw(int firstQuad, int[] clip) {
            this.firstQuad = firstQuad;
            this.clip = clip;
        }
    }

    final ByteBuffer vertices = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(MAX_QUADS * 4 * STRIDE).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
    final List<Draw> draws = new ArrayList<>();
    int quads;
    int width;
    int height;
    private final ArrayDeque<int[]> clips = new ArrayDeque<>();
    private boolean overflowReported;

    /** Atlas pixels, one byte of coverage each, row-major {@link #ATLAS} x {@link #ATLAS}. */
    final ByteBuffer atlas = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(ATLAS * ATLAS);
    /** The atlas region changed since the renderer last uploaded it, {x0, y0, x1, y1}; x0 > x1 when clean. */
    final int[] dirty = {ATLAS, ATLAS, 0, 0};

    /** One picture per frame for mode 2 quads: BGRA, top-down rows, replaced when its version changes. */
    ByteBuffer image;
    int imageWidth;
    int imageHeight;
    long imageVersion;

    void begin(int width, int height) {
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
        vertices.clear();
        draws.clear();
        clips.clear();
        quads = 0;
    }

    /** Finishes the frame: the vertex buffer is ready to read from position 0 to its limit. */
    void end() {
        vertices.flip();
    }

    boolean empty() {
        return quads == 0;
    }

    boolean atlasDirty() {
        return dirty[0] < dirty[2];
    }

    void atlasUploaded() {
        dirty[0] = dirty[1] = ATLAS;
        dirty[2] = dirty[3] = 0;
    }

    /** Copies a glyph bitmap ({@code w} x {@code h}, tightly packed) into the atlas at x, y. */
    void putGlyph(int x, int y, int w, int h, ByteBuffer pixels) {
        for (int row = 0; row < h; row++) {
            for (int col = 0; col < w; col++) {
                atlas.put((y + row) * ATLAS + x + col, pixels.get(row * w + col));
            }
        }
        dirty[0] = Math.min(dirty[0], x);
        dirty[1] = Math.min(dirty[1], y);
        dirty[2] = Math.max(dirty[2], x + w);
        dirty[3] = Math.max(dirty[3], y + h);
    }

    /** Restricts drawing to a rectangle (framebuffer pixels), intersected with the enclosing clip. */
    void pushClip(float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        int x0 = (int) Math.floor(x);
        int y0 = (int) Math.floor(y);
        int x1 = (int) Math.ceil(x + w);
        int y1 = (int) Math.ceil(y + h);
        int[] outer = clips.peek();
        if (outer != null) {
            x0 = Math.max(x0, outer[0]);
            y0 = Math.max(y0, outer[1]);
            x1 = Math.min(x1, outer[2]);
            y1 = Math.min(y1, outer[3]);
        }
        clips.push(new int[] {x0, y0, Math.max(x0, x1), Math.max(y0, y1)});
    }

    void popClip() {
        clips.pop();
    }

    void rect(float x, float y, float w, float h, int argb) {
        roundRect(x, y, w, h, 0, argb);
    }

    void roundRect(float x, float y, float w, float h, float radius, int argb) {
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0 || (argb >>> 24) == 0) {
            return;
        }
        float hw = w / 2;
        float hh = h / 2;
        float r = Math.min(radius, Math.min(hw, hh));
        // Grow by half a pixel so the anti-aliased edge is not cut off.
        float g = 0.5f;
        quad(x - g, y - g, x + w + g, y + h + g, -hw - g, -hh - g, hw + g, hh + g, hw, hh, r, 0, argb);
    }

    void glyph(float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1, float u0, float v0, float u1, float v1, int argb) {
        quad(x0, y0, x1, y1, u0, v0, u1, v1, 0, 0, 0, 1, argb);
    }

    /** Draws the whole {@link #image} into the rectangle; the colour's alpha fades it. */
    void image(float x, float y, float w, float h, int argb) {
        quad(x, y, x + w, y + h, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0, 2, argb);
    }

    private void quad(float x0, float y0, float x1, float y1, float u0, float v0, float u1, float v1,
            float hw, float hh, float radius, float mode, int argb) {
        if (quads == MAX_QUADS) {
            if (!overflowReported) {
                overflowReported = true;
                Overlay.log("frame has more than " + MAX_QUADS + " quads; the rest is not drawn");
            }
            return;
        }
        int[] clip = clips.peek();
        Draw last = draws.isEmpty() ? null : draws.get(draws.size() - 1);
        if (last == null || last.clip != clip) {
            last = new Draw(quads, clip);
            draws.add(last);
        }
        last.quads++;
        vertex(x0, y0, u0, v0, hw, hh, radius, mode, argb);
        vertex(x1, y0, u1, v0, hw, hh, radius, mode, argb);
        vertex(x1, y1, u1, v1, hw, hh, radius, mode, argb);
        vertex(x0, y1, u0, v1, hw, hh, radius, mode, argb);
        quads++;
    }

    private void vertex(float x, float y, float u, float v, float hw, float hh, float radius, float mode, int argb) {
        vertices.putFloat(x).putFloat(y).putFloat(u).putFloat(v)
                .putFloat(hw).putFloat(hh).putFloat(radius).putFloat(mode)
                .put((byte) (argb >> 16)).put((byte) (argb >> 8)).put((byte) argb).put((byte) (argb >>> 24));
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Browser.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.BufferedInputStream;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.net.HttpURLConnection;
import java.net.URL;
import java.net.URLClassLoader;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
import java.security.DigestInputStream;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
import java.util.zip.GZIPInputStream;
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry;
import java.util.zip.ZipInputStream;

/**
 * The in-game browser's engine: Chromium through JCEF, which is too big to ship in the agent (about
 * 170 MB per platform). The first time the player asks for it, JCEF and its natives are downloaded from
 * Maven Central (jcefmaven's builds), checked against the SHA-256 pinned here, and unpacked into
 * {@code <dataDir>/browser}; later games start it from there. The bridge classes ({@code src/cef}) ship
 * inside the agent as {@code cef-bridge.jar} and load next to JCEF in a class loader of their own.
 */
final class Browser {
    enum State { MISSING, INSTALLING, STARTING, READY, FAILED, UNSUPPORTED }

    private static final String VERSION = "jcef-17e805a+cef-152.0.6+g708dc14+chromium-152.0.7977.83";
    private static final String MAVEN = "https://repo1.maven.org/maven2/me/friwi/";
    private static final String API_SHA256 = "c95083e2dd36272d98db4b08ba7b4963bcab02f06c52e7c921fab17728834aeb";
    /** Natives per platform: {artifact, SHA-256, approximate size in MB}. */
    private static final String[][] NATIVES = {
        {"windows-amd64", "0dc2ce28766d267f551398e24af7a9bec244b888e651bd97a14ac2d128550875", "171"},
        {"linux-amd64", "83f427992cd783fb6f3d499fdf275675187818aa7ca9c4a2a4d43af534a8bdfc", "152"},
    };

    volatile State state;
    /** 0..1 while installing. */
    volatile float progress;
    volatile String error = "";
    private volatile BrowserEngine engine;
    /** An install or start is under way on the io thread. */
    private volatile boolean busy;

    private final Path root;
    private final Path natives;
    private final Executor io;
    private final String language;
    private final String[] platform;

    Browser(Path dataDir, Executor io, String language) {
        this.root = dataDir.resolve("browser");
        this.natives = root.resolve("cef-152.0.6");
        this.io = io;
        this.language = language;
        this.platform = platform();
        if (platform == null) {
            state = State.UNSUPPORTED;
        } else {
            state = Files.exists(natives.resolve(".installed")) ? State.STARTING : State.MISSING;
        }
    }

    private static String[] platform() {
        String os = System.getProperty("os.name", "").toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        String arch = System.getProperty("os.arch", "").toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!arch.equals("amd64") && !arch.equals("x86_64")) {
            return null;
        }
        String name = os.startsWith("windows") ? "windows-amd64" : os.startsWith("linux") ? "linux-amd64" : null;
        for (String[] entry : NATIVES) {
            if (entry[0].equals(name)) {
                return entry;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    /** Download size in MB for the install button. */
    String size() {
        return platform == null ? "" : platform[2];
    }

    BrowserEngine engine() {
        return engine;
    }

    /** Installs if needed and starts the engine at {@code url}; progress shows in {@link #state}. */
    void start(String url) {
        if (busy || engine != null || state == State.UNSUPPORTED) {
            return;
        }
        busy = true;
        boolean install = !Files.exists(natives.resolve(".installed"));
        state = install ? State.INSTALLING : State.STARTING;
        progress = 0;
        io.execute(() -> {
            try {
                if (install) {
                    install();
                }
                state = State.STARTING;
                engine = load(url);
                state = State.READY;
            } catch (IOException | ReflectiveOperationException | RuntimeException | LinkageError e) {
                Throwable cause = e instanceof java.lang.reflect.InvocationTargetException ? e.getCause() : e;
                error = String.valueOf(cause);
                state = State.FAILED;
                System.err.println("[k0t1k-overlay] browser failed: " + cause);
                cause.printStackTrace();
            } finally {
                busy = false;
            }
        });
    }

    void close() {
        BrowserEngine current = engine;
        if (current != null) {
            current.close();
        }
    }

    // ---- install ----

    private void install() throws IOException {
        Files.createDirectories(root);
        Path api = root.resolve("jcef-api.jar");
        if (!Files.exists(api)) {
            download("jcef-api", API_SHA256, api, 0, 0.02f);
        }
        Path nativeJar = root.resolve("natives-download.jar");
        download("jcef-natives-" + platform[0], platform[1], nativeJar, 0.02f, 0.8f);
        Path unpacking = root.resolve("cef-unpacking");
        deleteTree(unpacking);
        Files.createDirectories(unpacking);
        unpack(nativeJar, unpacking);
        deleteTree(natives);
        Files.move(unpacking, natives);
        Files.delete(nativeJar);
        Files.write(natives.resolve(".installed"), VERSION.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        progress = 1;
    }

    /** Downloads one Maven Central jar of this JCEF version and checks its SHA-256. */
    private void download(String artifact, String sha256, Path target, float from, float to) throws IOException {
        String version = java.net.URLEncoder.encode(VERSION, "UTF-8");
        URL url = new URL(MAVEN + artifact + "/" + version + "/" + artifact + "-" + version + ".jar");
        HttpURLConnection connection = (HttpURLConnection) url.openConnection();
        connection.setConnectTimeout(15000);
        connection.setReadTimeout(60000);
        if (connection.getResponseCode() != 200) {
            throw new IOException(url + " answered " + connection.getResponseCode());
        }
        long total = connection.getContentLengthLong();
        MessageDigest digest = sha256();
        Path part = target.resolveSibling(target.getFileName() + ".part");
        try (InputStream in = new DigestInputStream(new BufferedInputStream(connection.getInputStream()), digest);
                OutputStream out = Files.newOutputStream(part)) {
            byte[] buffer = new byte[1 << 16];
            long done = 0;
            for (int n; (n = in.read(buffer)) > 0; ) {
                out.write(buffer, 0, n);
                done += n;
                if (total > 0) {
                    progress = from + (to - from) * done / total;
                }
            }
        }
        String actual = hex(digest.digest());
        if (!actual.equals(sha256)) {
            Files.deleteIfExists(part);
            throw new IOException(artifact + " does not match its pinned SHA-256 (got " + actual + ")");
        }
        Files.move(part, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
    }

    /** The natives jar holds one .tar.gz; unpacks it with plain java.util.zip and a small tar reader. */
    private void unpack(Path jar, Path into) throws IOException {
        long total = Files.size(jar);
        try (InputStream file = Files.newInputStream(jar); ZipInputStream zip = new ZipInputStream(new BufferedInputStream(file))) {
            for (ZipEntry entry; (entry = zip.getNextEntry()) != null; ) {
                if (entry.getName().endsWith(".tar.gz")) {
                    untar(new GZIPInputStream(zip, 1 << 16), into, total);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
        throw new IOException("no .tar.gz in " + jar);
    }

    private void untar(InputStream in, Path into, long total) throws IOException {
        byte[] header = new byte[512];
        String longName = null;
        long unpacked = 0;
        Path base = into.toAbsolutePath().normalize();
        while (readFully(in, header)) {
            if (header[0] == 0) {
                return; // end of archive
            }
            String name = longName != null ? longName : string(header, 0, 100);
            longName = null;
            String prefix = string(header, 345, 155);
            if (!prefix.isEmpty()) {
                name = prefix + "/" + name;
            }
            long size = Long.parseLong(string(header, 124, 12).trim().isEmpty() ? "0" : string(header, 124, 12).trim(), 8);
            int mode = Integer.parseInt(string(header, 100, 8).trim().isEmpty() ? "0" : string(header, 100, 8).trim(), 8);
            char type = (char) header[156];
            long padded = (size + 511) / 512 * 512;
            if (type == 'L') {
                byte[] data = new byte[(int) padded];
                readFully(in, data);
                longName = new String(data, 0, (int) size, StandardCharsets.UTF_8).replace("\0", "");
                continue;
            }
            Path target = base.resolve(name).normalize();
            if (!target.startsWith(base)) {
                throw new IOException("archive entry escapes the install folder: " + name);
            }
            if (type == '5') {
                Files.createDirectories(target);
                skip(in, padded);
            } else if (type == '0' || type == 0) {
                Files.createDirectories(target.getParent());
                try (OutputStream out = Files.newOutputStream(target)) {
                    byte[] buffer = new byte[1 << 16];
                    long left = size;
                    while (left > 0) {
                        int n = in.read(buffer, 0, (int) Math.min(buffer.length, left));
                        if (n < 0) {
                            throw new IOException("archive ends inside " + name);
                        }
                        out.write(buffer, 0, n);
                        left -= n;
                    }
                }
                skip(in, padded - size);
                if ((mode & 0100) != 0) {
                    File f = target.toFile();
                    if (!f.setExecutable(true, false)) {
                        throw new IOException("cannot make " + target + " executable");
                    }
                }
                unpacked += size;
                // The archive is about twice the jar's size unpacked.
                progress = Math.min(0.99f, 0.8f + 0.19f * unpacked / (2f * total));
            } else {
                skip(in, padded); // links and pax headers: none in JCEF's archives that matter
            }
        }
    }

    private static boolean readFully(InputStream in, byte[] buffer) throws IOException {
        int read = 0;
        while (read < buffer.length) {
            int n = in.read(buffer, read, buffer.length - read);
            if (n < 0) {
                if (read == 0) {
                    return false;
                }
                throw new IOException("archive is cut off");
            }
            read += n;
        }
        return true;
    }

    private static void skip(InputStream in, long bytes) throws IOException {
        long left = bytes;
        byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
        while (left > 0) {
            int n = in.read(buffer, 0, (int) Math.min(buffer.length, left));
            if (n < 0) {
                throw new IOException("archive is cut off");
            }
            left -= n;
        }
    }

    private static String string(byte[] header, int offset, int length) {
        int end = offset;
        while (end < offset + length && header[end] != 0) {
            end++;
        }
        return new String(header, offset, end - offset, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    private static void deleteTree(Path dir) throws IOException {
        if (!Files.exists(dir)) {
            return;
        }
        try (java.util.stream.Stream<Path> walk = Files.walk(dir)) {
            for (Path p : (Iterable<Path>) walk.sorted(java.util.Comparator.reverseOrder())::iterator) {
                Files.delete(p);
            }
        }
    }

    private static MessageDigest sha256() {
        try {
            return MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(e);
        }
    }

    private static String hex(byte[] bytes) {
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
        for (byte b : bytes) {
            out.append(String.format("%02x", b));
        }
        return out.toString();
    }

    // ---- start ----

    /** Loads JCEF and the bridge in a class loader of their own and opens the first page. */
    private BrowserEngine load(String url) throws IOException, ReflectiveOperationException {
        Path bridge = root.resolve("cef-bridge.jar");
        try (InputStream in = Browser.class.getResourceAsStream("cef-bridge.jar")) {
            if (in == null) {
                throw new IOException("cef-bridge.jar is missing from the overlay jar");
            }
            // Rewritten every start so an updated overlay brings its own bridge.
            Files.copy(in, bridge, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
        }
        URL[] jars = {root.resolve("jcef-api.jar").toUri().toURL(), bridge.toUri().toURL()};
        @SuppressWarnings("resource") // lives as long as the game: CEF cannot be started twice
        URLClassLoader loader = new URLClassLoader(jars, Browser.class.getClassLoader());
        Path cache = root.resolve("profile");
        Files.createDirectories(cache);
        Object engine = Class.forName("dev.k0t1k.overlay.cef.CefEngine", true, loader)
                .getMethod("create", File.class, File.class, String.class, String.class)
                .invoke(null, natives.toFile(), cache.toFile(), language, url);
        return (BrowserEngine) engine;
    }

    /** Pixels of the browser's newest frame, for the renderer. */
    static final class Frame {
        ByteBuffer pixels = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(4);
        final int[] size = new int[2];
        long version;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/BrowserEngine.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;

/**
 * A web browser drawn off screen, implemented by the CEF bridge ({@code src/cef}), which lives in a
 * class loader of its own next to the downloaded JCEF. Only JDK types cross, so the bridge needs
 * nothing else from the overlay. Input uses java.awt.event ids, key codes and extended modifier masks.
 */
public interface BrowserEngine {
    /** The view size in pixels. */
    void resize(int width, int height);

    void load(String url);

    void back();

    void forward();

    void reload();

    String url();

    String title();

    boolean loading();

    void mouse(int id, int x, int y, int modifiers, int clicks, int button);

    /** {@code amount} in lines, positive scrolls down. */
    void wheel(int x, int y, int modifiers, int amount);

    void key(int id, int keyCode, char keyChar, int modifiers);

    void focus(boolean focused);

    /**
     * Copies the newest frame (BGRA, top-down rows) into {@code out} when it is newer than
     * {@code seen}; fills {@code size} with its width and height and returns its version.
     */
    long frame(long seen, ByteBuffer out, int[] size);

    void close();
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/BrowserView.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.awt.event.InputEvent;
import java.awt.event.KeyEvent;
import java.awt.event.MouseEvent;
import java.io.UnsupportedEncodingException;
import java.net.URLEncoder;

/**
 * The browser tab: a real Chromium page (see {@link Browser}) with back, forward, reload, an address
 * bar and quick links. Mouse and keyboard over the page go to Chromium; the page's pixels are drawn as
 * the batch's image, so this works where the overlay draws with OpenGL.
 */
final class BrowserView {
    static final String HOME = "https://minecraft.wiki/";
    private static final String[][] LINKS = {
        {"Minecraft Wiki", HOME},
        {"Modrinth", "https://modrinth.com/"},
        {"CurseForge", "https://www.curseforge.com/minecraft"},
        {"YouTube", "https://www.youtube.com/"},
    };

    private final Browser browser;
    private final Strings strings;
    private final Overlay overlay;
    private final TextEdit address = new TextEdit();
    private final Browser.Frame frame = new Browser.Frame();
    /** Stands for the page in {@link Ui#focus}: the page has the keyboard while it is focused. */
    private final Object pageFocus = new Object();
    private String shownUrl = "";
    private float lastX = -1;
    private float lastY = -1;
    private boolean pressed;

    BrowserView(Browser browser, Strings strings, Overlay overlay) {
        this.browser = browser;
        this.strings = strings;
        this.overlay = overlay;
    }

    void draw(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        BrowserEngine engine = browser.engine();
        float barH = 34;
        float bx = x;
        String[] icons = {"←", "→", "↻"};
        for (int i = 0; i < icons.length; i++) {
            if (ui.button(bx, y, barH, barH, icons[i], false) && engine != null) {
                if (i == 0) {
                    engine.back();
                } else if (i == 1) {
                    engine.forward();
                } else {
                    engine.reload();
                }
            }
            bx += barH + 6;
        }
        if (engine != null && ui.focus != address && !engine.url().equals(shownUrl)) {
            shownUrl = engine.url();
            address.set(shownUrl);
        }
        float linksW = 0;
        for (String[] link : LINKS) {
            linksW += ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, link[0]) + 20 + 4;
        }
        boolean roomForLinks = w - (bx - x) - linksW > 260;
        float fieldW = x + w - bx - (roomForLinks ? linksW + 6 : 0);
        ui.field(address, bx, y, fieldW, barH, false, strings.get("browser.address"));
        if (ui.focus == address && pressed(ui, Keys.ENTER, Keys.KP_ENTER)) {
            open(target(address.value()));
            ui.focus = pageFocus;
        }
        if (roomForLinks) {
            float lx = bx + fieldW + 6;
            for (String[] link : LINKS) {
                float lw = ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, link[0]) + 20;
                if (ui.pill(lx, y + 3, lw, barH - 6, link[0], false)) {
                    open(link[1]);
                }
                lx += lw + 4;
            }
        }

        float py = y + barH + 10;
        float ph = h - barH - 10;
        ui.panel(x, py, w, ph, 10, Ui.BASE, Ui.LINE);
        if (engine == null || browser.state != Browser.State.READY) {
            drawSetup(ui, x, py, w, ph);
            return;
        }
        if (!overlay.imagesSupported()) {
            center(ui, x, py, w, ph, strings.get("browser.glOnly"), Ui.FAINT);
            return;
        }
        float vx = x + 1;
        float vy = py + 1;
        float vw = w - 2;
        float vh = ph - 2;
        engine.resize(Math.max(1, Math.round(vw * ui.scale)), Math.max(1, Math.round(vh * ui.scale)));
        long version = engine.frame(frame.version, frame.pixels, frame.size);
        if (version == -1) {
            frame.pixels = java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(frame.size[0] * frame.size[1] * 4);
            version = engine.frame(frame.version, frame.pixels, frame.size);
        }
        if (version > 0) {
            frame.version = version;
            Batch batch = ui.batch;
            batch.image = frame.pixels;
            batch.imageWidth = frame.size[0];
            batch.imageHeight = frame.size[1];
            batch.imageVersion = version;
            ui.image(vx, vy, vw, vh);
        } else {
            center(ui, x, py, w, ph, strings.get("browser.loading"), Ui.FAINT);
        }
        input(ui, engine, vx, vy, vw, vh);
        if (engine.loading()) {
            ui.rect(vx, vy, vw * (0.3f + 0.4f * (float) Math.abs(Math.sin(System.currentTimeMillis() / 400.0))), 2, Ui.ACCENT);
        }
    }

    private void drawSetup(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        Browser.State state = browser.state;
        float cx = x + w / 2;
        float cy = y + h / 2;
        switch (state) {
            case UNSUPPORTED:
                center(ui, x, y, w, h, strings.get("browser.unsupported"), Ui.FAINT);
                return;
            case MISSING:
            case FAILED: {
                String text = state == Browser.State.FAILED ? strings.format("browser.failed", browser.error)
                        : strings.format("browser.install", browser.size());
                float tw = Math.min(ui.measure(ui.regular, 14, text), w - 60);
                ui.textFit(ui.regular, 14, cx - tw / 2, cy - 40, tw, state == Browser.State.FAILED ? Ui.DANGER : Ui.MUTED, text);
                String label = strings.get(state == Browser.State.FAILED ? "browser.retry" : "browser.download");
                float bw = ui.buttonWidth(label);
                if (ui.button(cx - bw / 2, cy - 6, bw, 34, label, true)) {
                    browser.start(HOME);
                }
                return;
            }
            case STARTING:
                if (browser.engine() == null) {
                    browser.start(HOME);
                }
                center(ui, x, y, w, h, strings.get("browser.starting"), Ui.FAINT);
                return;
            case INSTALLING:
            default: {
                String text = strings.format("browser.downloading", Math.round(browser.progress * 100));
                float tw = ui.measure(ui.regular, 14, text);
                ui.text(ui.regular, 14, cx - tw / 2, cy - 30, Ui.MUTED, text);
                float bw = Math.min(360, w - 80);
                ui.round(cx - bw / 2, cy, bw, 8, 4, Ui.RAISED);
                ui.round(cx - bw / 2, cy, bw * browser.progress, 8, 4, Ui.ACCENT);
            }
        }
    }

    private static void center(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h, String text, int color) {
        float tw = Math.min(ui.measure(ui.regular, 14, text), w - 40);
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 14, x + (w - tw) / 2, y + h / 2 - 10, tw, color, text);
    }

    private void open(String url) {
        BrowserEngine engine = browser.engine();
        if (engine != null) {
            engine.load(url);
            shownUrl = url;
        }
    }

    /** An address as typed: a URL (with or without https://), or else a web search. */
    static String target(String typed) {
        String value = typed.trim();
        if (value.isEmpty()) {
            return HOME;
        }
        if (value.matches("(?i)^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*://.*")) {
            return value;
        }
        if (!value.contains(" ") && value.matches("^[^/]+\\.[a-zA-Z]{2,}(/.*)?$")) {
            return "https://" + value;
        }
        try {
            return "https://www.google.com/search?q=" + URLEncoder.encode(value, "UTF-8");
        } catch (UnsupportedEncodingException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(e);
        }
    }

    // ---- input to the page ----

    private void input(Ui ui, BrowserEngine engine, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        boolean over = ui.hover(x, y, w, h);
        int px = Math.round((ui.mouseX - x) * ui.scale);
        int py = Math.round((ui.mouseY - y) * ui.scale);
        if (over && (ui.mouseX != lastX || ui.mouseY != lastY)) {
            lastX = ui.mouseX;
            lastY = ui.mouseY;
            engine.mouse(pressed ? MouseEvent.MOUSE_DRAGGED : MouseEvent.MOUSE_MOVED, px, py,
                    pressed ? InputEvent.BUTTON1_DOWN_MASK : 0, 0, MouseEvent.NOBUTTON);
        }
        if (over && ui.clickedIn(x, y, w, h)) {
            ui.focus = pageFocus;
            engine.focus(true);
            pressed = true;
            engine.mouse(MouseEvent.MOUSE_PRESSED, px, py, InputEvent.BUTTON1_DOWN_MASK, ui.doubleClicked ? 2 : 1, MouseEvent.BUTTON1);
        }
        if (pressed && ui.released) {
            pressed = false;
            engine.mouse(MouseEvent.MOUSE_RELEASED, px, py, 0, 1, MouseEvent.BUTTON1);
        }
        float scroll = ui.takeScroll(x, y, w, h);
        if (scroll != 0) {
            engine.wheel(px, py, 0, -Math.round(scroll));
        }
        if (ui.focus != pageFocus) {
            return;
        }
        for (int[] key : ui.keys) {
            int code = awtKey(key[0]);
            if (code == KeyEvent.VK_UNDEFINED) {
                continue;
            }
            int mods = awtMods(key[1]);
            engine.key(KeyEvent.KEY_PRESSED, code, KeyEvent.CHAR_UNDEFINED, mods);
            if (code == KeyEvent.VK_ENTER) {
                engine.key(KeyEvent.KEY_TYPED, KeyEvent.VK_UNDEFINED, '\r', mods);
            }
            engine.key(KeyEvent.KEY_RELEASED, code, KeyEvent.CHAR_UNDEFINED, mods);
        }
        ui.keys.clear();
        for (int i = 0; i < ui.chars.length(); i++) {
            engine.key(KeyEvent.KEY_TYPED, KeyEvent.VK_UNDEFINED, ui.chars.charAt(i), 0);
        }
        ui.chars.setLength(0);
    }

    private static boolean pressed(Ui ui, int... wanted) {
        for (int[] key : ui.keys) {
            for (int k : wanted) {
                if (key[0] == k) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** GLFW key codes to AWT's: printable keys share ASCII codes, the rest are mapped. */
    private static int awtKey(int key) {
        if (key >= 32 && key < 128) {
            return key;
        }
        switch (key) {
            case Keys.ENTER:
            case Keys.KP_ENTER:
                return KeyEvent.VK_ENTER;
            case Keys.TAB:
                return KeyEvent.VK_TAB;
            case Keys.BACKSPACE:
                return KeyEvent.VK_BACK_SPACE;
            case Keys.DELETE:
                return KeyEvent.VK_DELETE;
            case Keys.LEFT:
                return KeyEvent.VK_LEFT;
            case Keys.RIGHT:
                return KeyEvent.VK_RIGHT;
            case Keys.UP:
                return KeyEvent.VK_UP;
            case Keys.DOWN:
                return KeyEvent.VK_DOWN;
            case Keys.HOME:
                return KeyEvent.VK_HOME;
            case Keys.END:
                return KeyEvent.VK_END;
            case Keys.PAGE_UP:
                return KeyEvent.VK_PAGE_UP;
            case Keys.PAGE_DOWN:
                return KeyEvent.VK_PAGE_DOWN;
            default:
                return KeyEvent.VK_UNDEFINED;
        }
    }

    private static int awtMods(int mods) {
        int out = 0;
        if ((mods & Keys.MOD_SHIFT) != 0) {
            out |= InputEvent.SHIFT_DOWN_MASK;
        }
        if ((mods & Keys.MOD_CONTROL) != 0) {
            out |= InputEvent.CTRL_DOWN_MASK;
        }
        if ((mods & Keys.MOD_ALT) != 0) {
            out |= InputEvent.ALT_DOWN_MASK;
        }
        if ((mods & Keys.MOD_SUPER) != 0) {
            out |= InputEvent.META_DOWN_MASK;
        }
        return out;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Cat.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

/**
 * The launcher's cat in a corner of the screen. It reacts to what happens in the game (a death, an
 * advancement, a long AFK, a timer) with a speech bubble; while the overlay is closed it only shows up
 * for those moments. Drawn from {@code cat.txt}, a pixel map of the mascot, as runs of rectangles.
 */
final class Cat {
    private static final long SHOW = 6000;
    private static final long SLIDE = 250;

    /** Horizontal runs {x, y, length, colour} of the pixel map. */
    private final List<int[]> runs = new ArrayList<>();
    /** The white pixels of the eyes, drawn closed while blinking. */
    private final List<int[]> eyes = new ArrayList<>();
    final int width;
    final int height;
    private final Random random = new Random();

    private String bubble;
    private long since;
    private long nextBlink = System.currentTimeMillis() + 3000;

    Cat() throws IOException {
        List<String> rows = new ArrayList<>();
        try (InputStream in = Cat.class.getResourceAsStream("cat.txt")) {
            if (in == null) {
                throw new IOException("cat.txt is missing from the overlay jar");
            }
            BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(in, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            for (String row; (row = reader.readLine()) != null; ) {
                if (!row.isEmpty()) {
                    rows.add(row);
                }
            }
        }
        int w = 0;
        for (int y = 0; y < rows.size(); y++) {
            String row = rows.get(y);
            w = Math.max(w, row.length());
            int x = 0;
            while (x < row.length()) {
                char c = row.charAt(x);
                int end = x + 1;
                while (end < row.length() && row.charAt(end) == c) {
                    end++;
                }
                int colour = colour(c);
                if (colour != 0) {
                    int[] run = {x, y, end - x, colour};
                    runs.add(run);
                    // The eyes are the white runs in the lower half; the ears' insides are above.
                    if (c == 'w' && y > rows.size() / 2) {
                        eyes.add(run);
                    }
                }
                x = end;
            }
        }
        width = w;
        height = rows.size();
    }

    private static int colour(char c) {
        switch (c) {
            case 'k':
                return 0xff0c0c0c;
            case 'p':
                return 0xffb200ff;
            case 'w':
                return 0xffffffff;
            default:
                return 0;
        }
    }

    /** Shows one of the phrases in a bubble for a few seconds. */
    void say(String... phrases) {
        bubble = phrases[random.nextInt(phrases.length)];
        since = System.currentTimeMillis();
    }

    /** Whether the cat has something to say right now. */
    boolean talking(long now) {
        return bubble != null && now - since < SHOW;
    }

    /**
     * Draws the cat with its bottom left corner at (x, bottom). While closed it slides in from below
     * the screen edge only while talking.
     */
    void draw(Ui ui, float x, float bottom, float pixel, boolean always, long now) {
        boolean talking = talking(now);
        if (!talking && !always) {
            return;
        }
        float offset = 0;
        if (!always) {
            long age = now - since;
            float t = Math.min(1, Math.min(age, SHOW - age) / (float) SLIDE);
            offset = (1 - t * t * (3 - 2 * t)) * (height * pixel + 20);
        }
        // A small hop when it starts talking.
        long age = now - since;
        if (talking && age < 400) {
            offset -= (float) Math.sin(age / 400.0 * Math.PI) * 6;
        }
        float top = bottom - height * pixel + offset;
        if (now >= nextBlink + 140) {
            nextBlink = now + 2500 + random.nextInt(4000);
        }
        boolean blinking = now >= nextBlink;
        for (int[] run : runs) {
            int colour = blinking && eyes.contains(run) ? 0xff0c0c0c : run[3];
            ui.rect(x + run[0] * pixel, top + run[1] * pixel, run[2] * pixel + 0.01f, pixel + 0.01f, colour);
        }
        if (talking) {
            drawBubble(ui, x + width * pixel * 0.55f, top - 6, ui.measure(ui.regular, 13, bubble));
        }
    }

    private void drawBubble(Ui ui, float x, float bottom, float textW) {
        float w = Math.min(textW, 260) + 24;
        List<int[]> lines = ui.wrap(ui.regular, 13, bubble, w - 24);
        float lh = ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 13);
        float h = lines.size() * lh + 16;
        float y = bottom - h;
        ui.panel(x, y, w, h, 10, 0xf21b1728, 0xffb200ff);
        ui.round(x + 14, bottom - 2, 8, 8, 2, 0xffb200ff);
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++) {
            int[] span = lines.get(i);
            ui.text(ui.regular, 13, x + 12, y + 8 + i * lh, Ui.FG, bubble, span[0], span[1]);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/ChatHistory.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.io.RandomAccessFile;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.ScheduledExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Chat history without a mod: the game logs every chat line to {@code logs/latest.log} as
 * {@code ... [CHAT] text}, so the overlay tails that file and appends each new line to its own store,
 * which keeps everything ever seen across sessions and instances.
 *
 * <p>The store is a text file, one {@code time TAB server TAB text} line per message (backslash
 * escapes tab, newline and itself). Only the newest messages are kept in memory; older ones are read
 * back from the end of the file on demand and searches stream over the whole file.
 *
 * <p>File work runs on {@link #io}; results come back to the render thread through {@link #render}.
 */
final class ChatHistory {
    static final String SINGLEPLAYER = "@singleplayer";
    private static final int PAGE = 2000;
    private static final int SEARCH_LIMIT = 2000;

    static final class Line {
        final long time;
        final String server;
        final String text;

        Line(long time, String server, String text) {
            this.time = time;
            this.server = server;
            this.text = text;
        }
    }

    private final Path log;
    private final Path store;
    private final ScheduledExecutorService io;
    private final ExecutorService searcher;
    private final Executor render;
    private final Consumer<Throwable> onError;

    // ---- render thread ----
    /** Loaded messages, oldest first. */
    final List<Line> lines = new ArrayList<>();
    /** Bumped whenever {@link #lines} changes, so views can tell when to lay out again. */
    int version;
    /** Bumped whenever older messages are put in front of {@link #lines}. */
    int prepends;
    boolean hasOlder;
    boolean loadingOlder;
    private boolean loaded;
    private long olderEnd;
    private volatile int searchGeneration;
    /** Called on the render thread for every new message seen in this session. */
    Consumer<Line> onNew = line -> { };

    // ---- io thread ----
    private long logPosition;
    private final ByteArrayOutputStream partial = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
    private String server = "";

    ChatHistory(Path gameDir, Path dataDir, ScheduledExecutorService io, ExecutorService searcher, Executor render,
            Consumer<Throwable> onError) {
        this.log = gameDir.resolve("logs").resolve("latest.log");
        this.store = dataDir.resolve("chat-history.tsv");
        this.io = io;
        this.searcher = searcher;
        this.render = render;
        this.onError = onError;
    }

    void start() {
        io.execute(() -> {
            try {
                startOnIo();
            } catch (RuntimeException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
            }
        });
    }

    private void startOnIo() {
        try {
            Files.createDirectories(store.getParent());
            long length = Files.exists(store) ? Files.size(store) : 0;
            Chunk chunk = readBefore(length, PAGE);
            render.execute(() -> {
                lines.addAll(0, chunk.lines);
                prepends++;
                olderEnd = chunk.start;
                hasOlder = chunk.start > 0;
                loaded = true;
                version++;
            });
        } catch (IOException e) {
            onError.accept(e);
        }
        // The game already rotated latest.log before its window opened, so the whole file is
        // this session and nothing in it has been stored yet.
        io.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::pollNow, 0, 250, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
    }

    /** Asks for the page of messages before the oldest loaded one. */
    void loadOlder() {
        if (!loaded || !hasOlder || loadingOlder) {
            return;
        }
        loadingOlder = true;
        long end = olderEnd;
        io.execute(() -> {
            Chunk chunk = null;
            try {
                chunk = readBefore(end, PAGE);
            } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
            } finally {
                Chunk loaded = chunk;
                render.execute(() -> {
                    loadingOlder = false;
                    if (loaded != null) {
                        lines.addAll(0, loaded.lines);
                        prepends++;
                        olderEnd = loaded.start;
                        hasOlder = loaded.start > 0;
                        version++;
                    }
                });
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * Searches the whole store for {@code query} (case-insensitive, text or server) and hands the newest
     * {@value #SEARCH_LIMIT} matches, oldest first, to {@code done} on the render thread. A newer search
     * supersedes a running one.
     */
    void search(String query, Consumer<List<Line>> done) {
        int generation = ++searchGeneration;
        String needle = query.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        searcher.execute(() -> {
            ArrayDeque<Line> found = new ArrayDeque<>();
            try (InputStream in = Files.exists(store) ? Files.newInputStream(store) : null) {
                if (in != null) {
                    ByteArrayOutputStream current = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
                    byte[] buffer = new byte[1 << 16];
                    int counter = 0;
                    for (int n; (n = in.read(buffer)) > 0; ) {
                        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                            if (buffer[i] != '\n') {
                                current.write(buffer[i]);
                                continue;
                            }
                            Line line = decode(new String(current.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                            current.reset();
                            if (line != null && (line.text.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(needle)
                                    || line.server.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(needle))) {
                                found.addLast(line);
                                if (found.size() > SEARCH_LIMIT) {
                                    found.removeFirst();
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        // Stop early when a newer search has started.
                        if (++counter % 16 == 0 && generation != searchGeneration) {
                            return;
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
            }
            List<Line> result = new ArrayList<>(found);
            render.execute(() -> {
                if (generation == searchGeneration) {
                    done.accept(result);
                }
            });
        });
    }

    /** Cancels the result of a running search. */
    void cancelSearch() {
        searchGeneration++;
    }

    // ---- io thread ----

    /**
     * Moves new chat lines from the log into the store. Runs on the io thread, and once more from the
     * exit hook, hence synchronized. The log position only advances once the lines are stored, so a
     * failed write is retried on the next poll instead of losing them.
     */
    synchronized void pollNow() {
        try {
            LogRead read = readLog();
            List<Line> fresh = read.lines;
            if (fresh.isEmpty()) {
                commit(read);
                return;
            }
            StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
            for (Line line : fresh) {
                out.append(line.time).append('\t').append(escape(line.server)).append('\t')
                        .append(escape(line.text)).append('\n');
            }
            // One write per batch: other games sharing the data folder append to the same file.
            try (OutputStream file = new FileOutputStream(store.toFile(), true)) {
                file.write(out.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            }
            commit(read);
            render.execute(() -> {
                lines.addAll(fresh);
                version++;
                for (Line line : fresh) {
                    onNew.accept(line);
                }
            });
        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
            onError.accept(e);
        }
    }

    /** What one poll read: the lines, and the log state to keep once they are stored. */
    private static final class LogRead {
        final List<Line> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        long position;
        byte[] partial;
        String server;
    }

    private void commit(LogRead read) {
        logPosition = read.position;
        partial.reset();
        partial.write(read.partial, 0, read.partial.length);
        server = read.server;
    }

    private LogRead readLog() throws IOException {
        LogRead read = new LogRead();
        read.position = logPosition;
        read.partial = partial.toByteArray();
        read.server = server;
        if (!Files.exists(log)) {
            return read;
        }
        try (RandomAccessFile file = new RandomAccessFile(log.toFile(), "r")) {
            long length = file.length();
            if (length < read.position) {
                // Truncated or replaced: start over.
                read.position = 0;
                read.partial = new byte[0];
            }
            if (length == read.position) {
                return read;
            }
            file.seek(read.position);
            byte[] bytes = new byte[(int) Math.min(length - read.position, 1 << 22)];
            file.readFully(bytes);
            read.position += bytes.length;
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            ByteArrayOutputStream current = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            current.write(read.partial, 0, read.partial.length);
            for (byte b : bytes) {
                if (b != '\n') {
                    current.write(b);
                    continue;
                }
                String raw = new String(current.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                current.reset();
                Line line = parseLogLine(raw, now, read);
                if (line != null) {
                    read.lines.add(line);
                }
            }
            read.partial = current.toByteArray();
        }
        return read;
    }

    /** Turns a log line into a chat message, tracking the server on the way; null for other lines. */
    private static Line parseLogLine(String raw, long now, LogRead read) {
        if (raw.isEmpty() || raw.charAt(0) != '[') {
            return null; // stack traces and wrapped output
        }
        if (raw.endsWith("\r")) {
            raw = raw.substring(0, raw.length() - 1);
        }
        int chat = raw.indexOf("[CHAT] ");
        if (chat >= 0) {
            // The game writes newlines inside a message as a literal backslash-n.
            String text = stripFormatting(raw.substring(chat + 7)).replace("\\n", "\n");
            return text.trim().isEmpty() ? null : new Line(now, read.server, text);
        }
        int connecting = raw.indexOf("]: Connecting to ");
        if (connecting >= 0) {
            String target = raw.substring(connecting + 17).trim();
            int comma = target.lastIndexOf(", ");
            if (comma >= 0) {
                String port = target.substring(comma + 2);
                target = target.substring(0, comma) + ("25565".equals(port) ? "" : ":" + port);
            }
            read.server = target;
        } else if (raw.contains("]: Starting integrated minecraft server")) {
            read.server = SINGLEPLAYER;
        }
        return null;
    }

    /** Drops legacy section-sign colour codes some servers still put into messages. */
    static String stripFormatting(String text) {
        if (text.indexOf('§') < 0) {
            return text;
        }
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(text.length());
        for (int i = 0; i < text.length(); i++) {
            char c = text.charAt(i);
            if (c == '§' && i + 1 < text.length()) {
                i++;
            } else {
                out.append(c);
            }
        }
        return out.toString();
    }

    private static final class Chunk {
        final List<Line> lines;
        final long start;

        Chunk(List<Line> lines, long start) {
            this.lines = lines;
            this.start = start;
        }
    }

    /** Reads up to {@code max} whole lines that end at or before byte {@code end} of the store. */
    private Chunk readBefore(long end, int max) throws IOException {
        List<Line> result = new ArrayList<>();
        if (end == 0) {
            return new Chunk(result, 0);
        }
        try (RandomAccessFile file = new RandomAccessFile(store.toFile(), "r")) {
            long start = end;
            byte[] acc = new byte[0];
            int newlines = 0;
            while (start > 0 && newlines <= max) {
                int n = (int) Math.min(1 << 16, start);
                start -= n;
                byte[] block = new byte[n + acc.length];
                file.seek(start);
                file.readFully(block, 0, n);
                for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                    if (block[i] == '\n') {
                        newlines++;
                    }
                }
                System.arraycopy(acc, 0, block, n, acc.length);
                acc = block;
            }
            int from = 0;
            if (start > 0) {
                // The first piece is the tail of a line that belongs to the next page.
                while (acc[from] != '\n') {
                    from++;
                }
                from++;
            }
            List<int[]> spans = new ArrayList<>();
            for (int i = from, lineStart = from; i < acc.length; i++) {
                if (acc[i] == '\n') {
                    spans.add(new int[] {lineStart, i});
                    lineStart = i + 1;
                }
            }
            int skip = Math.max(0, spans.size() - max);
            long chunkStart = skip > 0 ? start + spans.get(skip)[0] : start + from;
            for (int i = skip; i < spans.size(); i++) {
                int[] span = spans.get(i);
                Line line = decode(new String(acc, span[0], span[1] - span[0], StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                if (line != null) {
                    result.add(line);
                }
            }
            return new Chunk(result, chunkStart);
        }
    }

    private static Line decode(String row) {
        int a = row.indexOf('\t');
        int b = a < 0 ? -1 : row.indexOf('\t', a + 1);
        if (b < 0) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            return new Line(Long.parseLong(row.substring(0, a)), unescape(row.substring(a + 1, b)),
                    unescape(row.substring(b + 1)));
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return null; // a torn write from a crash; skip the row
        }
    }

    static String escape(String value) {
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(value.length());
        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {
            char c = value.charAt(i);
            if (c == '\\') {
                out.append("\\\\");
            } else if (c == '\t') {
                out.append("\\t");
            } else if (c == '\n') {
                out.append("\\n");
            } else if (c != '\r') {
                out.append(c);
            }
        }
        return out.toString();
    }

    static String unescape(String value) {
        if (value.indexOf('\\') < 0) {
            return value;
        }
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(value.length());
        for (int i = 0; i < value.length(); i++) {
            char c = value.charAt(i);
            if (c == '\\' && i + 1 < value.length()) {
                char next = value.charAt(++i);
                out.append(next == 't' ? '\t' : next == 'n' ? '\n' : next);
            } else {
                out.append(c);
            }
        }
        return out.toString();
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/ChatView.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * The chat tab: the whole stored history, newest at the bottom, with a full-text search and filters
 * for mentions, private messages and one player's messages.
 */
final class ChatView {
    private static final float TEXT = 14;
    private static final float TIME_COLUMN = 46;
    private static final DateTimeFormatter TIME = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm");
    private static final DateTimeFormatter DATE = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd.MM.yyyy");

    private final ChatHistory history;
    private final Strings strings;
    private final Overlay overlay;
    private final ZoneId zone = ZoneId.systemDefault();

    private final TextEdit search = new TextEdit();
    private String typedQuery = "";
    private long typedAt;
    private String activeQuery = "";
    private boolean searching;
    private List<ChatHistory.Line> results;

    /** 0 everything, 1 mentions of the player, 2 private messages. */
    private int filter;
    private final TextEdit player = new TextEdit();
    private static final String[] FILTERS = {"chat.all", "chat.mentions", "chat.private"};
    /** Markers of private messages in vanilla and on common servers (EssentialsX, CMI, Hypixel...). */
    private static final String[] PRIVATE = {
        "whispers to you", "you whisper to", "шепчет вам", "вы шепчете", "-> me]", "-> you]", "-> я]",
        "[pm]", "[msg]", "(msg)", "[лс]", "from ", "to ",
    };
    private List<ChatHistory.Line> filtered;
    private List<ChatHistory.Line> filteredBase;
    private int filteredVersion = -1;
    private int filteredSize = -1;
    private String filteredKey = "";

    /** Distance of the view's bottom from the bottom of the content; 0 follows new messages. */
    private float scroll;
    private boolean draggingBar;
    private float dragOffset;

    private static final class Row {
        ChatHistory.Line line;
        String header;
        String time;
        List<int[]> wrapped;
        float y;
        float h;
    }

    private final List<Row> rows = new ArrayList<>();
    private float total;
    private List<ChatHistory.Line> laidOut;
    private int laidOutVersion = -1;
    private float laidOutWidth = -1;
    private float laidOutScale = -1;
    private int laidOutCount;
    private int laidOutPrepends = -1;
    private String lastServer;
    private LocalDate lastDay;
    private static final List<ChatHistory.Line> NONE = new ArrayList<>();

    ChatView(ChatHistory history, Strings strings, Overlay overlay) {
        this.history = history;
        this.strings = strings;
        this.overlay = overlay;
    }

    void draw(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        updateSearch();

        float barH = 34;
        float searchW = Math.min(300, w * 0.3f);
        ui.field(search, x, y, searchW, barH, false, strings.get("chat.search"));
        float playerW = Math.min(170, w * 0.17f);
        ui.field(player, x + searchW + 8, y, playerW, barH, false, strings.get("chat.player"));
        float fx = x + searchW + playerW + 20;
        for (int i = 0; i < FILTERS.length; i++) {
            String label = strings.get(FILTERS[i]);
            float pw = ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, label) + 24;
            if (ui.pill(fx, y + 2, pw, barH - 4, label, filter == i)) {
                filter = i;
                scroll = 0;
            }
            fx += pw + 4;
        }
        String status = results != null || searching
                ? (searching ? strings.get("chat.searching") : strings.format("chat.found", results.size()))
                : strings.format("chat.count", history.lines.size()) + " · " + strings.get("chat.copyHint");
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, fx + 10, y + (barH - ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 12)) / 2,
                x + w - fx - 10, Ui.FAINT, status);

        float listY = y + barH + 12;
        float listH = h - barH - 12;
        ui.panel(x, listY, w, listH, 10, Ui.BASE, Ui.LINE);
        float padX = 14;
        float innerX = x + padX;
        float innerW = w - padX * 2 - 10;
        float innerY = listY + 6;
        float innerH = listH - 12;

        layout(ui, innerW);

        // Scrolling: the wheel moves three lines at a time.
        float lh = ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, TEXT);
        scroll += ui.takeScroll(x, listY, w, listH) * lh * 3;
        float maxScroll = Math.max(0, total - innerH);
        scroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(scroll, maxScroll));

        // Content shorter than the view sits at the top; otherwise the bottom follows scroll.
        float viewTop = total <= innerH ? 0 : total - innerH - scroll;
        if (results == null && !searching && viewTop < innerH && history.hasOlder) {
            history.loadOlder();
        }

        boolean inList = ui.hover(x, innerY, w - 12, innerH);
        ui.clip(x + 1, innerY, w - 2, innerH);
        int first = firstVisible(viewTop);
        for (int i = first; i < rows.size(); i++) {
            Row row = rows.get(i);
            float ry = innerY + row.y - viewTop;
            if (ry > innerY + innerH) {
                break;
            }
            drawRow(ui, row, innerX, ry, innerW, lh, inList);
        }
        ui.unclip();

        drawScrollbar(ui, x + w - 10, innerY, innerH, viewTop, maxScroll);

        if (scroll > lh * 3) {
            String label = strings.get("chat.toBottom");
            float bw = ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, label) + 24;
            if (ui.pill(x + w - bw - 22, listY + listH - 40, bw, 28, label, true)) {
                scroll = 0;
            }
        }
    }

    private void drawRow(Ui ui, Row row, float x, float y, float w, float lh, boolean inList) {
        if (row.header != null) {
            float th = ui.lineHeight(ui.bold, 12);
            float tw = ui.measure(ui.bold, 12, row.header);
            float cy = y + row.h / 2;
            ui.rect(x, cy, (w - tw) / 2 - 10, 1, Ui.LINE);
            ui.rect(x + (w + tw) / 2 + 10, cy, (w - tw) / 2 - 10, 1, Ui.LINE);
            ui.text(ui.bold, 12, x + (w - tw) / 2, cy - th / 2, row.line == null ? Ui.FAINT : Ui.MUTED, row.header);
            return;
        }
        boolean hot = inList && ui.hover(x - 6, y, w + 12, row.h);
        if (hot) {
            ui.round(x - 6, y, w + 12, row.h, 5, Ui.RAISED);
        }
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, x, y + (lh - ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 12)) / 2 + 1, Ui.FAINT, row.time);
        String text = row.line.text;
        for (int i = 0; i < row.wrapped.size(); i++) {
            int[] span = row.wrapped.get(i);
            ui.text(ui.regular, TEXT, x + TIME_COLUMN, y + 2 + i * lh, Ui.FG, text, span[0], span[1]);
        }
        if (hot && ui.clickedIn(x - 6, y, w + 12, row.h)) {
            ui.platform.setClipboard(text);
            overlay.toast(strings.get("chat.copied"), text);
        }
    }

    private void drawScrollbar(Ui ui, float x, float y, float h, float viewTop, float maxScroll) {
        if (maxScroll <= 0) {
            draggingBar = false;
            return;
        }
        float thumbH = Math.max(28, h * h / total);
        float travel = h - thumbH;
        float thumbY = y + travel * (viewTop / maxScroll);
        boolean hot = ui.hover(x - 4, thumbY, 12, thumbH);
        if (hot && ui.clicked) {
            ui.clickedIn(x - 4, thumbY, 12, thumbH);
            draggingBar = true;
            dragOffset = ui.mouseY - thumbY;
        }
        if (draggingBar) {
            if (!ui.mouseDown) {
                draggingBar = false;
            } else {
                float t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (ui.mouseY - dragOffset - y) / travel));
                scroll = maxScroll - t * maxScroll;
            }
        }
        ui.round(x, thumbY, 5, thumbH, 2.5f, draggingBar || hot ? 0xff4a3f6e : 0xff332b4d);
    }

    private int firstVisible(float viewTop) {
        int lo = 0;
        int hi = rows.size() - 1;
        int found = rows.size();
        while (lo <= hi) {
            int mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1;
            Row row = rows.get(mid);
            if (row.y + row.h >= viewTop) {
                found = mid;
                hi = mid - 1;
            } else {
                lo = mid + 1;
            }
        }
        return found;
    }

    private void updateSearch() {
        String typed = search.value().trim();
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (!typed.equals(typedQuery)) {
            typedQuery = typed;
            typedAt = now;
        }
        if (typedQuery.equals(activeQuery) || now - typedAt < 250) {
            return;
        }
        activeQuery = typedQuery;
        scroll = 0;
        if (activeQuery.isEmpty()) {
            history.cancelSearch();
            searching = false;
            results = null;
            return;
        }
        searching = true;
        history.search(activeQuery, found -> {
            searching = false;
            results = found;
        });
    }

    /**
     * Rebuilds the rows when the messages, the width or the UI scale changed. New messages at the end
     * are laid out on their own, so a long history costs nothing per message; pages of older messages
     * rebuild everything, which keeps the distance from the bottom and so the reading position.
     */
    private void layout(Ui ui, float width) {
        List<ChatHistory.Line> source = results != null ? results : searching ? NONE : history.lines;
        int version = results != null ? 0 : history.version;
        source = filter(source, version);
        // The header above the first message ("no messages yet") changes with it, so start over then.
        boolean sameShape = source == laidOut && width == laidOutWidth && ui.scale == laidOutScale && laidOutCount > 0;
        if (sameShape && version == laidOutVersion) {
            return;
        }
        if (sameShape && source == history.lines && history.prepends == laidOutPrepends
                && source.size() >= laidOutCount) {
            float before = total;
            appendRows(ui, source, laidOutCount, width);
            if (scroll > 0) {
                // Scrolled up to read: stay on the same messages while new ones arrive below.
                scroll += total - before;
            }
        } else {
            rows.clear();
            total = 0;
            lastServer = null;
            lastDay = null;
            String top = searching ? null
                    : results != null ? (results.isEmpty() ? strings.get("chat.notFound") : strings.format("chat.found", results.size()))
                    : history.hasOlder ? strings.get("chat.older")
                    : history.lines.isEmpty() ? strings.get("chat.empty") : strings.get("chat.start");
            if (top != null) {
                addHeader(top, null);
            }
            appendRows(ui, source, 0, width);
        }
        laidOut = source;
        laidOutVersion = version;
        laidOutWidth = width;
        laidOutScale = ui.scale;
        laidOutCount = source.size();
        laidOutPrepends = history.prepends;
    }

    private void appendRows(Ui ui, List<ChatHistory.Line> source, int from, float width) {
        float lh = ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, TEXT);
        for (int i = from; i < source.size(); i++) {
            ChatHistory.Line line = source.get(i);
            LocalDate day = Instant.ofEpochMilli(line.time).atZone(zone).toLocalDate();
            if (!line.server.equals(lastServer) || !day.equals(lastDay)) {
                lastServer = line.server;
                lastDay = day;
                addHeader(serverLabel(line.server) + "  ·  " + DATE.format(day), line);
            }
            Row row = new Row();
            row.line = line;
            row.time = TIME.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(line.time).atZone(zone));
            row.wrapped = ui.wrap(ui.regular, TEXT, line.text, width - TIME_COLUMN);
            row.y = total;
            row.h = row.wrapped.size() * lh + 4;
            rows.add(row);
            total += row.h;
        }
    }

    /**
     * The lines that pass the filters, as a list that stays the same object until the lines or the
     * filters change, so the layout is only rebuilt then.
     */
    private List<ChatHistory.Line> filter(List<ChatHistory.Line> base, int version) {
        String name = player.value().trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (filter == 0 && name.isEmpty()) {
            return base;
        }
        String key = filter + "\n" + name;
        if (base == filteredBase && version == filteredVersion && base.size() == filteredSize && key.equals(filteredKey)) {
            return filtered;
        }
        List<ChatHistory.Line> out = new ArrayList<>();
        String me = overlay.username();
        for (ChatHistory.Line line : base) {
            if (filter == 1 && !Overlay.mentions(line.text, me)) {
                continue;
            }
            if (filter == 2 && !isPrivate(line.text)) {
                continue;
            }
            if (!name.isEmpty() && !fromPlayer(line.text, name)) {
                continue;
            }
            out.add(line);
        }
        filtered = out;
        filteredBase = base;
        filteredVersion = version;
        filteredSize = base.size();
        filteredKey = key;
        return out;
    }

    static boolean isPrivate(String text) {
        String lower = text.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        for (String marker : PRIVATE) {
            if (marker.endsWith(" ") ? lower.startsWith(marker) : lower.contains(marker)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /**
     * Whether a line was written by the player: "&lt;Name&gt; text" in vanilla, "[Rank] Name: text" or
     * "Name » text" on most servers. Looks only at the part before the message itself.
     */
    static boolean fromPlayer(String text, String lowerName) {
        String lower = text.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        int end = lower.indexOf("> ");
        for (String separator : new String[] {": ", " » ", " > ", " -> "}) {
            int at = lower.indexOf(separator);
            if (at >= 0 && (end < 0 || at < end)) {
                end = at;
            }
        }
        // Without a sender part the line is a system message (deaths, joins), not something the player wrote.
        return end >= 0 && lower.substring(0, end + 1).contains(lowerName);
    }

    private void addHeader(String text, ChatHistory.Line line) {
        Row row = new Row();
        row.header = text;
        row.line = line;
        row.y = total;
        row.h = 30;
        rows.add(row);
        total += row.h;
    }

    private String serverLabel(String server) {
        if (server.isEmpty()) {
            return strings.get("chat.unknownServer");
        }
        return ChatHistory.SINGLEPLAYER.equals(server) ? strings.get("chat.singleplayer") : server;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/CrashGuard.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.PrintWriter;
import java.io.StringWriter;
import java.lang.management.ManagementFactory;
import java.lang.management.ThreadInfo;
import java.net.URISyntaxException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;

/**
 * Crash insurance. While the game runs it records exceptions that kill any thread; when the game
 * exits after writing a crash report, it collects what only the live JVM still knows (a dump of every
 * thread, those exceptions, the overlay's view of the session) and starts the crash window
 * ({@code dev.k0t1k.overlay.CrashDialog}) in a JVM of its own, which outlives the game.
 */
final class CrashGuard {
    /** Where "Send to support" leads after the upload. */
    static final String SUPPORT = "https://discord.gg/zxnMAfUvJ";
    private static final DateTimeFormatter NAME = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd_HH.mm.ss");

    private final Path folder;
    private final Deque<String> uncaught = new ArrayDeque<>();

    CrashGuard(Path dataDir) {
        this.folder = dataDir.resolve("crashes");
    }

    /** Records uncaught exceptions, then hands them on to whatever handler was there before. */
    void install() {
        Thread.UncaughtExceptionHandler previous = Thread.getDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler();
        Thread.setDefaultUncaughtExceptionHandler((thread, error) -> {
            StringWriter trace = new StringWriter();
            error.printStackTrace(new PrintWriter(trace));
            synchronized (uncaught) {
                uncaught.addLast("Thread \"" + thread.getName() + "\": " + trace);
                if (uncaught.size() > 5) {
                    uncaught.removeFirst();
                }
            }
            if (previous != null) {
                previous.uncaughtException(thread, error);
            } else {
                System.err.print("Exception in thread \"" + thread.getName() + "\" ");
                error.printStackTrace();
            }
        });
    }

    /**
     * From the shutdown hook, when the game crashed: writes the bundle and opens the crash window.
     * {@code summary} is the overlay's own notes on the session.
     */
    void onCrash(Path report, String lang, String summary) throws IOException {
        Files.createDirectories(folder);
        Path bundle = folder.resolve("crash_" + LocalDateTime.now().format(NAME) + ".txt");
        StringBuilder text = new StringBuilder();
        text.append("---- _k0t1k overlay: notes from the live game ----\n").append(summary).append('\n');
        synchronized (uncaught) {
            if (!uncaught.isEmpty()) {
                text.append("\n-- Uncaught exceptions --\n");
                for (String entry : uncaught) {
                    text.append(entry).append('\n');
                }
            }
        }
        text.append("\n-- Threads at exit --\n");
        for (ThreadInfo info : ManagementFactory.getThreadMXBean().dumpAllThreads(false, false)) {
            text.append(info.toString());
        }
        Files.write(bundle, text.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        launch(lang, report, bundle);
    }

    private static void launch(String lang, Path report, Path bundle) throws IOException {
        String jar;
        try {
            jar = new File(CrashGuard.class.getProtectionDomain().getCodeSource().getLocation().toURI()).getAbsolutePath();
        } catch (URISyntaxException | RuntimeException e) {
            throw new IOException("cannot locate the overlay jar", e);
        }
        boolean windows = System.getProperty("os.name", "").startsWith("Windows");
        File java = new File(System.getProperty("java.home"), windows ? "bin/javaw.exe" : "bin/java");
        ProcessBuilder builder = new ProcessBuilder(java.getAbsolutePath(), "-cp", jar,
                "dev.k0t1k.overlay.CrashDialog", lang, report.toString(), bundle.toString(), SUPPORT);
        File discard = new File(windows ? "NUL" : "/dev/null");
        builder.redirectErrorStream(true).redirectOutput(discard).redirectInput(ProcessBuilder.Redirect.from(discard));
        builder.start();
        Overlay.log("crash window started for " + report);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Font.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * A font rasterised on demand into the shared atlas of {@link Batch}. Glyphs are cached per pixel
 * size, so any size and any codepoint the font has can be drawn without baking a fixed range up
 * front. {@link StbFont} rasterises with stb_truetype (shipped with every Minecraft since 1.13);
 * {@link AwtFont} with java.awt, for Minecraft versions on LWJGL 2, which has no stb.
 */
abstract class Font {
    private final Atlas atlas;
    private final Map<Long, Glyph> glyphs = new HashMap<>();
    private int generation;

    static final class Glyph {
        final float advance; // pixels
        final int x0;
        final int y0;
        final int w;
        final int h;
        final float u0;
        final float v0;
        final float u1;
        final float v1;

        Glyph(float advance, int x0, int y0, int w, int h, float u0, float v0, float u1, float v1) {
            this.advance = advance;
            this.x0 = x0;
            this.y0 = y0;
            this.w = w;
            this.h = h;
            this.u0 = u0;
            this.v0 = v0;
            this.u1 = u1;
            this.v1 = v1;
        }
    }

    /** Shelf packer over the square atlas texture; shared by all fonts. */
    static final class Atlas {
        final int size;
        private final Batch batch;
        private int shelfX;
        private int shelfY;
        private int shelfH;
        private int generation;
        boolean full;

        Atlas(Batch batch) {
            this.batch = batch;
            this.size = Batch.ATLAS;
        }

        /** Returns {x, y} of a free w x h region, or null when the atlas is full. */
        int[] allocate(int w, int h) {
            if (shelfX + w + 1 > size) {
                shelfX = 0;
                shelfY += shelfH + 1;
                shelfH = 0;
            }
            if (shelfY + h + 1 > size) {
                full = true;
                return null;
            }
            int[] at = {shelfX, shelfY};
            shelfX += w + 1;
            shelfH = Math.max(shelfH, h);
            return at;
        }

        /** Forgets every glyph; called between frames when the atlas ran full. */
        void reset() {
            shelfX = 0;
            shelfY = 0;
            shelfH = 0;
            full = false;
            generation++;
        }
    }

    Font(Atlas atlas) {
        this.atlas = atlas;
    }

    /** Distance from the top of a line to its baseline. */
    abstract float ascent(float px);

    abstract float lineHeight(float px);

    /** Whether the font has an outline for the codepoint; others fall back to a placeholder. */
    abstract boolean has(int codepoint);

    abstract float kern(int left, int right, float px);

    /** Rasterises one glyph at an exact pixel size into the atlas. */
    abstract Glyph rasterise(int codepoint, float px);

    final Glyph glyph(int codepoint, float px) {
        if (generation != atlas.generation) {
            glyphs.clear();
            generation = atlas.generation;
        }
        int size = Math.round(px * 4); // quarter-pixel buckets
        long key = ((long) size << 32) | (codepoint & 0xffffffffL);
        Glyph glyph = glyphs.get(key);
        if (glyph == null) {
            glyph = rasterise(codepoint, size / 4f);
            glyphs.put(key, glyph);
        }
        return glyph;
    }

    /** Copies a rasterised glyph bitmap into the atlas; blank when the atlas is full. */
    final Glyph place(float advance, int x0, int y0, int w, int h, ByteBuffer pixels) {
        int[] at = atlas.allocate(w, h);
        if (at == null) {
            // Drawn as blank this frame; the overlay resets the atlas before the next one.
            return new Glyph(advance, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
        }
        atlas.batch.putGlyph(at[0], at[1], w, h, pixels);
        float s = atlas.size;
        return new Glyph(advance, x0, y0, w, h, at[0] / s, at[1] / s, (at[0] + w) / s, (at[1] + h) / s);
    }
}
```
