`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/GameState.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.lang.reflect.Field;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;

/**
 * The player's position, dimension and health, read by reflection on the render thread. Only works
 * where Minecraft runs with readable names: 26.1+ (shipped unobfuscated) and loaders that remap to
 * Mojang names (NeoForge). Elsewhere {@link #available} stays false and the overlay does without.
 */
final class GameState {
    private static final long INTERVAL = 250;

    boolean available;
    boolean inWorld;
    double x;
    double y;
    double z;
    /** "overworld", "the_nether", "the_end", or the dimension's full id for others. */
    String dimension = "";
    float health = -1;

    private boolean tried;
    private long nextUpdate;
    private Method getInstance;
    private Field player;
    private Method getX;
    private Method getY;
    private Method getZ;
    private Method level;
    private Method dimensionOf;
    private Method getHealth;

    /** Refreshes the values a few times a second; never throws. */
    void update() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (now < nextUpdate || (tried && !available)) {
            return;
        }
        nextUpdate = now + INTERVAL;
        try {
            if (!tried) {
                tried = true;
                resolve();
            }
            read();
        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException | RuntimeException | LinkageError e) {
            if (available) {
                Overlay.log("game state unavailable: " + e);
            }
            available = false;
            inWorld = false;
        }
    }

    private void resolve() throws ReflectiveOperationException {
        Class<?> minecraft = find("net.minecraft.client.Minecraft");
        if (minecraft == null) {
            Overlay.log("no readable Minecraft classes, coordinates are not shown");
            return;
        }
        getInstance = minecraft.getMethod("getInstance");
        player = minecraft.getField("player");
        Class<?> entity = find("net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity");
        Class<?> living = find("net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity");
        if (entity == null || living == null) {
            return;
        }
        getX = entity.getMethod("getX");
        getY = entity.getMethod("getY");
        getZ = entity.getMethod("getZ");
        level = entity.getMethod("level");
        dimensionOf = level.getReturnType().getMethod("dimension");
        getHealth = living.getMethod("getHealth");
        available = true;
    }

    private static Class<?> find(String name) {
        ClassLoader[] loaders = {GameState.class.getClassLoader(), Thread.currentThread().getContextClassLoader()};
        for (ClassLoader loader : loaders) {
            if (loader == null) {
                continue;
            }
            try {
                return Class.forName(name, false, loader);
            } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
                // Try the next loader.
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    private void read() throws ReflectiveOperationException {
        if (!available) {
            return;
        }
        Object mc = getInstance.invoke(null);
        Object p = mc == null ? null : player.get(mc);
        inWorld = p != null;
        if (p == null) {
            return;
        }
        x = (Double) getX.invoke(p);
        y = (Double) getY.invoke(p);
        z = (Double) getZ.invoke(p);
        health = (Float) getHealth.invoke(p);
        Object world = level.invoke(p);
        dimension = world == null ? "" : dimensionName(String.valueOf(dimensionOf.invoke(world)));
    }

    /** {@code ResourceKey[minecraft:dimension / minecraft:the_nether]} to {@code the_nether}. */
    static String dimensionName(String key) {
        int slash = key.lastIndexOf(" / ");
        String id = slash >= 0 ? key.substring(slash + 3) : key;
        if (id.endsWith("]")) {
            id = id.substring(0, id.length() - 1);
        }
        return id.startsWith("minecraft:") ? id.substring("minecraft:".length()) : id;
    }

    /** "X Y Z" rounded down to blocks, as F3 shows the block position. */
    String blockPosition() {
        return (long) Math.floor(x) + " " + (long) Math.floor(y) + " " + (long) Math.floor(z);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/GifWriter.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.awt.Graphics2D;
import java.awt.RenderingHints;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;

/**
 * A looping animated GIF writer. Every frame uses one fixed palette (6 x 7 x 6 levels of red, green and
 * blue) with ordered dithering: no per-frame colour search, so 30 seconds encode in a few seconds.
 */
final class GifWriter {
    private static final int[] BAYER = {0, 8, 2, 10, 12, 4, 14, 6, 3, 11, 1, 9, 15, 7, 13, 5};

    private final OutputStream out;
    private final int width;
    private final int height;
    private final BufferedImage scaled;
    private final byte[] indices;

    GifWriter(OutputStream out, int width, int height) throws IOException {
        this.out = out;
        this.width = width;
        this.height = height;
        this.scaled = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB);
        this.indices = new byte[width * height];
        out.write("GIF89a".getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));
        short16(width);
        short16(height);
        out.write(0xf7); // global colour table, 8 bits per channel, 256 entries
        out.write(0);
        out.write(0);
        for (int i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
            if (i < 252) {
                out.write(i / 42 * 255 / 5);
                out.write(i / 6 % 7 * 255 / 6);
                out.write(i % 6 * 255 / 5);
            } else {
                out.write(0);
                out.write(0);
                out.write(0);
            }
        }
        // NETSCAPE2.0: loop forever.
        out.write(new byte[] {0x21, (byte) 0xff, 0x0b});
        out.write("NETSCAPE2.0".getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));
        out.write(new byte[] {0x03, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00});
    }

    /** Adds a frame shown for {@code delay} hundredths of a second, scaled to the GIF's size. */
    void frame(BufferedImage image, int delay) throws IOException {
        Graphics2D g = scaled.createGraphics();
        g.setRenderingHint(RenderingHints.KEY_INTERPOLATION, RenderingHints.VALUE_INTERPOLATION_BILINEAR);
        g.drawImage(image, 0, 0, width, height, null);
        g.dispose();
        int[] rgb = scaled.getRGB(0, 0, width, height, null, 0, width);
        for (int y = 0; y < height; y++) {
            for (int x = 0; x < width; x++) {
                int c = rgb[y * width + x];
                // Threshold in [-0.5, 0.5) of one palette step.
                float t = BAYER[(y & 3) * 4 + (x & 3)] / 16f - 0.5f;
                int r = level((c >> 16) & 255, 5, t);
                int gr = level((c >> 8) & 255, 6, t);
                int b = level(c & 255, 5, t);
                indices[y * width + x] = (byte) (r * 42 + gr * 6 + b);
            }
        }
        // Graphic control extension: delay, no transparency.
        out.write(new byte[] {0x21, (byte) 0xf9, 0x04, 0x00});
        short16(delay);
        out.write(new byte[] {0x00, 0x00});
        // Image descriptor: full frame, global palette.
        out.write(0x2c);
        short16(0);
        short16(0);
        short16(width);
        short16(height);
        out.write(0);
        lzw(indices);
    }

    void finish() throws IOException {
        out.write(0x3b);
        out.flush();
    }

    private static int level(int value, int steps, float threshold) {
        int level = Math.round(value * steps / 255f + threshold);
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(steps, level));
    }

    private void short16(int value) throws IOException {
        out.write(value & 0xff);
        out.write((value >> 8) & 0xff);
    }

    // ---- LZW with 8-bit codes, variable code width up to 12 bits ----

    private final byte[] block = new byte[255];
    private int blockSize;
    private int bitBuffer;
    private int bitCount;

    private void lzw(byte[] data) throws IOException {
        final int minCodeSize = 8;
        final int clear = 1 << minCodeSize;
        final int end = clear + 1;
        out.write(minCodeSize);
        blockSize = 0;
        bitBuffer = 0;
        bitCount = 0;
        // Dictionary: key = prefix code << 8 | byte, open addressing.
        int tableSize = 5003;
        int[] keys = new int[tableSize];
        int[] codes = new int[tableSize];
        java.util.Arrays.fill(keys, -1);
        int nextCode = end + 1;
        int codeSize = minCodeSize + 1;
        write(clear, codeSize);
        int prefix = data[0] & 0xff;
        for (int i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
            int c = data[i] & 0xff;
            int key = prefix << 8 | c;
            int slot = (key * 31 + 7) % tableSize;
            if (slot < 0) {
                slot += tableSize;
            }
            while (keys[slot] != -1 && keys[slot] != key) {
                slot = (slot + 1) % tableSize;
            }
            if (keys[slot] == key) {
                prefix = codes[slot];
                continue;
            }
            write(prefix, codeSize);
            if (nextCode < 4096) {
                keys[slot] = key;
                codes[slot] = nextCode++;
                if (nextCode > (1 << codeSize) && codeSize < 12) {
                    codeSize++;
                }
            } else {
                write(clear, codeSize);
                java.util.Arrays.fill(keys, -1);
                nextCode = end + 1;
                codeSize = minCodeSize + 1;
            }
            prefix = c;
        }
        write(prefix, codeSize);
        write(end, codeSize);
        if (bitCount > 0) {
            put(bitBuffer & 0xff);
        }
        if (blockSize > 0) {
            out.write(blockSize);
            out.write(block, 0, blockSize);
        }
        out.write(0);
    }

    private void write(int code, int size) throws IOException {
        bitBuffer |= code << bitCount;
        bitCount += size;
        while (bitCount >= 8) {
            put(bitBuffer & 0xff);
            bitBuffer >>>= 8;
            bitCount -= 8;
        }
    }

    private void put(int value) throws IOException {
        block[blockSize++] = (byte) value;
        if (blockSize == 255) {
            out.write(255);
            out.write(block, 0, 255);
            blockSize = 0;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/GlCapture.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL15.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL21.GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL21.GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER_BINDING;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL30.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL32.*;

/**
 * Reads the game's finished frame back for the replay buffer without stalling it: the frame is scaled
 * down into a small framebuffer, then read into one of a few pixel buffers, which is mapped a couple of
 * frames later when its fence says the copy is done. Every piece of GL state touched is put back.
 */
final class GlCapture {
    private static final int SLOTS = 3;

    private int width;
    private int height;
    private int fbo;
    private int texture;
    private final int[] pbo = new int[SLOTS];
    private final long[] fence = new long[SLOTS];
    private final long[] taken = new long[SLOTS];
    private int next;

    /**
     * Queues a read of the current frame ({@code fw} x {@code fh} pixels, default framebuffer) at
     * {@code w} x {@code h}, and hands any earlier read that finished to {@code done} as bottom-up RGBA.
     */
    void capture(int fw, int fh, int w, int h, long time, Consumer<Frame> done) {
        int readFbo = glGetInteger(GL_READ_FRAMEBUFFER_BINDING);
        int drawFbo = glGetInteger(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER_BINDING);
        int pack = glGetInteger(GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER_BINDING);
        int packAlignment = glGetInteger(GL_PACK_ALIGNMENT);
        int packRowLength = glGetInteger(GL_PACK_ROW_LENGTH);
        int texture2d = glGetInteger(GL_TEXTURE_BINDING_2D);
        boolean scissor = glIsEnabled(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
        try {
            if (w != width || h != height) {
                resize(w, h);
            }
            collect(done);
            if (fence[next] != 0) {
                return; // all slots busy; skip this frame rather than wait
            }
            glDisable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
            glBindFramebuffer(GL_READ_FRAMEBUFFER, 0);
            glBindFramebuffer(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER, fbo);
            glBlitFramebuffer(0, 0, fw, fh, 0, 0, w, h, GL_COLOR_BUFFER_BIT, GL_LINEAR);
            glBindFramebuffer(GL_READ_FRAMEBUFFER, fbo);
            glBindBuffer(GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER, pbo[next]);
            glPixelStorei(GL_PACK_ALIGNMENT, 1);
            glPixelStorei(GL_PACK_ROW_LENGTH, 0);
            glReadPixels(0, 0, w, h, GL_RGBA, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, 0L);
            fence[next] = glFenceSync(GL_SYNC_GPU_COMMANDS_COMPLETE, 0);
            taken[next] = time;
            next = (next + 1) % SLOTS;
        } finally {
            glBindFramebuffer(GL_READ_FRAMEBUFFER, readFbo);
            glBindFramebuffer(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER, drawFbo);
            glBindBuffer(GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER, pack);
            glPixelStorei(GL_PACK_ALIGNMENT, packAlignment);
            glPixelStorei(GL_PACK_ROW_LENGTH, packRowLength);
            glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, texture2d);
            if (scissor) {
                glEnable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
            }
        }
    }

    /** A finished read: bottom-up RGBA rows, owned by the receiver. */
    static final class Frame {
        final ByteBuffer pixels;
        final int width;
        final int height;
        final long time;

        Frame(ByteBuffer pixels, int width, int height, long time) {
            this.pixels = pixels;
            this.width = width;
            this.height = height;
            this.time = time;
        }
    }

    private void collect(Consumer<Frame> done) {
        for (int k = 0; k < SLOTS; k++) {
            int slot = (next + k) % SLOTS;
            if (fence[slot] == 0) {
                continue;
            }
            int status = glClientWaitSync(fence[slot], 0, 0);
            if (status != GL_ALREADY_SIGNALED && status != GL_CONDITION_SATISFIED) {
                continue;
            }
            glDeleteSync(fence[slot]);
            fence[slot] = 0;
            glBindBuffer(GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER, pbo[slot]);
            ByteBuffer mapped = glMapBuffer(GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER, GL_READ_ONLY, width * height * 4L, null);
            if (mapped != null) {
                ByteBuffer copy = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(width * height * 4).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
                copy.put(mapped).flip();
                glUnmapBuffer(GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER);
                done.accept(new Frame(copy, width, height, taken[slot]));
            }
        }
    }

    private void resize(int w, int h) {
        release();
        width = w;
        height = h;
        texture = glGenTextures();
        glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, texture);
        glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
        glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
        glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL_RGBA8, w, h, 0, GL_RGBA, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, (ByteBuffer) null);
        fbo = glGenFramebuffers();
        glBindFramebuffer(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER, fbo);
        glFramebufferTexture2D(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER, GL_COLOR_ATTACHMENT0, GL_TEXTURE_2D, texture, 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < SLOTS; i++) {
            pbo[i] = glGenBuffers();
            glBindBuffer(GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER, pbo[i]);
            glBufferData(GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER, (long) w * h * 4, GL_STREAM_READ);
        }
    }

    /** Frees the GL objects; the next capture makes new ones. */
    void release() {
        for (int i = 0; i < SLOTS; i++) {
            if (fence[i] != 0) {
                glDeleteSync(fence[i]);
                fence[i] = 0;
            }
            if (pbo[i] != 0) {
                glDeleteBuffers(pbo[i]);
                pbo[i] = 0;
            }
        }
        if (fbo != 0) {
            glDeleteFramebuffers(fbo);
            fbo = 0;
        }
        if (texture != 0) {
            glDeleteTextures(texture);
            texture = 0;
        }
        width = 0;
        height = 0;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/GlRenderer.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.nio.IntBuffer;

import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GLCapabilities;

import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12.GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12.GL_BGRA;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL12.GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL13.GL_ACTIVE_TEXTURE;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL13.GL_TEXTURE0;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL13.GL_TEXTURE1;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL13.glActiveTexture;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL14.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL15.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL20.*;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL21.GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL21.GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER_BINDING;
import static org.lwjgl.opengl.GL30.*;

/**
 * Draws a {@link Batch} with OpenGL 3.2 core (or a compatibility context of at least 3.2): rounded
 * rectangles as a signed distance in the fragment shader, glyphs from a single-channel atlas.
 *
 * <p>Minecraft caches GL state in {@code GlStateManager}, so every piece of state touched here is read
 * before drawing and put back afterwards; otherwise the game would render with wrong state.
 */
final class GlRenderer {
    private static final String VERTEX = "#version 150\n"
            + "uniform vec2 uViewport;\n"
            + "in vec2 aPos; in vec2 aUv; in vec4 aColor; in vec4 aParams;\n"
            + "out vec2 vUv; out vec4 vColor; out vec4 vParams;\n"
            + "void main() {\n"
            + "  vUv = aUv; vColor = aColor; vParams = aParams;\n"
            + "  gl_Position = vec4(aPos.x / uViewport.x * 2.0 - 1.0, 1.0 - aPos.y / uViewport.y * 2.0, 0.0, 1.0);\n"
            + "}\n";
    // vParams = (half width, half height, corner radius, mode); mode 1 samples the glyph atlas and
    // vUv is a texture coordinate, mode 2 samples the image the same way, mode 0 is a rounded box and
    // vUv is the offset from its centre.
    private static final String FRAGMENT = "#version 150\n"
            + "uniform sampler2D uAtlas;\n"
            + "uniform sampler2D uImage;\n"
            + "in vec2 vUv; in vec4 vColor; in vec4 vParams;\n"
            + "out vec4 fragColor;\n"
            + "void main() {\n"
            + "  if (vParams.w > 1.5) {\n"
            + "    fragColor = vec4(texture(uImage, vUv).rgb, vColor.a);\n"
            + "    return;\n"
            + "  }\n"
            + "  if (vParams.w > 0.5) {\n"
            + "    fragColor = vec4(vColor.rgb, vColor.a * texture(uAtlas, vUv).r);\n"
            + "    return;\n"
            + "  }\n"
            + "  vec2 q = abs(vUv) - vParams.xy + vParams.z;\n"
            + "  float d = length(max(q, 0.0)) + min(max(q.x, q.y), 0.0) - vParams.z;\n"
            + "  fragColor = vec4(vColor.rgb, vColor.a * clamp(0.5 - d, 0.0, 1.0));\n"
            + "}\n";

    private static final int STRIDE = Batch.STRIDE;
    private static final int MAX_QUADS = Batch.MAX_QUADS;

    private final int program;
    private final int uViewport;
    private final int vao;
    private final int vbo;
    private final int ebo;
    private final int atlas;
    private final int image;
    private long imageVersion = -1;
    private final boolean samplers;
    private int height;

    private final IntBuffer ints = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(16 * 4).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder()).asIntBuffer();
    private final int[] saved = new int[32];
    private final int[] savedViewport = new int[4];
    private final int[] savedScissor = new int[4];
    private final boolean[] savedColorMask = new boolean[4];
    private final ByteBuffer mask = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(16).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
    /** A compatibility context (Minecraft 1.13-1.16), where fixed-function alpha testing still applies. */
    private final boolean legacy;

    GlRenderer() {
        GLCapabilities caps = GL.getCapabilities();
        samplers = caps.OpenGL33 || caps.GL_ARB_sampler_objects;
        legacy = !caps.OpenGL32
                || (glGetInteger(org.lwjgl.opengl.GL32.GL_CONTEXT_PROFILE_MASK) & org.lwjgl.opengl.GL32.GL_CONTEXT_CORE_PROFILE_BIT) == 0;
        saveState();
        try {
            program = link(compile(GL_VERTEX_SHADER, VERTEX), compile(GL_FRAGMENT_SHADER, FRAGMENT));
            uViewport = glGetUniformLocation(program, "uViewport");

            vao = glGenVertexArrays();
            glBindVertexArray(vao);
            vbo = glGenBuffers();
            glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
            int pos = glGetAttribLocation(program, "aPos");
            int uv = glGetAttribLocation(program, "aUv");
            int params = glGetAttribLocation(program, "aParams");
            int color = glGetAttribLocation(program, "aColor");
            glEnableVertexAttribArray(pos);
            glVertexAttribPointer(pos, 2, GL_FLOAT, false, STRIDE, 0);
            glEnableVertexAttribArray(uv);
            glVertexAttribPointer(uv, 2, GL_FLOAT, false, STRIDE, 8);
            glEnableVertexAttribArray(params);
            glVertexAttribPointer(params, 4, GL_FLOAT, false, STRIDE, 16);
            glEnableVertexAttribArray(color);
            glVertexAttribPointer(color, 4, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, true, STRIDE, 32);

            ByteBuffer indices = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(MAX_QUADS * 6 * 4).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
            for (int i = 0; i < MAX_QUADS; i++) {
                int v = i * 4;
                indices.putInt(v).putInt(v + 1).putInt(v + 2).putInt(v + 2).putInt(v + 3).putInt(v);
            }
            indices.flip();
            ebo = glGenBuffers();
            glBindBuffer(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, ebo);
            glBufferData(GL_ELEMENT_ARRAY_BUFFER, indices, GL_STATIC_DRAW);

            atlas = glGenTextures();
            glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
            glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, atlas);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL, 0);
            // The game leaves its own unpack state behind; with a large row length the upload would read
            // past the end of our buffer.
            resetUnpack();
            glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL_R8, Batch.ATLAS, Batch.ATLAS, 0, GL_RED, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE,
                    (ByteBuffer) null);

            image = glGenTextures();
            glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE1);
            glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, image);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER, GL_LINEAR);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_S, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_WRAP_T, GL_CLAMP_TO_EDGE);
            glTexParameteri(GL_TEXTURE_2D, GL_TEXTURE_MAX_LEVEL, 0);
            glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);

            glUseProgram(program);
            glUniform1i(glGetUniformLocation(program, "uAtlas"), 0);
            glUniform1i(glGetUniformLocation(program, "uImage"), 1);
        } finally {
            restoreState();
        }
    }

    private static int compile(int type, String source) {
        int shader = glCreateShader(type);
        glShaderSource(shader, source);
        glCompileShader(shader);
        if (glGetShaderi(shader, GL_COMPILE_STATUS) == GL_FALSE) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("shader: " + glGetShaderInfoLog(shader));
        }
        return shader;
    }

    private static int link(int vertex, int fragment) {
        int program = glCreateProgram();
        glAttachShader(program, vertex);
        glAttachShader(program, fragment);
        // Some compatibility drivers draw nothing unless generic attribute 0 is in use.
        glBindAttribLocation(program, 0, "aPos");
        glLinkProgram(program);
        glDeleteShader(vertex);
        glDeleteShader(fragment);
        if (glGetProgrami(program, GL_LINK_STATUS) == GL_FALSE) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("program: " + glGetProgramInfoLog(program));
        }
        return program;
    }

    /** Draws the batch over the default framebuffer. */
    void draw(Batch batch) {
        height = batch.height;
        saveState();
        try {
            glBindFramebuffer(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER, 0);
            glViewport(0, 0, batch.width, batch.height);
            glDisable(GL_DEPTH_TEST);
            glDisable(GL_CULL_FACE);
            glDisable(GL_STENCIL_TEST);
            glDisable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
            glDisable(GL_COLOR_LOGIC_OP);
            glDisable(GL_POLYGON_OFFSET_FILL);
            glEnable(GL_BLEND);
            glBlendEquation(GL_FUNC_ADD);
            // Leave destination alpha alone: some compositors use the window's alpha channel.
            glBlendFuncSeparate(GL_SRC_ALPHA, GL_ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA, GL_ZERO, GL_ONE);
            glColorMask(true, true, true, true);
            glPolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK, GL_FILL);
            if (legacy) {
                glDisable(GL_ALPHA_TEST);
            }
            glUseProgram(program);
            glUniform2f(uViewport, batch.width, batch.height);
            glBindVertexArray(vao);
            glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, vbo);
            glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
            glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, atlas);
            if (samplers) {
                org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.glBindSampler(0, 0);
            }
            resetUnpack();
            if (batch.atlasDirty()) {
                int[] d = batch.dirty;
                glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, Batch.ATLAS);
                ByteBuffer region = batch.atlas.duplicate();
                region.position(d[1] * Batch.ATLAS + d[0]);
                glTexSubImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, d[0], d[1], d[2] - d[0], d[3] - d[1], GL_RED, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, region);
                glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, 0);
                batch.atlasUploaded();
            }
            if (batch.image != null && batch.imageVersion != imageVersion) {
                glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE1);
                glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, image);
                if (samplers) {
                    org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.glBindSampler(1, 0);
                }
                ByteBuffer pixels = batch.image.duplicate();
                pixels.position(0).limit(batch.imageWidth * batch.imageHeight * 4);
                glTexImage2D(GL_TEXTURE_2D, 0, GL_RGBA8, batch.imageWidth, batch.imageHeight, 0, GL_BGRA, GL_UNSIGNED_BYTE, pixels);
                imageVersion = batch.imageVersion;
                glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
            }
            if (batch.image != null) {
                glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE1);
                glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, image);
                if (samplers) {
                    org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.glBindSampler(1, 0);
                }
                glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
            }
            // A fresh store each time, so the driver does not wait for the previous draw.
            glBufferData(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, batch.vertices, GL_STREAM_DRAW);
            for (Batch.Draw d : batch.draws) {
                if (d.clip == null) {
                    glDisable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
                } else {
                    glEnable(GL_SCISSOR_TEST);
                    glScissor(d.clip[0], height - d.clip[3], d.clip[2] - d.clip[0], d.clip[3] - d.clip[1]);
                }
                glDrawElements(GL_TRIANGLES, d.quads * 6, GL_UNSIGNED_INT, (long) d.firstQuad * 6 * 4);
            }
        } finally {
            restoreState();
        }
    }

    private static void resetUnpack() {
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, 1);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, 0);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS, 0);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS, 0);
        glBindBuffer(GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER, 0);
    }

    private int get(int name) {
        return glGetInteger(name);
    }

    private void saveState() {
        int i = 0;
        saved[i++] = get(GL_CURRENT_PROGRAM);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_VERTEX_ARRAY_BINDING);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER_BINDING);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_ACTIVE_TEXTURE);
        glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE1);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_TEXTURE_BINDING_2D);
        saved[i++] = samplers ? get(org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.GL_SAMPLER_BINDING) : 0;
        glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_TEXTURE_BINDING_2D);
        saved[i++] = samplers ? get(org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.GL_SAMPLER_BINDING) : 0;
        saved[i++] = get(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER_BINDING);
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_BLEND) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_SRC_RGB);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_DST_RGB);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_SRC_ALPHA);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_DST_ALPHA);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_EQUATION_RGB);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_BLEND_EQUATION_ALPHA);
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_DEPTH_TEST) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_CULL_FACE) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_STENCIL_TEST) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_SCISSOR_TEST) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_COLOR_LOGIC_OP) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = glIsEnabled(GL_POLYGON_OFFSET_FILL) ? 1 : 0;
        saved[i++] = get(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS);
        saved[i++] = get(GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER_BINDING);
        saved[i++] = legacy && glIsEnabled(GL_ALPHA_TEST) ? 1 : 0;
        ints.clear();
        glGetIntegerv(GL_POLYGON_MODE, ints);
        saved[i] = ints.get(0);
        ints.clear();
        glGetIntegerv(GL_VIEWPORT, ints);
        ints.get(savedViewport);
        ints.clear();
        glGetIntegerv(GL_SCISSOR_BOX, ints);
        ints.get(savedScissor);
        mask.clear();
        glGetBooleanv(GL_COLOR_WRITEMASK, mask);
        for (int c = 0; c < 4; c++) {
            savedColorMask[c] = mask.get(c) != 0;
        }
    }

    private void restoreState() {
        int i = 0;
        glUseProgram(saved[i++]);
        glBindVertexArray(saved[i++]);
        glBindBuffer(GL_ARRAY_BUFFER, saved[i++]);
        int activeTexture = saved[i++];
        glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE1);
        glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, saved[i++]);
        int sampler1 = saved[i++];
        if (samplers) {
            org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.glBindSampler(1, sampler1);
        }
        glActiveTexture(GL_TEXTURE0);
        glBindTexture(GL_TEXTURE_2D, saved[i++]);
        int sampler = saved[i++];
        if (samplers) {
            org.lwjgl.opengl.GL33.glBindSampler(0, sampler);
        }
        glActiveTexture(activeTexture);
        glBindFramebuffer(GL_DRAW_FRAMEBUFFER, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_BLEND, saved[i++]);
        glBlendFuncSeparate(saved[i], saved[i + 1], saved[i + 2], saved[i + 3]);
        i += 4;
        glBlendEquationSeparate(saved[i], saved[i + 1]);
        i += 2;
        toggle(GL_DEPTH_TEST, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_CULL_FACE, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_STENCIL_TEST, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_SCISSOR_TEST, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_COLOR_LOGIC_OP, saved[i++]);
        toggle(GL_POLYGON_OFFSET_FILL, saved[i++]);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ALIGNMENT, saved[i++]);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_ROW_LENGTH, saved[i++]);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_ROWS, saved[i++]);
        glPixelStorei(GL_UNPACK_SKIP_PIXELS, saved[i++]);
        glBindBuffer(GL_PIXEL_UNPACK_BUFFER, saved[i++]);
        if (legacy) {
            toggle(GL_ALPHA_TEST, saved[i]);
        }
        i++;
        glPolygonMode(GL_FRONT_AND_BACK, saved[i]);
        glViewport(savedViewport[0], savedViewport[1], savedViewport[2], savedViewport[3]);
        glScissor(savedScissor[0], savedScissor[1], savedScissor[2], savedScissor[3]);
        glColorMask(savedColorMask[0], savedColorMask[1], savedColorMask[2], savedColorMask[3]);
    }

    private static void toggle(int cap, int enabled) {
        if (enabled != 0) {
            glEnable(cap);
        } else {
            glDisable(cap);
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/GlfwPlatform.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.nio.DoubleBuffer;
import java.nio.FloatBuffer;
import java.nio.IntBuffer;

import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFWCharCallbackI;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFWCharModsCallbackI;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFWCursorPosCallbackI;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFWKeyCallbackI;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFWMouseButtonCallbackI;
import org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFWScrollCallbackI;

import static org.lwjgl.glfw.GLFW.*;

/**
 * GLFW backend (Minecraft 1.13 to 26.2). The game's input callbacks are wrapped as it installs them;
 * each wrapper offers the event to the overlay first and forwards it to the game unless consumed.
 */
final class GlfwPlatform implements Platform {
    private final Overlay overlay;
    long window;
    private boolean ownCursorCall;
    private int savedMode = GLFW_CURSOR_NORMAL;
    /** The cursor mode the game asked for while the overlay held the cursor. */
    private int pendingMode = GLFW_CURSOR_NORMAL;
    private double savedX;
    private double savedY;
    /**
     * Nesting depth of the wrappers. A mod that chains to the callback it replaced calls a wrapper
     * from inside another one; only the outermost one may offer the event to the overlay.
     */
    private int depth;

    private final IntBuffer w = ints();
    private final IntBuffer h = ints();
    private final FloatBuffer sx = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(4).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder()).asFloatBuffer();
    private final FloatBuffer sy = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(4).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder()).asFloatBuffer();
    private final DoubleBuffer cx = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(8).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder()).asDoubleBuffer();
    private final DoubleBuffer cy = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(8).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder()).asDoubleBuffer();

    GlfwPlatform(Overlay overlay) {
        this.overlay = overlay;
    }

    private static IntBuffer ints() {
        return ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(4).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder()).asIntBuffer();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean hasWindow() {
        return window != 0;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean hasGlContext() {
        return glfwGetCurrentContext() != 0;
    }

    @Override
    public void sizes(int[] out) {
        glfwGetFramebufferSize(window, w, h);
        out[0] = w.get(0);
        out[1] = h.get(0);
        glfwGetWindowSize(window, w, h);
        out[2] = w.get(0);
        out[3] = h.get(0);
    }

    @Override
    public float contentScale() {
        glfwGetWindowContentScale(window, sx, sy);
        return Math.max(1, sx.get(0));
    }

    @Override
    public String clipboard() {
        return glfwGetClipboardString(window);
    }

    @Override
    public void setClipboard(String text) {
        glfwSetClipboardString(window, text);
    }

    @Override
    public void showCursor() {
        savedMode = glfwGetInputMode(window, GLFW_CURSOR);
        pendingMode = savedMode;
        glfwGetCursorPos(window, cx, cy);
        savedX = cx.get(0);
        savedY = cy.get(0);
        setMode(GLFW_CURSOR_NORMAL);
        if (savedMode == GLFW_CURSOR_DISABLED) {
            glfwGetWindowSize(window, w, h);
            glfwSetCursorPos(window, w.get(0) / 2.0, h.get(0) / 2.0);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void restoreCursor() {
        setMode(pendingMode);
        if (pendingMode == GLFW_CURSOR_DISABLED && savedMode == GLFW_CURSOR_DISABLED) {
            // Put the virtual cursor back where the game last saw it, so the camera does not jump.
            glfwSetCursorPos(window, savedX, savedY);
        } else if (pendingMode == GLFW_CURSOR_DISABLED) {
            // The game grabbed the mouse meanwhile and expects it centred, as its grab call put it.
            glfwGetWindowSize(window, w, h);
            glfwSetCursorPos(window, w.get(0) / 2.0, h.get(0) / 2.0);
        }
    }

    private void setMode(int mode) {
        ownCursorCall = true;
        try {
            glfwSetInputMode(window, GLFW_CURSOR, mode);
        } finally {
            ownCursorCall = false;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Hook on {@code glfwSetInputMode}: returns true to skip the game's call. Under Ixeris the overlay's
     * own calls run a second time on its polling thread, where they must not be mistaken for the game's.
     */
    boolean onInputMode(int mode, int value) {
        if (mode != GLFW_CURSOR || ownCursorCall || !overlay.isOpen() || !overlay.onRenderThread()) {
            return false;
        }
        pendingMode = value;
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Hook on the {@code glfwSet*Callback} methods: returns the callback to install instead. The setter
     * decides the type, since one object may implement several callback interfaces.
     */
    Object wrap(String setter, Object callback) {
        if ("glfwSetKeyCallback".equals(setter) && callback instanceof GLFWKeyCallbackI) {
            final GLFWKeyCallbackI game = (GLFWKeyCallbackI) callback;
            return (GLFWKeyCallbackI) (win, key, scancode, action, mods) -> {
                if (!offer(() -> overlay.key(key, action, mods))) {
                    pass(() -> game.invoke(win, key, scancode, action, mods));
                }
            };
        }
        if ("glfwSetCharModsCallback".equals(setter) && callback instanceof GLFWCharModsCallbackI) {
            final GLFWCharModsCallbackI game = (GLFWCharModsCallbackI) callback;
            return (GLFWCharModsCallbackI) (win, codepoint, mods) -> {
                if (!offer(() -> overlay.character(codepoint))) {
                    pass(() -> game.invoke(win, codepoint, mods));
                }
            };
        }
        if ("glfwSetCharCallback".equals(setter) && callback instanceof GLFWCharCallbackI) {
            final GLFWCharCallbackI game = (GLFWCharCallbackI) callback;
            return (GLFWCharCallbackI) (win, codepoint) -> {
                if (!offer(() -> overlay.character(codepoint))) {
                    pass(() -> game.invoke(win, codepoint));
                }
            };
        }
        if ("glfwSetMouseButtonCallback".equals(setter) && callback instanceof GLFWMouseButtonCallbackI) {
            final GLFWMouseButtonCallbackI game = (GLFWMouseButtonCallbackI) callback;
            return (GLFWMouseButtonCallbackI) (win, button, action, mods) -> {
                if (!offer(() -> overlay.button(button, action))) {
                    pass(() -> game.invoke(win, button, action, mods));
                }
            };
        }
        if ("glfwSetCursorPosCallback".equals(setter) && callback instanceof GLFWCursorPosCallbackI) {
            final GLFWCursorPosCallbackI game = (GLFWCursorPosCallbackI) callback;
            return (GLFWCursorPosCallbackI) (win, x, y) -> {
                if (!offer(() -> overlay.cursor(x, y))) {
                    pass(() -> game.invoke(win, x, y));
                }
            };
        }
        if ("glfwSetScrollCallback".equals(setter) && callback instanceof GLFWScrollCallbackI) {
            final GLFWScrollCallbackI game = (GLFWScrollCallbackI) callback;
            return (GLFWScrollCallbackI) (win, dx, dy) -> {
                if (!offer(() -> overlay.scroll(dy))) {
                    pass(() -> game.invoke(win, dx, dy));
                }
            };
        }
        return callback;
    }

    private interface Handler {
        boolean handle();
    }

    /** Offers an event to the overlay; returns true when it consumed it. Overlay failures never block input. */
    private boolean offer(Handler handler) {
        if (depth > 0) {
            return false;
        }
        try {
            return handler.handle();
        } catch (Throwable e) {
            overlay.fail(e);
            return false;
        }
    }

    private void pass(Runnable game) {
        depth++;
        try {
            game.run();
        } finally {
            depth--;
        }
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

/**
 * Key codes and modifier bits the overlay works with. They are GLFW's values; a backend for another
 * window library translates its own codes into these.
 */
final class Keys {
    static final int A = 65;
    static final int C = 67;
    static final int V = 86;
    static final int X = 88;
    static final int ESCAPE = 256;
    static final int ENTER = 257;
    static final int TAB = 258;
    static final int BACKSPACE = 259;
    static final int DELETE = 261;
    static final int RIGHT = 262;
    static final int LEFT = 263;
    static final int DOWN = 264;
    static final int UP = 265;
    static final int PAGE_UP = 266;
    static final int PAGE_DOWN = 267;
    static final int HOME = 268;
    static final int END = 269;
    static final int F2 = 291;
    static final int KP_ENTER = 335;

    static final int MOD_SHIFT = 1;
    static final int MOD_CONTROL = 2;
    static final int MOD_ALT = 4;
    static final int MOD_SUPER = 8;

    static final int RELEASE = 0;
    static final int PRESS = 1;
    static final int REPEAT = 2;

    private Keys() {
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/LegacyBackend.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

/**
 * The LWJGL 2 backend (Minecraft 1.12.2 and older), kept behind an interface because it is compiled
 * against LWJGL 2, whose classes differ from LWJGL 3, and is only loaded when the game uses it.
 */
interface LegacyBackend extends Platform {
    /**
     * A hooked LWJGL 2 call on {@code type} ({@code Display}, {@code Keyboard} or {@code Mouse}),
     * answered as {@link Overlay#apply} does; {@code result} is set on exit.
     */
    Object call(Class<?> type, String method, Object[] args, Object result, boolean exit);
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Links.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Locale;

/**
 * Opens web pages and folders with the operating system's handler. java.awt.Desktop is avoided: it
 * starts AWT inside the game, which fights GLFW for the main thread on macOS. Each target is passed
 * as one process argument, never through a shell.
 */
final class Links {
    private Links() {
    }

    /** Opens an http(s) address in the default browser. */
    static void browse(String url) throws IOException {
        if (!url.startsWith("https://") && !url.startsWith("http://")) {
            throw new IOException("not a web address: " + url);
        }
        open(url);
    }

    /** Opens a folder in the file manager. */
    static void folder(Path dir) throws IOException {
        open(dir.toAbsolutePath().toString());
    }

    private static void open(String target) throws IOException {
        String os = System.getProperty("os.name", "").toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        boolean windows = os.startsWith("windows");
        ProcessBuilder builder;
        if (windows) {
            builder = new ProcessBuilder("rundll32", "url.dll,FileProtocolHandler", target);
        } else if (os.startsWith("mac")) {
            builder = new ProcessBuilder("open", target);
        } else {
            builder = new ProcessBuilder("xdg-open", target);
        }
        // Nobody reads the handler's output; a full pipe would block it.
        builder.redirectErrorStream(true).redirectOutput(new File(windows ? "NUL" : "/dev/null"));
        builder.start();
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Media.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
import java.util.concurrent.ScheduledExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * What the system's media session plays (Spotify, a browser tab, any player) and its play/next/previous
 * controls. On Windows a PowerShell child talks to the system media transport controls
 * ({@code media.ps1}); on Linux {@code playerctl} speaks MPRIS. Elsewhere it stays unavailable.
 * Started the first time the overlay opens, so a game that never opens it starts no process.
 */
final class Media {
    private static final boolean WINDOWS = System.getProperty("os.name", "").toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).startsWith("windows");
    private static final boolean LINUX = System.getProperty("os.name", "").toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).startsWith("linux");

    /** Whether anything is known to play or be paused right now. */
    volatile boolean active;
    volatile boolean playing;
    volatile String title = "";
    volatile String artist = "";
    /** Set once the backend gave up (no PowerShell, no playerctl). */
    volatile boolean unavailable = !WINDOWS && !LINUX;

    private final ScheduledExecutorService io;
    private final Executor render;
    private final Consumer<Throwable> onError;
    private boolean started;
    private Process bridge;
    private OutputStream commands;
    /** Called on the render thread when a new track starts playing. */
    Consumer<String> onTrack = track -> { };
    private String lastTrack = "";

    Media(ScheduledExecutorService io, Executor render, Consumer<Throwable> onError) {
        this.io = io;
        this.render = render;
        this.onError = onError;
    }

    void start() {
        if (started || unavailable) {
            return;
        }
        started = true;
        if (WINDOWS) {
            io.execute(this::startBridge);
        } else {
            io.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::pollPlayerctl, 0, 1500, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
        }
    }

    /** Toggles play/pause, or skips with "next" and "prev". */
    void command(String command) {
        if (WINDOWS) {
            OutputStream out = commands;
            if (out == null) {
                return;
            }
            io.execute(() -> {
                try {
                    out.write((command + "\n").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                    out.flush();
                } catch (IOException e) {
                    onError.accept(e);
                }
            });
        } else if (LINUX) {
            String verb = "play".equals(command) ? "play-pause" : "next".equals(command) ? "next" : "previous";
            io.execute(() -> {
                try {
                    run("playerctl", verb);
                } catch (IOException e) {
                    onError.accept(e);
                }
            });
        }
    }

    void stop() {
        Process process = bridge;
        if (process != null) {
            process.destroy();
        }
    }

    private void startBridge() {
        try {
            String script = resource("media.ps1");
            String encoded = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(script.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_16LE));
            ProcessBuilder builder = new ProcessBuilder("powershell.exe", "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive",
                    "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-EncodedCommand", encoded);
            builder.redirectErrorStream(true);
            Process process = builder.start();
            bridge = process;
            commands = process.getOutputStream();
            Thread reader = new Thread(() -> readBridge(process), "k0t1k-overlay-media");
            reader.setDaemon(true);
            reader.start();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            unavailable = true;
            onError.accept(e);
        }
    }

    private void readBridge(Process process) {
        try (BufferedReader in = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(process.getInputStream(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8))) {
            for (String line; (line = in.readLine()) != null; ) {
                if (line.startsWith("S\t")) {
                    String[] parts = line.split("\t", -1);
                    update(parts.length > 1 && "Playing".equals(parts[1]), part(parts, 2), part(parts, 3));
                } else if ("N".equals(line)) {
                    update(false, "", "");
                    active = false;
                } else if (!line.trim().isEmpty()) {
                    Overlay.log("media: " + line);
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            onError.accept(e);
        }
        active = false;
        unavailable = true;
    }

    private void pollPlayerctl() {
        if (unavailable) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            String out = run("playerctl", "metadata", "--format", "{{status}}\t{{title}}\t{{artist}}").trim();
            if (out.isEmpty() || !out.contains("\t")) {
                active = false;
                return;
            }
            String[] parts = out.split("\t", -1);
            update("Playing".equals(parts[0]), part(parts, 1), part(parts, 2));
        } catch (IOException e) {
            // No playerctl installed: say so once and stop polling.
            unavailable = true;
            Overlay.log("media controls need playerctl: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private void update(boolean nowPlaying, String newTitle, String newArtist) {
        active = !newTitle.isEmpty();
        playing = nowPlaying;
        title = newTitle;
        artist = newArtist;
        String track = label();
        if (nowPlaying && !track.isEmpty() && !track.equals(lastTrack)) {
            lastTrack = track;
            render.execute(() -> onTrack.accept(track));
        }
    }

    /** "Title — Artist", or just the title. */
    String label() {
        String t = title;
        String a = artist;
        return a.isEmpty() ? t : t + " — " + a;
    }

    private static String part(String[] parts, int index) {
        return index < parts.length ? parts[index].trim() : "";
    }

    private static String run(String... command) throws IOException {
        Process process = new ProcessBuilder(command).redirectErrorStream(true).start();
        String out = read(process.getInputStream());
        try {
            process.waitFor(5, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
        return out;
    }

    private static String resource(String name) throws IOException {
        try (InputStream in = Media.class.getResourceAsStream(name)) {
            if (in == null) {
                throw new IOException(name + " is missing from the overlay jar");
            }
            return read(in);
        }
    }

    private static String read(InputStream in) throws IOException {
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        byte[] buffer = new byte[4096];
        for (int n; (n = in.read(buffer)) > 0; ) {
            out.write(buffer, 0, n);
        }
        return new String(out.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Mods.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.DirectoryStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
import java.util.function.Consumer;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;
import java.util.zip.ZipEntry;
import java.util.zip.ZipFile;

/**
 * The instance's mods, read from the jars in {@code <gameDir>/mods}: name, version and description from
 * {@code fabric.mod.json}, {@code quilt.mod.json} or {@code META-INF/(neoforge.)mods.toml}. Scanned on
 * the io thread when the mods tab first opens.
 */
final class Mods {
    static final class Mod {
        final String file;
        final boolean enabled;
        String id = "";
        String name = "";
        String version = "";
        String description = "";
        String homepage = "";

        Mod(String file, boolean enabled) {
            this.file = file;
            this.enabled = enabled;
        }
    }

    private final Path folder;
    private final Executor io;
    private final Executor render;
    private final Consumer<Throwable> onError;
    /** Sorted by name; replaced as a whole on the render thread when a scan finishes. */
    List<Mod> mods = new ArrayList<>();
    boolean scanned;
    private boolean scanning;

    Mods(Path gameDir, Executor io, Executor render, Consumer<Throwable> onError) {
        this.folder = gameDir.resolve("mods");
        this.io = io;
        this.render = render;
        this.onError = onError;
    }

    void scan() {
        if (scanning) {
            return;
        }
        scanning = true;
        io.execute(() -> {
            List<Mod> found = new ArrayList<>();
            try {
                if (Files.isDirectory(folder)) {
                    try (DirectoryStream<Path> files = Files.newDirectoryStream(folder)) {
                        for (Path file : files) {
                            String name = file.getFileName().toString();
                            boolean enabled = name.endsWith(".jar");
                            if (enabled || name.endsWith(".jar.disabled")) {
                                found.add(read(file, enabled));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
            }
            found.sort((a, b) -> a.name.compareToIgnoreCase(b.name));
            render.execute(() -> {
                mods = found;
                scanned = true;
                scanning = false;
            });
        });
    }

    private static Mod read(Path file, boolean enabled) {
        Mod mod = new Mod(file.getFileName().toString(), enabled);
        try (ZipFile zip = new ZipFile(file.toFile())) {
            String fabric = entry(zip, "fabric.mod.json");
            String quilt = fabric == null ? entry(zip, "quilt.mod.json") : null;
            String toml = fabric == null && quilt == null ? entry(zip, "META-INF/neoforge.mods.toml") : null;
            if (toml == null && fabric == null && quilt == null) {
                toml = entry(zip, "META-INF/mods.toml");
            }
            if (fabric != null) {
                Object json = Json.parse(fabric);
                mod.id = Json.string(json, "id");
                mod.name = Json.string(json, "name");
                mod.version = Json.string(json, "version");
                mod.description = Json.string(json, "description");
                mod.homepage = Json.string(Json.get(json, "contact"), "homepage");
            } else if (quilt != null) {
                Object loader = Json.get(Json.parse(quilt), "quilt_loader");
                Object meta = Json.get(loader, "metadata");
                mod.id = Json.string(loader, "id");
                mod.version = Json.string(loader, "version");
                mod.name = Json.string(meta, "name");
                mod.description = Json.string(meta, "description");
                mod.homepage = Json.string(Json.get(meta, "contact"), "homepage");
            } else if (toml != null) {
                mod.id = tomlValue(toml, "modId");
                mod.name = tomlValue(toml, "displayName");
                mod.version = tomlValue(toml, "version");
                mod.description = tomlValue(toml, "description");
                mod.homepage = tomlValue(toml, "displayURL");
            }
        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
            // A broken or unusual jar still shows with its file name.
        }
        if (mod.version.startsWith("$")) {
            mod.version = "";
        }
        if (mod.name.isEmpty()) {
            mod.name = mod.id.isEmpty() ? mod.file.replaceAll("\\.jar(\\.disabled)?$", "") : mod.id;
        }
        mod.description = mod.description.trim();
        return mod;
    }

    private static String entry(ZipFile zip, String name) throws IOException {
        ZipEntry entry = zip.getEntry(name);
        if (entry == null) {
            return null;
        }
        try (InputStream in = zip.getInputStream(entry)) {
            ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
            for (int n; (n = in.read(buffer)) > 0; ) {
                out.write(buffer, 0, n);
            }
            return new String(out.toByteArray(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        }
    }

    /** The first {@code key = "..."} or {@code key = '''...'''} in a mods.toml; enough for display. */
    private static String tomlValue(String toml, String key) {
        Matcher m = Pattern.compile("(?m)^\\s*" + key + "\\s*=\\s*(?:'''([\\s\\S]*?)'''|\"\"\"([\\s\\S]*?)\"\"\"|\"((?:[^\"\\\\]|\\\\.)*)\"|'([^']*)')")
                .matcher(toml);
        if (!m.find()) {
            return "";
        }
        for (int g = 1; g <= 4; g++) {
            if (m.group(g) != null) {
                return g == 3 ? m.group(g).replace("\\\"", "\"").replace("\\n", "\n") : m.group(g);
            }
        }
        return "";
    }

    /** Whether the mod matches a search typed by the player. */
    static boolean matches(Mod mod, String query) {
        if (query.isEmpty()) {
            return true;
        }
        String q = query.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        return mod.name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(q) || mod.id.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(q)
                || mod.description.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(q) || mod.file.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains(q);
    }

    /** A minimal JSON reader for mod metadata: objects, arrays, strings, numbers, literals. */
    static final class Json {
        private final String s;
        private int i;

        private Json(String s) {
            this.s = s;
        }

        static Object parse(String text) {
            Json json = new Json(text);
            json.skip();
            return json.value();
        }

        static Object get(Object object, String key) {
            return object instanceof Map ? ((Map<?, ?>) object).get(key) : null;
        }

        /** A string member, or "" when missing or not a string (fabric also allows arrays for some). */
        static String string(Object object, String key) {
            Object value = get(object, key);
            return value instanceof String ? (String) value : "";
        }

        private Object value() {
            char c = s.charAt(i);
            if (c == '{') {
                Map<String, Object> map = new LinkedHashMap<>();
                i++;
                skip();
                while (s.charAt(i) != '}') {
                    String key = string();
                    skip();
                    expect(':');
                    skip();
                    map.put(key, value());
                    skip();
                    if (s.charAt(i) == ',') {
                        i++;
                        skip();
                    }
                }
                i++;
                return map;
            }
            if (c == '[') {
                List<Object> list = new ArrayList<>();
                i++;
                skip();
                while (s.charAt(i) != ']') {
                    list.add(value());
                    skip();
                    if (s.charAt(i) == ',') {
                        i++;
                        skip();
                    }
                }
                i++;
                return list;
            }
            if (c == '"') {
                return string();
            }
            int start = i;
            while (i < s.length() && ",}] \t\r\n".indexOf(s.charAt(i)) < 0) {
                i++;
            }
            return s.substring(start, i);
        }

        private String string() {
            expect('"');
            StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
            while (s.charAt(i) != '"') {
                char c = s.charAt(i++);
                if (c != '\\') {
                    out.append(c);
                    continue;
                }
                char e = s.charAt(i++);
                switch (e) {
                    case 'n':
                        out.append('\n');
                        break;
                    case 't':
                        out.append('\t');
                        break;
                    case 'r':
                        break;
                    case 'u':
                        out.append((char) Integer.parseInt(s.substring(i, i + 4), 16));
                        i += 4;
                        break;
                    default:
                        out.append(e);
                }
            }
            i++;
            return out.toString();
        }

        private void expect(char c) {
            if (s.charAt(i) != c) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException("expected " + c + " at " + i);
            }
            i++;
        }

        private void skip() {
            while (i < s.length()) {
                char c = s.charAt(i);
                if (c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\r' || c == '\n' || c == '﻿') {
                    i++;
                } else if (c == '/' && i + 1 < s.length() && s.charAt(i + 1) == '/') {
                    // fabric.mod.json is read leniently; some mods ship comments.
                    while (i < s.length() && s.charAt(i) != '\n') {
                        i++;
                    }
                } else {
                    return;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/ModsView.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/** The mods tab: the instance's mods with a search, and what the selected one does. */
final class ModsView {
    private final Mods mods;
    private final Strings strings;
    private final Overlay overlay;
    private final TextEdit search = new TextEdit();
    private Mods.Mod selected;
    private float listScroll;

    ModsView(Mods mods, Strings strings, Overlay overlay) {
        this.mods = mods;
        this.strings = strings;
        this.overlay = overlay;
    }

    void draw(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (!mods.scanned) {
            mods.scan();
        }
        float listW = Math.min(320, w * 0.38f);
        ui.field(search, x, y, listW, 34, false, strings.get("mods.search"));
        String query = search.value().trim();
        List<Mods.Mod> shown = new ArrayList<>();
        int disabled = 0;
        for (Mods.Mod mod : mods.mods) {
            if (!mod.enabled) {
                disabled++;
            }
            if (Mods.matches(mod, query)) {
                shown.add(mod);
            }
        }
        if (selected == null && !shown.isEmpty()) {
            selected = shown.get(0);
        }

        float listY = y + 46;
        float listH = h - 46;
        ui.panel(x, listY, listW, listH, 10, Ui.BASE, Ui.LINE);
        float itemH = 44;
        listScroll -= ui.takeScroll(x, listY, listW, listH) * itemH * 2;
        listScroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(listScroll, Math.max(0, shown.size() * itemH + 8 - listH)));
        ui.clip(x + 1, listY + 1, listW - 2, listH - 2);
        if (shown.isEmpty()) {
            ui.text(ui.regular, 13, x + 14, listY + 14, Ui.FAINT,
                    !mods.scanned ? strings.get("mods.loading") : mods.mods.isEmpty() ? strings.get("mods.none") : strings.get("chat.notFound"));
        }
        boolean inList = ui.hover(x, listY, listW, listH);
        for (int i = 0; i < shown.size(); i++) {
            Mods.Mod mod = shown.get(i);
            float iy = listY + 4 + i * itemH - listScroll;
            if (iy + itemH < listY || iy > listY + listH) {
                continue;
            }
            boolean active = mod == selected;
            boolean hot = inList && ui.hover(x + 4, iy, listW - 8, itemH - 4);
            if (active || hot) {
                ui.round(x + 4, iy, listW - 8, itemH - 4, 7, active ? 0x26a259f7 : Ui.RAISED);
            }
            ui.textFit(ui.bold, 13, x + 14, iy + 5, listW - 28, mod.enabled ? Ui.FG : Ui.FAINT, mod.name);
            String sub = (mod.version.isEmpty() ? mod.file : mod.version) + (mod.enabled ? "" : " · " + strings.get("mods.disabled"));
            ui.textFit(ui.regular, 11, x + 14, iy + 23, listW - 28, mod.enabled ? Ui.FAINT : Ui.WARN, sub);
            if (hot && ui.clickedIn(x + 4, iy, listW - 8, itemH - 4)) {
                selected = mod;
            }
        }
        ui.unclip();

        float dx = x + listW + 14;
        float dw = w - listW - 14;
        String count = strings.format("mods.count", mods.mods.size() - disabled, disabled);
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, dx + 2, y + (34 - ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 12)) / 2, Ui.FAINT, count);
        float refreshW = ui.buttonWidth(strings.get("mods.refresh"));
        if (ui.button(dx + dw - refreshW, y, refreshW, 34, strings.get("mods.refresh"), false)) {
            mods.scan();
        }
        ui.panel(dx, listY, dw, listH, 10, Ui.BASE, Ui.LINE);
        if (selected == null || !mods.mods.contains(selected)) {
            selected = null;
            return;
        }
        float px = dx + 18;
        float pw = dw - 36;
        float py = listY + 16;
        ui.textFit(ui.bold, 18, px, py, pw, Ui.FG, selected.name);
        py += 28;
        String meta = (selected.id.isEmpty() ? "" : selected.id + " · ") + (selected.version.isEmpty() ? "" : selected.version + " · ") + selected.file;
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, px, py, pw, Ui.FAINT, meta);
        py += 26;
        ui.text(ui.bold, 12, px, py, Ui.MUTED, strings.get("mods.what"));
        py += 20;
        String description = selected.description.isEmpty() ? strings.get("mods.noDescription") : selected.description;
        float lh = ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 14);
        float buttonsY = listY + listH - 50;
        List<int[]> lines = ui.wrap(ui.regular, 14, description, pw);
        ui.clip(px, py, pw, buttonsY - py - 8);
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++) {
            int[] span = lines.get(i);
            ui.text(ui.regular, 14, px, py + i * lh, selected.description.isEmpty() ? Ui.FAINT : Ui.FG, description, span[0], span[1]);
        }
        ui.unclip();

        float bx = px;
        String modrinth = strings.get("mods.modrinth");
        float mw = ui.buttonWidth(modrinth);
        if (ui.button(bx, buttonsY, mw, 34, modrinth, true)) {
            open(modrinthSearch(selected));
        }
        bx += mw + 8;
        if (!selected.homepage.isEmpty()) {
            String home = strings.get("mods.homepage");
            float hw = ui.buttonWidth(home);
            if (ui.button(bx, buttonsY, hw, 34, home, false)) {
                open(selected.homepage);
            }
        }
    }

    private static String modrinthSearch(Mods.Mod mod) {
        try {
            return "https://modrinth.com/mods?q=" + URLEncoder.encode(mod.name, StandardCharsets.UTF_8.name());
        } catch (java.io.UnsupportedEncodingException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(e);
        }
    }

    private void open(String url) {
        try {
            Links.browse(url);
            overlay.toast(strings.get("mods.opened"), url);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            overlay.report(e);
            overlay.toast(strings.get("error"), e.getMessage());
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Notes.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.AtomicMoveNotSupportedException;
import java.nio.file.DirectoryStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Notes, one UTF-8 text file per note in {@code notes/}, named by creation time. The render thread
 * owns {@link #notes}; disk work runs on {@code io}.
 */
final class Notes {
    private static final long SAVE_DELAY_MS = 800;

    static final class Note {
        final String id;
        final TextEdit edit;
        long modified;
        boolean dirty;
        long dirtySince;

        Note(String id, String text, long modified) {
            this.id = id;
            this.edit = new TextEdit(text);
            this.modified = modified;
        }

        String title() {
            String text = edit.value().trim();
            int newline = text.indexOf('\n');
            return newline < 0 ? text : text.substring(0, newline).trim();
        }
    }

    private final Path dir;
    private final Executor io;
    private final Executor render;
    private final Consumer<Throwable> onError;
    /** Newest first. */
    final List<Note> notes = new ArrayList<>();
    boolean loaded;

    Notes(Path dataDir, Executor io, Executor render, Consumer<Throwable> onError) {
        this.dir = dataDir.resolve("notes");
        this.io = io;
        this.render = render;
        this.onError = onError;
    }

    void load() {
        io.execute(() -> {
            List<Note> found = new ArrayList<>();
            try {
                if (Files.isDirectory(dir)) {
                    try (DirectoryStream<Path> files = Files.newDirectoryStream(dir, "*.txt")) {
                        for (Path file : files) {
                            String name = file.getFileName().toString();
                            found.add(new Note(name.substring(0, name.length() - 4),
                                    new String(Files.readAllBytes(file), StandardCharsets.UTF_8),
                                    Files.getLastModifiedTime(file).toMillis()));
                        }
                    }
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
            }
            found.sort(Comparator.comparingLong((Note note) -> note.modified).reversed());
            render.execute(() -> {
                notes.addAll(found);
                loaded = true;
            });
        });
    }

    /** A new empty note; it reaches the disk with its first edit. */
    Note create() {
        Note note = new Note(Long.toString(System.currentTimeMillis()), "", System.currentTimeMillis());
        notes.add(0, note);
        return note;
    }

    void changed(Note note) {
        note.modified = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (!note.dirty) {
            note.dirty = true;
            note.dirtySince = note.modified;
        }
    }

    /** Saves notes edited a moment ago; with {@code now} set, every unsaved note. */
    void flush(boolean now) {
        long time = System.currentTimeMillis();
        for (Note note : notes) {
            if (note.dirty && (now || time - note.dirtySince >= SAVE_DELAY_MS)) {
                note.dirty = false;
                save(note.id, note.edit.value());
            }
        }
    }

    /** Writes every unsaved note right here, for the exit hook when the io thread may be gone. */
    void saveNow() {
        for (Note note : new ArrayList<>(notes)) {
            if (note.dirty) {
                note.dirty = false;
                write(note.id, note.edit.value());
            }
        }
    }

    void delete(Note note) {
        notes.remove(note);
        String id = note.id;
        io.execute(() -> {
            try {
                Files.deleteIfExists(dir.resolve(id + ".txt"));
            } catch (IOException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
            }
        });
    }

    private void save(String id, String text) {
        io.execute(() -> write(id, text));
    }

    private synchronized void write(String id, String text) {
        try {
            Files.createDirectories(dir);
            Path target = dir.resolve(id + ".txt");
            Path temp = dir.resolve(id + ".txt.tmp");
            Files.write(temp, text.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            try {
                Files.move(temp, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING, StandardCopyOption.ATOMIC_MOVE);
            } catch (AtomicMoveNotSupportedException e) {
                Files.move(temp, target, StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            onError.accept(e);
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/NotesView.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;

/** The notes tab: a list on the left, the selected note's editor on the right. */
final class NotesView {
    private static final DateTimeFormatter WHEN = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd.MM HH:mm");

    private final Notes notes;
    private final Strings strings;
    private final ZoneId zone = ZoneId.systemDefault();
    private Notes.Note selected;
    private Notes.Note confirmDelete;
    private float listScroll;

    NotesView(Notes notes, Strings strings) {
        this.notes = notes;
        this.strings = strings;
    }

    void draw(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (selected == null && !notes.notes.isEmpty()) {
            selected = notes.notes.get(0);
        }
        float listW = Math.min(240, w * 0.32f);
        if (ui.button(x, y, listW, 34, strings.get("notes.new"), true)) {
            selected = notes.create();
            ui.focus = selected.edit;
        }

        float listY = y + 46;
        float listH = h - 46;
        ui.panel(x, listY, listW, listH, 10, Ui.BASE, Ui.LINE);
        float itemH = 50;
        listScroll -= ui.takeScroll(x, listY, listW, listH) * itemH;
        listScroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(listScroll, Math.max(0, notes.notes.size() * itemH + 8 - listH)));
        ui.clip(x + 1, listY + 1, listW - 2, listH - 2);
        if (notes.notes.isEmpty() && notes.loaded) {
            ui.text(ui.regular, 13, x + 14, listY + 14, Ui.FAINT, strings.get("notes.empty"));
        }
        boolean inList = ui.hover(x, listY, listW, listH);
        for (int i = 0; i < notes.notes.size(); i++) {
            Notes.Note note = notes.notes.get(i);
            float iy = listY + 4 + i * itemH - listScroll;
            if (iy + itemH < listY || iy > listY + listH) {
                continue;
            }
            boolean active = note == selected;
            boolean hot = inList && ui.hover(x + 4, iy, listW - 8, itemH - 4);
            if (active || hot) {
                ui.round(x + 4, iy, listW - 8, itemH - 4, 7, active ? 0x26a259f7 : Ui.RAISED);
            }
            if (active) {
                ui.round(x + 4, iy + 10, 3, itemH - 24, 1.5f, Ui.ACCENT);
            }
            String title = note.title();
            ui.textFit(ui.bold, 13, x + 16, iy + 7, listW - 30, title.isEmpty() ? Ui.FAINT : Ui.FG,
                    title.isEmpty() ? strings.get("notes.untitled") : title);
            ui.text(ui.regular, 11, x + 16, iy + 26, Ui.FAINT, WHEN.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(note.modified).atZone(zone)));
            if (hot && ui.clickedIn(x + 4, iy, listW - 8, itemH - 4)) {
                selected = note;
                confirmDelete = null;
            }
        }
        ui.unclip();

        float ex = x + listW + 14;
        float ew = w - listW - 14;
        if (selected == null) {
            String pick = strings.get("notes.pick");
            ui.text(ui.regular, 14, ex + (ew - ui.measure(ui.regular, 14, pick)) / 2, y + h / 2 - 10, Ui.FAINT, pick);
            return;
        }
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, ex + 2, y + 10, Ui.FAINT, strings.get("notes.saved"));
        String label = confirmDelete == selected ? strings.get("notes.confirm") : strings.get("notes.delete");
        float bw = ui.buttonWidth(label);
        boolean hotDelete = ui.hover(ex + ew - bw, y, bw, 34);
        ui.panel(ex + ew - bw, y, bw, 34, 7, hotDelete ? 0x40f87171 : Ui.RAISED, confirmDelete == selected ? Ui.DANGER : Ui.LINE);
        ui.text(ui.bold, 13, ex + ew - bw + 12, y + (34 - ui.lineHeight(ui.bold, 13)) / 2, Ui.DANGER, label);
        if (ui.clickedIn(ex + ew - bw, y, bw, 34)) {
            if (confirmDelete == selected) {
                notes.delete(selected);
                selected = notes.notes.isEmpty() ? null : notes.notes.get(0);
                confirmDelete = null;
                ui.focus = null;
                return;
            }
            confirmDelete = selected;
        }

        if (ui.field(selected.edit, ex, y + 46, ew, h - 46, true, strings.get("notes.placeholder"))) {
            notes.changed(selected);
            confirmDelete = null;
            // Keep the list newest first.
            if (notes.notes.indexOf(selected) > 0) {
                notes.notes.remove(selected);
                notes.notes.add(0, selected);
            }
        }
        notes.flush(false);
    }
}
```
