`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Options.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.util.Properties;

/**
 * Start-up options and the settings the player changes in the overlay.
 *
 * <p>Options come from the agent argument ({@code -javaagent:overlay.jar=dataDir=...,lang=ru}) and
 * can be overridden by system properties ({@code -Dk0t1k.overlay.dataDir=...}), which suit paths
 * with commas. Known options:
 * <ul>
 *   <li>{@code dataDir}: where history, notes and settings live; default {@code <gameDir>/k0t1k-overlay}
 *   <li>{@code gameDir}: the game folder; default the working directory, which launchers set to it
 *   <li>{@code lang}: {@code en} (default) or {@code ru}
 *   <li>{@code crashDialog}: {@code false} turns off the overlay's own crash window
 * </ul>
 */
final class Options {
    final Path gameDir;
    final Path dataDir;
    final Strings strings;
    /** The player's name, for highlighting mentions; empty when unknown. */
    final String username;

    // Saved settings.
    boolean hud;
    /** 0 top left, 1 top right, 2 bottom left, 3 bottom right. */
    int hudCorner;
    /** Whether the cat pops up to react to deaths, advancements, AFK and timers. */
    boolean cat = true;
    /** Whether the last seconds of the game are kept for Shift+F2 (OpenGL only). */
    boolean replay = true;
    /** "video" (MP4 with ffmpeg, otherwise AVI) or "gif". */
    String replayFormat = "video";
    /** Whether the overlay shows its own crash window; a launcher with its own can turn it off. */
    final boolean crashDialog;
    final String lang;

    private final Path settingsFile;

    Options(String agentArgs) {
        Properties args = new Properties();
        for (String pair : agentArgs.split(",")) {
            int eq = pair.indexOf('=');
            if (eq > 0) {
                args.setProperty(pair.substring(0, eq).trim(), pair.substring(eq + 1).trim());
            }
        }
        gameDir = Paths.get(option(args, "gameDir", System.getProperty("user.dir"))).toAbsolutePath();
        dataDir = Paths.get(option(args, "dataDir", gameDir.resolve("k0t1k-overlay").toString())).toAbsolutePath();
        lang = option(args, "lang", "en");
        strings = new Strings(lang);
        crashDialog = !"false".equalsIgnoreCase(option(args, "crashDialog", "true"));
        username = usernameFromCommandLine();
        settingsFile = dataDir.resolve("overlay.properties");
    }

    private static String option(Properties args, String key, String fallback) {
        String value = System.getProperty("k0t1k.overlay." + key);
        if (value == null || value.isEmpty()) {
            value = args.getProperty(key);
        }
        return value == null || value.isEmpty() ? fallback : value;
    }

    /** {@code --username} from the game's arguments; player names never contain spaces. */
    private static String usernameFromCommandLine() {
        String command = System.getProperty("sun.java.command", "");
        String[] parts = command.split(" ");
        for (int i = 0; i < parts.length - 1; i++) {
            if ("--username".equals(parts[i])) {
                return parts[i + 1];
            }
        }
        return "";
    }

    void loadSettings() throws IOException {
        Properties saved = new Properties();
        if (Files.exists(settingsFile)) {
            try (InputStream in = Files.newInputStream(settingsFile)) {
                saved.load(in);
            }
        }
        hud = Boolean.parseBoolean(saved.getProperty("hud", "false"));
        cat = Boolean.parseBoolean(saved.getProperty("cat", "true"));
        replay = Boolean.parseBoolean(saved.getProperty("replay", "true"));
        replayFormat = "gif".equals(saved.getProperty("replayFormat")) ? "gif" : "video";
        try {
            hudCorner = Math.max(0, Math.min(3, Integer.parseInt(saved.getProperty("hudCorner", "0"))));
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            hudCorner = 0;
        }
    }

    /** Runs on the io thread; takes a snapshot so the render thread can keep changing the fields. */
    Runnable saveTask() {
        Properties snapshot = new Properties();
        snapshot.setProperty("hud", Boolean.toString(hud));
        snapshot.setProperty("hudCorner", Integer.toString(hudCorner));
        snapshot.setProperty("cat", Boolean.toString(cat));
        snapshot.setProperty("replay", Boolean.toString(replay));
        snapshot.setProperty("replayFormat", replayFormat);
        return () -> {
            try {
                Files.createDirectories(dataDir);
                try (OutputStream out = Files.newOutputStream(settingsFile)) {
                    snapshot.store(out, "k0t1k overlay");
                }
            } catch (IOException e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("could not save " + settingsFile, e);
            }
        };
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Overlay.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentLinkedQueue;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.ScheduledExecutorService;
import java.util.function.Function;

import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL;
import org.lwjgl.opengl.GL11;

/**
 * The overlay: opened with Shift+Tab over the running game, like the Steam overlay. It owns the
 * render-thread state and is driven by the agent's hooks through {@link #apply}: a frame hook of the
 * window library or of Vulkan builds a {@link Batch} once per frame and a renderer for the game's
 * graphics API draws it; the window library's input reaches the key, button and cursor methods.
 */
public final class Overlay implements Function<Object[], Object> {
    private static final DateTimeFormatter CLOCK = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm");
    private static final String[] TABS = {"tab.chat", "tab.browser", "tab.notes", "tab.tools", "tab.mods", "tab.profile", "tab.perf"};
    /** No input for this long while playing counts as AFK, and the cat says so. */
    private static final long AFK = 5 * 60_000;
    /** How often the lifetime stats are written. */
    private static final long STATS_SAVE = 60_000;

    private final Options options;
    private final Strings strings;
    private final GlfwPlatform glfw = new GlfwPlatform(this);
    /** The window library in use, known from the first hook it receives. */
    private Platform platform = glfw;
    private final Perf perf = new Perf();
    private final long started = System.currentTimeMillis();

    /** Work posted to the render thread, run at the start of the next frame. */
    private final ConcurrentLinkedQueue<Runnable> renderQueue = new ConcurrentLinkedQueue<>();
    private final Executor render = renderQueue::add;
    private final ScheduledExecutorService ioThread = Executors.newSingleThreadScheduledExecutor(daemon("io"));
    private final ExecutorService searchThread = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(daemon("search"));
    /** {@link #ioThread} with failures reported instead of dropped inside a future. */
    private final Executor io = task -> ioThread.execute(() -> {
        try {
            task.run();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            report(e);
        }
    });

    private final ChatHistory chat;
    private final Notes notes;
    private final ChatView chatView;
    private final NotesView notesView;
    private final PerfView perfView;
    private final GameState game = new GameState();
    private final Stats stats;
    private final Timers timers;
    private final Screenshots screenshots;
    private final Mods mods;
    private final ToolsView toolsView;
    private final ModsView modsView;
    private final ProfileView profileView;
    private Cat cat;
    private final Media media;
    private final Replay replay;
    private GlCapture capture;
    /** Set when reading frames back failed; the replay buffer then stays off for this game. */
    private boolean captureBroken;
    private final CrashGuard crashGuard;
    private final Browser browser;
    private final BrowserView browserView;
    private long lastInput = System.currentTimeMillis();
    private boolean afk;
    private long nextStatsSave = System.currentTimeMillis() + STATS_SAVE;
    /** Health in the last frame, to notice the moment the player dies. */
    private float lastHealth = -1;
    /** The instance's name: the folder holding the game folder (instances/NAME/minecraft) or the game folder. */
    private final String instance;

    private final Batch batch = new Batch();
    private Font.Atlas atlas;
    private Ui ui;
    private GlRenderer gl;
    private VulkanBackend vk;
    private SdlBackend sdl;
    private LegacyBackend legacy;
    private boolean broken;
    private volatile boolean open;
    /**
     * The thread that draws frames, where the game also reads input and changes the cursor. Mods like
     * Ixeris poll the window on another thread and replay the render thread's window calls there.
     */
    private volatile Thread renderThread;
    private int tab;
    private final int[] sizes = new int[4];
    /** Framebuffer pixels per window coordinate, from the last frame; converts cursor positions. */
    private float pixelRatio = 1;
    /** Keys and buttons whose press reached the game, so only their releases are passed on while open. */
    private final java.util.Set<Integer> gameKeys = new java.util.HashSet<>();
    private final java.util.Set<Integer> gameButtons = new java.util.HashSet<>();

    private static final class Toast {
        final String title;
        final String body;
        final long until;

        Toast(String title, String body, long until) {
            this.title = title;
            this.body = body;
            this.until = until;
        }
    }

    private final List<Toast> toasts = new ArrayList<>();

    public Overlay(String args) {
        options = new Options(args);
        strings = options.strings;
        try {
            options.loadSettings();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            report(e);
        }
        chat = new ChatHistory(options.gameDir, options.dataDir, ioThread, searchThread, render, this::report);
        notes = new Notes(options.dataDir, io, render, this::report);
        Runnable saveSettings = () -> io.execute(options.saveTask());
        chatView = new ChatView(chat, strings, this);
        notesView = new NotesView(notes, strings);
        perfView = new PerfView(perf, options, saveSettings);
        Path gameDir = options.gameDir;
        Path parent = gameDir.getParent();
        instance = "minecraft".equals(String.valueOf(gameDir.getFileName())) && parent != null
                ? String.valueOf(parent.getFileName()) : String.valueOf(gameDir.getFileName());
        stats = new Stats(options.dataDir, options.gameDir);
        stats.onUnlock = this::achievement;
        try {
            stats.load();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            report(e);
        }
        timers = new Timers(options.dataDir, io, this::report);
        timers.onFire = this::timerFired;
        timers.load();
        screenshots = new Screenshots(options.gameDir, options.dataDir, ioThread, render, this::report);
        screenshots.onNew = this::screenshot;
        mods = new Mods(options.gameDir, io, render, this::report);
        toolsView = new ToolsView(strings, this, game, timers, screenshots);
        modsView = new ModsView(mods, strings, this);
        profileView = new ProfileView(stats, options, saveSettings);
        media = new Media(ioThread, render, this::report);
        media.onTrack = track -> {
            if (!open) {
                toast(strings.get("media.now"), track);
            }
        };
        replay = new Replay(options.gameDir, this::report);
        browser = new Browser(options.dataDir, io, options.lang);
        browserView = new BrowserView(browser, strings, this);
        crashGuard = new CrashGuard(options.dataDir);
        crashGuard.install();
        chat.onNew = this::onChat;
        chat.start();
        notes.load();
        screenshots.start();
        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(new Thread(this::saveOnExit, "k0t1k-overlay-exit"));
        toast(strings.get("welcome"), strings.get("welcome.body"));
        log("started, data in " + options.dataDir);
    }

    /**
     * Every hooked LWJGL call: {@code {class, method, arguments}} before it runs, with the return
     * value appended after it. Answers as {@code Agent.Enter} and {@code Agent.Exit} expect: null to
     * change nothing, new arguments, {@code Boolean.TRUE} to skip the call, or a new return value.
     */
    @Override
    public Object apply(Object[] call) {
        String method = (String) call[1];
        Object[] args = (Object[]) call[2];
        boolean exit = call.length == 4;
        if (broken) {
            // Even disabled, the Vulkan objects made on the game's swapchains and device must go with them.
            if (vk != null && ("vkDestroySwapchainKHR".equals(method) || "vkDestroyDevice".equals(method))) {
                try {
                    vk.call(method, args, null, false);
                } catch (Throwable e) {
                    report(e);
                }
            }
            return null;
        }
        try {
            switch (method) {
                case "glfwSwapBuffers":
                    glfw.window = (Long) args[0];
                    platform = glfw;
                    glFrame();
                    return null;
                case "glfwSetInputMode":
                    return glfw.onInputMode((Integer) args[1], (Integer) args[2]) ? Boolean.TRUE : null;
                case "glfwGetKey":
                case "glfwGetMouseButton":
                    // Keys and buttons read as released by the game while the overlay is open.
                    return isOpen() && onRenderThread() ? Integer.valueOf(0) : null;
                case "glfwSetKeyCallback":
                case "glfwSetCharCallback":
                case "glfwSetCharModsCallback":
                case "glfwSetMouseButtonCallback":
                case "glfwSetCursorPosCallback":
                case "glfwSetScrollCallback": {
                    glfw.window = (Long) args[0];
                    platform = glfw;
                    Object wrapped = args[1] == null ? null : glfw.wrap(method, args[1]);
                    if (wrapped == args[1]) {
                        return null;
                    }
                    Object[] replaced = args.clone();
                    replaced[1] = wrapped;
                    return replaced;
                }
                case "SDL_GL_SwapWindow":
                case "SDL_PollEvent":
                case "SDL_SetWindowRelativeMouseMode":
                case "SDL_WarpMouseInWindow":
                case "SDL_StartTextInput":
                case "SDL_StopTextInput":
                    platform = sdl();
                    return sdl.call(method, args, exit ? call[3] : null, exit);
                // LWJGL 2 (Minecraft 1.12.2 and older); the class tells Keyboard.next from Mouse.next
                case "update":
                case "setGrabbed":
                case "next":
                case "isKeyDown":
                case "isButtonDown":
                case "getDX":
                case "getDY":
                case "getDWheel":
                    platform = legacy();
                    return legacy.call((Class<?>) call[0], method, args, exit ? call[3] : null, exit);
                case "vkCreateSwapchainKHR":
                case "vkDestroySwapchainKHR":
                case "vkGetDeviceQueue":
                case "vkGetDeviceQueue2":
                case "vkQueuePresentKHR":
                case "vkDestroyDevice":
                    return vulkan().call(method, args, exit ? call[3] : null, exit);
                default:
                    return null;
            }
        } catch (Throwable e) {
            // Errors too (e.g. LWJGL's "Out of stack space"): the advice would drop them silently.
            fail(e);
            return null;
        }
    }

    private SdlBackend sdl() throws ReflectiveOperationException {
        if (sdl == null) {
            sdl = (SdlBackend) Class.forName("dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl.SdlPlatform", true, Overlay.class.getClassLoader())
                    .getDeclaredConstructor(Overlay.class)
                    .newInstance(this);
        }
        return sdl;
    }

    private LegacyBackend legacy() throws ReflectiveOperationException {
        if (legacy == null) {
            legacy = (LegacyBackend) Class.forName("dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl.LegacyPlatform", true, Overlay.class.getClassLoader())
                    .getDeclaredConstructor(Overlay.class)
                    .newInstance(this);
        }
        return legacy;
    }

    private VulkanBackend vulkan() throws ReflectiveOperationException {
        if (vk == null) {
            vk = (VulkanBackend) Class.forName("dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl.VkRenderer", true, Overlay.class.getClassLoader())
                    .getDeclaredConstructor(Overlay.class)
                    .newInstance(this);
        }
        return vk;
    }

    private static java.util.concurrent.ThreadFactory daemon(String name) {
        return runnable -> {
            Thread thread = new Thread(runnable, "k0t1k-overlay-" + name);
            thread.setDaemon(true);
            thread.setPriority(Thread.MIN_PRIORITY);
            return thread;
        };
    }

    static void log(String message) {
        System.out.println("[k0t1k-overlay] " + message);
    }

    /** Reports a recoverable failure (disk, parsing) without disabling the overlay. */
    void report(Throwable error) {
        System.err.println("[k0t1k-overlay] " + error);
        error.printStackTrace();
    }

    /**
     * Last chance on JVM exit: the io thread is a daemon and may never run queued work, so the newest
     * chat lines and unsaved notes are written from here.
     */
    private void saveOnExit() {
        chat.pollNow();
        notes.saveNow();
        media.stop();
        replay.stop();
        Path report = stats.saveOnExit();
        if (report != null && options.crashDialog) {
            try {
                crashGuard.onCrash(report, options.lang, sessionSummary());
            } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
                report(e);
            }
        }
    }

    /** What the overlay knows about this session, for the crash window's support bundle. */
    private String sessionSummary() {
        long minutes = (System.currentTimeMillis() - started) / 60000;
        return "Instance: " + instance + "\n"
                + "Session: " + minutes + " min\n"
                + "Renderer: " + perfView.renderer + "\n"
                + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "FPS: %d, frame %.1f ms, 1%% low %.1f ms%n", perf.fps, perf.average, perf.worst)
                + "Heap: " + PerfView.mb(perf.heapUsed) + " / " + PerfView.mb(perf.heapMax) + "\n"
                + "Mods: " + (mods.scanned ? Integer.toString(mods.mods.size()) : "not read") + "\n"
                + "Position: " + (game.inWorld ? game.dimension + " " + game.blockPosition() : "not in a world") + "\n";
    }

    /** Saves the replay buffer (Shift+F2 or the tools tab). */
    void saveReplay() {
        if (!replayAvailable()) {
            toast(strings.get("replay.title"), strings.get(options.replay ? "replay.glOnly" : "replay.off"));
            return;
        }
        if (replay.saving()) {
            return;
        }
        toast(strings.get("replay.title"), strings.get("replay.saving"));
        replay.save(options.replayFormat, file -> render.execute(() -> {
            if (file == null) {
                toast(strings.get("replay.title"), strings.get("replay.failed"));
                return;
            }
            toast(strings.get("replay.saved"), file.getFileName() + "\n" + file.getParent());
            catSays("cat.replay");
        }));
    }

    /** Whether frames are being recorded: OpenGL, the setting on, nothing broken. */
    boolean replayAvailable() {
        return options.replay && capture != null && !captureBroken;
    }

    Replay replay() {
        return replay;
    }

    Options options() {
        return options;
    }

    void saveSettings() {
        io.execute(options.saveTask());
    }

    Media media() {
        return media;
    }

    /** Whether the renderer can draw pictures (the browser's page): only the OpenGL 3.2 one so far. */
    boolean imagesSupported() {
        return gl != null;
    }

    /** Disables the overlay after a failure in drawing or input, leaving the game untouched. */
    void fail(Throwable error) {
        if (broken) {
            return;
        }
        broken = true;
        System.err.println("[k0t1k-overlay] disabled after an error:");
        error.printStackTrace();
        renderQueue.clear();
        notes.flush(true);
        if (open) {
            open = false;
            try {
                platform.restoreCursor();
            } catch (RuntimeException | LinkageError e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
        if (vk != null) {
            vk.disable();
        }
    }

    boolean isOpen() {
        return open && !broken;
    }

    /**
     * Whether the current call comes from the render thread. Calls from other threads are replays of
     * render thread calls the overlay already saw (Ixeris), so they pass untouched.
     */
    boolean onRenderThread() {
        return Thread.currentThread() == renderThread;
    }

    void toast(String title, String body) {
        toasts.add(new Toast(title, body, System.currentTimeMillis() + 4500));
        if (toasts.size() > 4) {
            toasts.remove(0);
        }
    }

    String username() {
        return options.username;
    }

    /** Every new chat line: mention toasts, and the player's advancements for the stats and the cat. */
    private void onChat(ChatHistory.Line line) {
        String name = options.username;
        if (name.isEmpty()) {
            return;
        }
        if (isAdvancement(line.text, name)) {
            stats.add("advancements", 1);
            catSays("cat.advancement1", "cat.advancement2", "cat.advancement3");
            return;
        }
        if (!open && mentions(line.text, name)) {
            toast(strings.get("chat.mention"), line.text);
        }
    }

    /** "Name has made the advancement [...]" and its challenge and goal variants, in English and Russian. */
    private static boolean isAdvancement(String text, String name) {
        String lower = text.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        String me = name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        if (!lower.startsWith(me + " ")) {
            return false;
        }
        String[] markers = {"has made the advancement", "has completed the challenge", "has reached the goal",
            "получил достижение", "получила достижение", "выполнил испытание", "выполнила испытание",
            "достиг цели", "достигла цели"};
        for (String marker : markers) {
            if (lower.contains(marker)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** Whether a chat line names the player as a whole word, not counting the player's own messages. */
    static boolean mentions(String line, String name) {
        if (name.isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }
        String text = line.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        String lower = name.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        // Ignore the player's own messages ("<Name> ...") and lines about the player rather than to them
        // ("Name was slain by...", "Name joined the game").
        if (text.startsWith("<" + lower + ">") || text.startsWith(lower + " ")) {
            return false;
        }
        int at = text.indexOf(lower);
        while (at >= 0) {
            boolean startOk = at == 0 || !Character.isLetterOrDigit(text.charAt(at - 1)) && text.charAt(at - 1) != '_';
            int end = at + lower.length();
            boolean endOk = end == text.length() || !Character.isLetterOrDigit(text.charAt(end)) && text.charAt(end) != '_';
            if (startOk && endOk) {
                return true;
            }
            at = text.indexOf(lower, at + 1);
        }
        return false;
    }

    private void catSays(String... keys) {
        if (cat == null || !options.cat) {
            return;
        }
        String[] phrases = new String[keys.length];
        for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
            phrases[i] = strings.get(keys[i]);
        }
        cat.say(phrases);
    }

    private void achievement(String id) {
        toast(strings.get("ach.unlocked"), strings.get("ach." + id));
        catSays("cat.achievement");
    }

    private void timerFired(Timers.Timer timer) {
        stats.add("timers", 1);
        toasts.add(new Toast(strings.get("tools.timerDone"), timer.label, System.currentTimeMillis() + 12000));
        if (cat != null && options.cat) {
            cat.say(timer.label);
        }
    }

    private void screenshot(String name) {
        stats.add("screenshots", 1);
        game.update();
        String where = game.available && game.inWorld ? game.blockPosition() : "";
        screenshots.caption(name, instance, game.inWorld ? game.dimension : "", where);
        String body = instance + (where.isEmpty() ? "" : " · " + where) + "\n" + name;
        toast(strings.get("shot.saved"), body);
        catSays("cat.shot1", "cat.shot2");
    }

    /** Once per frame, overlay open or not: game state, timers, stats, deaths and AFK. */
    private void tickWorld(long now) {
        game.update();
        timers.tick(now);
        stats.tick(now);
        if (game.inWorld) {
            if (lastHealth > 0 && game.health <= 0) {
                stats.add("deaths", 1);
                catSays("cat.death1", "cat.death2", "cat.death3");
            }
            lastHealth = game.health;
        } else {
            lastHealth = -1;
        }
        if (!afk && !open && game.inWorld && now - lastInput > AFK) {
            afk = true;
            catSays("cat.afk1", "cat.afk2");
        }
        if (now >= nextStatsSave) {
            nextStatsSave = now + STATS_SAVE;
            io.execute(stats.saveTask());
        }
    }

    private void input() {
        lastInput = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (afk) {
            afk = false;
            catSays("cat.back");
        }
    }

    // ---- input, from the wrapped GLFW callbacks; each returns true when the game must not see it ----

    boolean key(int key, int action, int mods) {
        if (broken) {
            return false;
        }
        ensureUi();
        input();
        boolean press = action != Keys.RELEASE;
        if (!press && !gameKeys.remove(key) && open) {
            // The game never saw this press (the overlay took it), so it must not see the release.
            return true;
        }
        if (press && key == Keys.TAB && (mods & Keys.MOD_SHIFT) != 0
                && (mods & (Keys.MOD_CONTROL | Keys.MOD_ALT | Keys.MOD_SUPER)) == 0) {
            if (action == Keys.PRESS) {
                setOpen(!open);
            }
            return true;
        }
        if (press && key == Keys.F2 && (mods & Keys.MOD_SHIFT) != 0) {
            // Shift+F2 saves the replay; the game would take a screenshot on F2 alone.
            if (action == Keys.PRESS) {
                saveReplay();
            }
            return true;
        }
        if (!open) {
            if (press) {
                gameKeys.add(key);
            }
            return false;
        }
        if (!press) {
            // Released after the overlay opened: the game saw the press, so it gets the release too
            // and the key does not stay down.
            return false;
        }
        if (key == Keys.ESCAPE) {
            setOpen(false);
        } else {
            ui.onKey(key, mods);
        }
        return true;
    }

    boolean character(int codepoint) {
        if (!isOpen()) {
            return false;
        }
        ui.onChar(codepoint);
        return true;
    }

    boolean button(int button, int action) {
        input();
        boolean press = action != Keys.RELEASE;
        if (!isOpen()) {
            if (press) {
                gameButtons.add(button);
            } else {
                gameButtons.remove(button);
            }
            return false;
        }
        ui.onButton(button, press);
        // Same rule as for keys: a release passes only when the game saw the press.
        return press || !gameButtons.remove(button);
    }

    boolean cursor(double x, double y) {
        input();
        if (!isOpen()) {
            return false;
        }
        ui.onMove((float) (x * pixelRatio / ui.scale), (float) (y * pixelRatio / ui.scale));
        return true;
    }

    boolean scroll(double dy) {
        if (!isOpen()) {
            return false;
        }
        ui.onScroll(dy);
        return true;
    }

    private void setOpen(boolean value) {
        if (open == value) {
            return;
        }
        ui.resetInput();
        if (value) {
            media.start();
            open = true;
            platform.showCursor();
            platform.sizes(sizes);
            ui.onMove(sizes[2] / 2f * pixelRatio / ui.scale, sizes[3] / 2f * pixelRatio / ui.scale);
            toasts.clear();
        } else {
            open = false;
            notes.flush(true);
            platform.restoreCursor();
        }
    }

    // ---- frame ----

    /** OpenGL: draws right before the window library presents the frame. */
    void glFrame() {
        if (!platform.hasGlContext()) {
            buildFrame(0, 0); // no OpenGL here (e.g. Vulkan); keep the clock and queues going
            return;
        }
        platform.sizes(sizes);
        captureFrame(sizes[0], sizes[1]);
        if (!buildFrame(sizes[0], sizes[1])) {
            return;
        }
        if (gl == null) {
            // Throws without a context made current by the game; caught by apply.
            GL.getCapabilities();
            gl = new GlRenderer();
            perfView.renderer = GL11.glGetString(GL11.GL_RENDERER) + " · OpenGL " + GL11.glGetString(GL11.GL_VERSION);
        }
        gl.draw(batch);
    }

    /** Reads the game's frame (before the overlay draws on it) into the replay buffer. */
    private void captureFrame(int width, int height) {
        if (!options.replay || captureBroken || width <= 0 || height <= 0) {
            return;
        }
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        if (capture == null) {
            capture = new GlCapture();
        }
        if (!replay.due(now)) {
            return;
        }
        int w = Math.min(width, Replay.MAX_WIDTH) & ~1;
        int h = Math.max(2, Math.round(height * (w / (float) width))) & ~1;
        try {
            capture.capture(width, height, w, h, now, replay::add);
        } catch (RuntimeException | LinkageError e) {
            // The replay buffer is optional: turn it off, keep the overlay.
            captureBroken = true;
            report(e);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Runs once per frame on the render thread, whatever the graphics API: updates the frame clock,
     * runs posted work and, when anything is on screen, fills {@link #batch} for a target of
     * {@code width} x {@code height} pixels. Returns whether there is something to draw.
     */
    boolean buildFrame(int width, int height) {
        renderThread = Thread.currentThread();
        perf.frame();
        for (Runnable task; (task = renderQueue.poll()) != null; ) {
            task.run();
        }
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        tickWorld(now);
        for (Iterator<Toast> it = toasts.iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) {
            if (it.next().until < now) {
                it.remove();
            }
        }
        if (!open && !options.hud && toasts.isEmpty() && (cat == null || !cat.talking(now))) {
            return false; // nothing on screen, so the game's frame stays untouched
        }
        if (!platform.hasWindow()) {
            return false; // no window seen yet
        }
        platform.sizes(sizes);
        if (width <= 0 || height <= 0 || sizes[2] <= 0) {
            return false; // minimised, or no target
        }
        ensureUi();
        ui.platform = platform;
        pixelRatio = width / (float) sizes[2];
        // HiDPI: the larger of the OS scale and the framebuffer density, shrunk if the UI would not fit.
        float scale = Math.max(platform.contentScale(), pixelRatio);
        scale = Math.min(scale, Math.min(width / 860f, height / 480f));
        ui.scale = Math.max(0.75f, scale);
        if (atlas.full) {
            atlas.reset();
        }
        batch.begin(width, height);
        try {
            draw(width / ui.scale, height / ui.scale);
        } finally {
            batch.end();
            ui.endFrame();
        }
        return !batch.empty();
    }

    Batch batch() {
        return batch;
    }

    void setRendererName(String name) {
        perfView.renderer = name;
    }

    private void ensureUi() {
        if (ui != null) {
            return;
        }
        atlas = new Font.Atlas(batch);
        try {
            // LWJGL 2 ships no stb_truetype, so its games rasterise with java.awt.
            boolean awt = platform instanceof LegacyBackend;
            ui = new Ui(batch, font("Inter-Regular.ttf", awt), font("Inter-SemiBold.ttf", awt), platform);
            cat = new Cat();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(e);
        }
    }

    private Font font(String resource, boolean awt) throws IOException {
        return awt ? new AwtFont(resource, atlas) : new StbFont(resource, atlas);
    }

    private void draw(float w, float h) {
        if (open) {
            drawOverlay(w, h);
        } else if (options.hud) {
            perfView.drawHud(ui, w, h);
        }
        if (open) {
            // Sits in the strip under the content card, next to the close hint.
            cat.draw(ui, 16, h - 3, 0.75f, true, System.currentTimeMillis());
        } else if (options.cat) {
            cat.draw(ui, 16, h - 10, 1.5f, false, System.currentTimeMillis());
        }
        drawToasts(w, h);
    }

    private void drawOverlay(float w, float h) {
        ui.rect(0, 0, w, h, 0xc0060509);

        // Top bar: brand, tabs, clock.
        float barH = 48;
        ui.rect(0, 0, w, barH, 0xf013101c);
        ui.rect(0, barH, w, 1, Ui.LINE);
        float bx = 18;
        bx += ui.text(ui.bold, 16, bx, (barH - ui.lineHeight(ui.bold, 16)) / 2, Ui.ACCENT, "_k0t1k");
        ui.text(ui.regular, 13, bx + 6, (barH - ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 13)) / 2 + 1, Ui.FAINT, "overlay");

        float tabsW = 0;
        float[] widths = new float[TABS.length];
        // Narrow windows get tighter tabs so they stay clear of the brand and the clock.
        float tabPad = w < 1000 ? 18 : 32;
        for (int i = 0; i < TABS.length; i++) {
            widths[i] = ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, strings.get(TABS[i])) + tabPad;
            tabsW += widths[i] + 6;
        }
        float tx = (w - tabsW) / 2;
        for (int i = 0; i < TABS.length; i++) {
            if (ui.pill(tx, 9, widths[i], 30, strings.get(TABS[i]), tab == i)) {
                tab = i;
                ui.focus = null;
            }
            tx += widths[i] + 6;
        }

        String clock = LocalTime.now().format(CLOCK);
        float cw = ui.measure(ui.bold, 16, clock);
        ui.text(ui.bold, 16, w - 18 - cw, 7, Ui.FG, clock);
        long minutes = (System.currentTimeMillis() - started) / 60000;
        String session = strings.format("session", String.format("%d:%02d", minutes / 60, minutes % 60));
        float sw = ui.measure(ui.regular, 11, session);
        ui.text(ui.regular, 11, w - 18 - sw, 27, Ui.FAINT, session);

        // Content card.
        float cardW = Math.min(w - 32, 1120);
        float cx = (w - cardW) / 2;
        float cy = barH + 16;
        float cardH = h - cy - 34;
        ui.panel(cx, cy, cardW, cardH, 14, 0xf513101c, Ui.LINE);
        float pad = 18;
        float x = cx + pad;
        float y = cy + pad;
        float innerW = cardW - pad * 2;
        float innerH = cardH - pad * 2;
        switch (tab) {
            case 0:
                chatView.draw(ui, x, y, innerW, innerH);
                break;
            case 1:
                browserView.draw(ui, x, y, innerW, innerH);
                break;
            case 2:
                notesView.draw(ui, x, y, innerW, innerH);
                break;
            case 3:
                toolsView.draw(ui, x, y, innerW, innerH);
                break;
            case 4:
                modsView.draw(ui, x, y, innerW, innerH);
                break;
            case 5:
                profileView.draw(ui, x, y, innerW, innerH);
                break;
            default:
                perfView.draw(ui, x, y, innerW, innerH);
                break;
        }

        String hint = strings.get("hint.close");
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, (w - ui.measure(ui.regular, 12, hint)) / 2, h - 24, Ui.FAINT, hint);
        drawMedia(w, h);
    }

    /** What plays on the computer, with previous, play/pause and next, in the strip under the card. */
    private void drawMedia(float w, float h) {
        if (!media.active) {
            return;
        }
        float size = 24;
        float y = h - 30;
        float x = w - 18 - size * 3 - 8;
        String[] icons = {"«", media.playing ? "‖" : "▶", "»"};
        String[] commands = {"prev", "play", "next"};
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            float bx = x + i * (size + 4);
            boolean hot = ui.hover(bx, y, size, size);
            ui.round(bx, y, size, size, size / 2, hot ? Ui.HOVER : Ui.RAISED);
            float tw = ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, icons[i]);
            ui.text(ui.bold, 13, bx + (size - tw) / 2, y + (size - ui.lineHeight(ui.bold, 13)) / 2, Ui.FG, icons[i]);
            if (hot && ui.clickedIn(bx, y, size, size)) {
                media.command(commands[i]);
            }
        }
        String label = "♪ " + media.label();
        float labelW = Math.min(ui.measure(ui.regular, 12, label), Math.min(320, w * 0.3f));
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, x - 10 - labelW, y + (size - ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 12)) / 2, labelW,
                media.playing ? Ui.MUTED : Ui.FAINT, label);
    }

    private void drawToasts(float w, float h) {
        float y = h - 16;
        for (int i = toasts.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            Toast toast = toasts.get(i);
            float tw = 300;
            List<int[]> lines = ui.wrap(ui.regular, 13, toast.body, tw - 28);
            int shown = Math.min(lines.size(), 3);
            float th = 38 + shown * ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 13);
            y -= th;
            float x = w - tw - 16;
            ui.panel(x, y, tw, th, 10, 0xf21b1728, 0xff3a3256);
            ui.round(x + 1, y + 10, 3, th - 20, 1.5f, Ui.ACCENT);
            ui.text(ui.bold, 13, x + 14, y + 10, Ui.FG, toast.title);
            for (int l = 0; l < shown; l++) {
                int[] span = lines.get(l);
                ui.text(ui.regular, 13, x + 14, y + 30 + l * ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 13), Ui.MUTED,
                        toast.body, span[0], span[1]);
            }
            y -= 10;
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Perf.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.lang.management.GarbageCollectorMXBean;
import java.lang.management.ManagementFactory;
import java.lang.management.MemoryUsage;
import java.lang.management.OperatingSystemMXBean;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Frame and JVM metrics. Frame times come from the interval between two buffer swaps, so they are
 * what the player sees, including vsync and frame limiter waits.
 */
final class Perf {
    static final int HISTORY = 600;

    /** Frame times in milliseconds, a ring buffer; {@link #head} is the next slot. */
    final float[] frames = new float[HISTORY];
    int head;
    int count;
    private long lastSwap;

    // Refreshed twice a second.
    int fps;
    float average;
    /** The frame time 99% of recent frames stay under ("1% low" as a frame time). */
    float worst;
    long heapUsed;
    long heapMax;
    long heapCommitted;
    long gcCount;
    long gcMillis;
    /** GC time spent in the last refresh interval. */
    long gcRecentMillis;
    double cpu = -1;
    int threads;
    private long lastRefresh;
    private int framesSinceRefresh;
    private long lastGcMillis = -1;

    /** Cleared when the runtime lacks {@code com.sun.management} (no jdk.management module). */
    private boolean cpuAvailable = true;
    private final List<GarbageCollectorMXBean> collectors = ManagementFactory.getGarbageCollectorMXBeans();
    private final OperatingSystemMXBean os = ManagementFactory.getOperatingSystemMXBean();
    private final float[] sorted = new float[HISTORY];

    void frame() {
        long now = System.nanoTime();
        if (lastSwap != 0) {
            frames[head] = (now - lastSwap) / 1e6f;
            head = (head + 1) % HISTORY;
            count = Math.min(count + 1, HISTORY);
        }
        lastSwap = now;
        framesSinceRefresh++;
        if (now - lastRefresh >= 500_000_000L) {
            refresh(now);
        }
    }

    /** The frame time {@code back} frames ago (0 = the last one). */
    float frame(int back) {
        return frames[((head - 1 - back) % HISTORY + HISTORY) % HISTORY];
    }

    private void refresh(long now) {
        if (lastRefresh != 0) {
            fps = Math.round(framesSinceRefresh * 1e9f / (now - lastRefresh));
        }
        lastRefresh = now;
        framesSinceRefresh = 0;

        int n = Math.min(count, 240);
        if (n > 0) {
            float sum = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                sorted[i] = frame(i);
                sum += sorted[i];
            }
            average = sum / n;
            Arrays.sort(sorted, 0, n);
            worst = sorted[Math.min(n - 1, (int) Math.floor(n * 0.99))];
        }

        MemoryUsage heap = ManagementFactory.getMemoryMXBean().getHeapMemoryUsage();
        heapUsed = heap.getUsed();
        heapCommitted = heap.getCommitted();
        heapMax = heap.getMax() > 0 ? heap.getMax() : heap.getCommitted();

        long count = 0;
        long millis = 0;
        for (GarbageCollectorMXBean gc : collectors) {
            count += Math.max(0, gc.getCollectionCount());
            millis += Math.max(0, gc.getCollectionTime());
        }
        gcCount = count;
        gcMillis = millis;
        gcRecentMillis = lastGcMillis < 0 ? 0 : millis - lastGcMillis;
        lastGcMillis = millis;

        if (cpuAvailable) {
            try {
                if (os instanceof com.sun.management.OperatingSystemMXBean) {
                    cpu = ((com.sun.management.OperatingSystemMXBean) os).getProcessCpuLoad();
                }
            } catch (LinkageError e) {
                cpuAvailable = false;
                cpu = -1;
            }
        }
        threads = ManagementFactory.getThreadMXBean().getThreadCount();
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/PerfView.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

/** The performance tab and the small HUD shown while playing. */
final class PerfView {
    private final Perf perf;
    private final Options options;
    private final Strings strings;
    private final Runnable saveSettings;
    /** GL renderer and version, read once by the overlay on the render thread. */
    String renderer = "";
    private final String system;

    PerfView(Perf perf, Options options, Runnable saveSettings) {
        this.perf = perf;
        this.options = options;
        this.strings = options.strings;
        this.saveSettings = saveSettings;
        system = "Java " + System.getProperty("java.version") + " · " + System.getProperty("os.name") + " "
                + System.getProperty("os.arch") + " · " + Runtime.getRuntime().availableProcessors() + " CPU";
    }

    static int frameColor(float ms) {
        return ms <= 20 ? Ui.OK : ms <= 40 ? Ui.WARN : Ui.DANGER;
    }

    static String mb(long bytes) {
        return bytes >= 1L << 30 ? String.format("%.1f GB", bytes / (double) (1L << 30)) : (bytes >> 20) + " MB";
    }

    void draw(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        float gap = 12;
        float cardW = (w - gap * 5) / 6;
        float cardH = 74;
        card(ui, x, y, cardW, cardH, strings.get("perf.fps"), Integer.toString(perf.fps), Ui.FG, null);
        card(ui, x + (cardW + gap), y, cardW, cardH, strings.get("perf.frame"),
                String.format("%.1f ms", perf.average), frameColor(perf.average), null);
        card(ui, x + (cardW + gap) * 2, y, cardW, cardH, strings.get("perf.low"),
                String.format("%.1f ms", perf.worst), frameColor(perf.worst), null);
        float heapShare = perf.heapMax > 0 ? perf.heapUsed / (float) perf.heapMax : 0;
        card(ui, x + (cardW + gap) * 3, y, cardW, cardH, strings.get("perf.heap"), mb(perf.heapUsed),
                heapShare > 0.9f ? Ui.DANGER : Ui.FG, "/ " + mb(perf.heapMax));
        card(ui, x + (cardW + gap) * 4, y, cardW, cardH, strings.get("perf.gc"),
                perf.gcRecentMillis + " ms", perf.gcRecentMillis > 50 ? Ui.WARN : Ui.FG,
                strings.format("perf.gcBody", perf.gcCount, perf.gcMillis));
        card(ui, x + (cardW + gap) * 5, y, cardW, cardH, strings.get("perf.cpu"),
                perf.cpu < 0 ? "—" : Math.round(perf.cpu * 100) + "%", Ui.FG, strings.format("perf.threads", perf.threads));

        float gy = y + cardH + gap;
        float gh = h - cardH - gap - 90;
        ui.panel(x, gy, w, gh, 10, Ui.BASE, Ui.LINE);
        int frames = Math.min(perf.count, 300);
        ui.text(ui.bold, 12, x + 14, gy + 10, Ui.MUTED, strings.format("perf.graph", frames));
        graph(ui, x + 14, gy + 32, w - 28, gh - 46, frames, true);

        float sy = gy + gh + gap;
        boolean hud = ui.checkbox(x, sy + 4, strings.get("perf.hud"), options.hud);
        if (hud != options.hud) {
            options.hud = hud;
            saveSettings.run();
        }
        String corner = strings.get("perf.corner");
        float cx = x + 300;
        ui.text(ui.regular, 13, cx, sy + 5, Ui.MUTED, corner);
        cx += ui.measure(ui.regular, 13, corner) + 10;
        String[] arrows = {"↖", "↗", "↙", "↘"};
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            if (ui.pill(cx + i * 34, sy, 30, 26, arrows[i], options.hudCorner == i) && options.hudCorner != i) {
                options.hudCorner = i;
                saveSettings.run();
            }
        }
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, x, sy + 40, w, Ui.FAINT, strings.get("perf.system") + ": " + system);
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, x, sy + 58, w, Ui.FAINT, renderer);
    }

    private static void card(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h, String label, String value, int color, String sub) {
        ui.panel(x, y, w, h, 10, Ui.RAISED, Ui.LINE);
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, x + 12, y + 10, w - 24, Ui.MUTED, label);
        ui.textFit(ui.bold, 22, x + 12, y + 28, w - 24, color, value);
        if (sub != null) {
            float vw = Math.min(w - 24, ui.measure(ui.bold, 22, value));
            ui.textFit(ui.regular, 11, x + 12 + vw + 6, y + 38, w - 30 - vw, Ui.FAINT, sub);
        }
    }

    /** Bars of the last {@code frames} frame times, newest on the right, scaled to the slowest one. */
    void graph(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h, int frames, boolean guides) {
        if (frames == 0) {
            return;
        }
        float max = 16.7f;
        for (int i = 0; i < frames; i++) {
            max = Math.max(max, perf.frame(i));
        }
        max = Math.min(max * 1.1f, 250);
        float bar = w / frames;
        for (int i = 0; i < frames; i++) {
            float ms = perf.frame(i);
            float bh = Math.max(1, h * Math.min(ms, max) / max);
            ui.rect(x + w - (i + 1) * bar, y + h - bh, Math.max(bar - (bar > 3 ? 1 : 0), 0.6f), bh, frameColor(ms));
        }
        // Guides go over the bars so they stay readable.
        if (guides) {
            for (float ms : new float[] {16.7f, 33.3f}) {
                if (ms < max) {
                    float gy = y + h - h * ms / max;
                    ui.rect(x, gy, w, 1, 0x60ece8f6);
                    ui.text(ui.regular, 10, x + 4, gy - 13, Ui.FG, ms < 20 ? "60 FPS" : "30 FPS");
                }
            }
        }
    }

    void drawHud(Ui ui, float screenW, float screenH) {
        float w = 196;
        float h = 64;
        float m = 8;
        float x = options.hudCorner % 2 == 0 ? m : screenW - w - m;
        float y = options.hudCorner < 2 ? m : screenH - h - m;
        ui.round(x, y, w, h, 8, 0xc00b0910);
        float tw = ui.text(ui.bold, 15, x + 10, y + 7, Ui.FG, Integer.toString(perf.fps));
        ui.text(ui.regular, 11, x + 10 + tw + 4, y + 10, Ui.MUTED, "FPS");
        String frame = String.format("%.1f ms", perf.average);
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, x + 82, y + 9, frameColor(perf.average), frame);
        String heap = mb(perf.heapUsed);
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, x + w - 10 - ui.measure(ui.regular, 12, heap), y + 9, Ui.MUTED, heap);
        graph(ui, x + 10, y + 30, w - 20, h - 38, 120, false);
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Platform.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

/** What the overlay needs from the window library the game uses. */
interface Platform {
    /** Whether the game's window is known yet; nothing else may be called before. */
    boolean hasWindow();

    /** Whether the game's OpenGL context is current on this thread (false under Vulkan). */
    boolean hasGlContext();

    /** Fills {framebuffer width, framebuffer height, window width, window height}. */
    void sizes(int[] out);

    /** The monitor's UI scale as the OS reports it (1 when unknown). */
    float contentScale();

    String clipboard();

    void setClipboard(String text);

    /** Frees the cursor for the overlay, remembering how the game had it. */
    void showCursor();

    /** Gives the cursor back to the game in the mode it last asked for. */
    void restoreCursor();
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/ProfileView.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

/** The profile tab: lifetime stats across every game with the overlay, and the launcher's achievements. */
final class ProfileView {
    private final Stats stats;
    private final Options options;
    private final Strings strings;
    private final Runnable saveSettings;
    private float scroll;

    ProfileView(Stats stats, Options options, Runnable saveSettings) {
        this.stats = stats;
        this.options = options;
        this.strings = options.strings;
        this.saveSettings = saveSettings;
    }

    void draw(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        String[] keys = {"playMillis", "sessions", "deaths", "advancements", "screenshots", "crashes"};
        float gap = 12;
        float cardW = (w - gap * (keys.length - 1)) / keys.length;
        float cardH = 70;
        for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
            long value = stats.get(keys[i]);
            String shown = "playMillis".equals(keys[i]) ? hours(value) : Long.toString(value);
            float cx = x + i * (cardW + gap);
            ui.panel(cx, y, cardW, cardH, 10, Ui.RAISED, Ui.LINE);
            ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, cx + 12, y + 12, cardW - 24, Ui.MUTED, strings.get("stat." + keys[i]));
            ui.textFit(ui.bold, 20, cx + 12, y + 32, cardW - 24, Ui.FG, shown);
        }

        float ay = y + cardH + gap + 4;
        ui.text(ui.bold, 14, x, ay, Ui.FG, strings.format("ach.title", stats.unlocked.size(), Stats.ACHIEVEMENTS.length));
        boolean cat = ui.checkbox(x + w - 16 - ui.measure(ui.regular, 13, strings.get("cat.toggle")) - 24, ay, strings.get("cat.toggle"), options.cat);
        if (cat != options.cat) {
            options.cat = cat;
            saveSettings.run();
        }
        ay += 30;
        int columns = w > 640 ? 3 : 2;
        float tileW = (w - gap * (columns - 1)) / columns;
        float tileH = 50;
        float step = tileH + 8;
        int rows = (Stats.ACHIEVEMENTS.length + columns - 1) / columns;
        float viewH = y + h - ay;
        scroll -= ui.takeScroll(x, ay, w, viewH) * step;
        scroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(scroll, Math.max(0, rows * step - 8 - viewH)));
        ui.clip(x, ay, w, viewH);
        for (int i = 0; i < Stats.ACHIEVEMENTS.length; i++) {
            Stats.Achievement a = Stats.ACHIEVEMENTS[i];
            float tx = x + (i % columns) * (tileW + gap);
            float ty = ay + (i / columns) * step - scroll;
            if (ty + tileH < ay || ty > y + h) {
                continue;
            }
            boolean done = stats.unlocked.contains(a.id);
            ui.panel(tx, ty, tileW, tileH, 10, done ? 0x26a259f7 : Ui.BASE, done ? 0xff6d3fc0 : Ui.LINE);
            ui.round(tx + 12, ty + 11, 28, 28, 8, done ? Ui.ACCENT : Ui.RAISED);
            if (done) {
                ui.round(tx + 21, ty + 20, 10, 10, 5, 0xffffffff);
            }
            ui.textFit(ui.bold, 13, tx + 52, ty + 8, tileW - 64, done ? Ui.FG : Ui.MUTED, strings.get("ach." + a.id));
            String detail = strings.get("ach." + a.id + ".d");
            if (!done && a.counter != null) {
                long have = stats.get(a.counter);
                detail += " · " + ("playMillis".equals(a.counter) ? hours(have) + " / " + hours(a.goal) : have + " / " + a.goal);
            }
            ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, tx + 52, ty + 27, tileW - 64, done ? Ui.MUTED : Ui.FAINT, detail);
        }
        ui.unclip();
    }

    private String hours(long millis) {
        long minutes = millis / 60000;
        return strings.format("stat.hours", minutes / 60, minutes % 60);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Replay.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.awt.image.DataBufferByte;
import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

import javax.imageio.IIOImage;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import javax.imageio.ImageWriteParam;
import javax.imageio.ImageWriter;
import javax.imageio.stream.MemoryCacheImageOutputStream;

/**
 * The replay buffer: the last {@link #SECONDS} seconds of the game, kept as JPEG frames in memory
 * (about a megabyte a second), saved on request as a GIF, an MJPEG AVI or, when ffmpeg is installed,
 * an MP4. Frames are read back on the render thread by {@link GlCapture}; compressing and saving run on
 * a worker thread of their own so the game never waits for them.
 */
final class Replay {
    static final int SECONDS = 30;
    static final int FPS = 15;
    /** Captured frames are at most this wide; GIFs are scaled down further. */
    static final int MAX_WIDTH = 854;
    private static final int GIF_WIDTH = 480;
    private static final int GIF_FPS = 10;
    private static final DateTimeFormatter NAME = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd_HH.mm.ss");

    private static final class Shot {
        final byte[] jpeg;
        final long time;
        final int width;
        final int height;

        Shot(byte[] jpeg, long time, int width, int height) {
            this.jpeg = jpeg;
            this.time = time;
            this.width = width;
            this.height = height;
        }
    }

    private final Path folder;
    private final ExecutorService worker = Executors.newSingleThreadExecutor(runnable -> {
        Thread thread = new Thread(runnable, "k0t1k-overlay-replay");
        thread.setDaemon(true);
        thread.setPriority(Thread.MIN_PRIORITY);
        return thread;
    });
    /** Touched only by the worker thread. */
    private final ArrayDeque<Shot> shots = new ArrayDeque<>();
    private long nextCapture;
    /** Frames read back but not compressed yet; past a few, capturing pauses. */
    private final AtomicInteger pending = new AtomicInteger();
    private volatile boolean saving;
    private final Consumer<Throwable> onError;

    Replay(Path gameDir, Consumer<Throwable> onError) {
        this.folder = gameDir.resolve("replays");
        this.onError = onError;
    }

    /** Whether the render thread should read back a frame now; keeps to {@link #FPS}. */
    boolean due(long now) {
        if (now < nextCapture || pending.get() > 4) {
            return false;
        }
        nextCapture = Math.max(nextCapture + 1000 / FPS, now);
        return true;
    }

    /** Takes a read-back frame (render thread) and compresses it on the worker. */
    void add(GlCapture.Frame frame) {
        pending.incrementAndGet();
        worker.execute(() -> {
            try {
                byte[] jpeg = jpeg(frame);
                shots.addLast(new Shot(jpeg, frame.time, frame.width, frame.height));
                long oldest = frame.time - SECONDS * 1000L;
                while (!shots.isEmpty() && shots.peekFirst().time < oldest) {
                    shots.removeFirst();
                }
            } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
            } finally {
                pending.decrementAndGet();
            }
        });
    }

    boolean saving() {
        return saving;
    }

    /**
     * Saves what the buffer holds as "gif" or "video" (MP4 with ffmpeg, else AVI) and reports the
     * file, or null with nothing recorded yet, to {@code done} on the worker thread.
     */
    void save(String format, Consumer<Path> done) {
        saving = true;
        worker.execute(() -> {
            try {
                List<Shot> copy = new ArrayList<>(shots);
                if (copy.isEmpty()) {
                    done.accept(null);
                    return;
                }
                Files.createDirectories(folder);
                String base = "replay_" + LocalDateTime.now().format(NAME);
                Path file;
                if ("gif".equals(format)) {
                    file = folder.resolve(base + ".gif");
                    writeGif(copy, file);
                } else {
                    String ffmpeg = findFfmpeg();
                    if (ffmpeg != null) {
                        file = folder.resolve(base + ".mp4");
                        writeMp4(copy, file, ffmpeg);
                    } else {
                        file = folder.resolve(base + ".avi");
                        writeAvi(copy, file);
                    }
                }
                done.accept(file);
            } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
                done.accept(null);
            } finally {
                saving = false;
            }
        });
    }

    void stop() {
        worker.shutdownNow();
    }

    // ---- encoding ----

    /** Bottom-up RGBA to a top-down JPEG. */
    private static byte[] jpeg(GlCapture.Frame frame) throws IOException {
        BufferedImage image = new BufferedImage(frame.width, frame.height, BufferedImage.TYPE_3BYTE_BGR);
        byte[] bgr = ((DataBufferByte) image.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).getData();
        ByteBuffer rgba = frame.pixels;
        int w = frame.width;
        for (int y = 0; y < frame.height; y++) {
            int src = (frame.height - 1 - y) * w * 4;
            int dst = y * w * 3;
            for (int x = 0; x < w; x++) {
                bgr[dst + x * 3] = rgba.get(src + x * 4 + 2);
                bgr[dst + x * 3 + 1] = rgba.get(src + x * 4 + 1);
                bgr[dst + x * 3 + 2] = rgba.get(src + x * 4);
            }
        }
        Iterator<ImageWriter> writers = ImageIO.getImageWritersByFormatName("jpeg");
        if (!writers.hasNext()) {
            throw new IOException("no JPEG encoder in this Java");
        }
        ImageWriter writer = writers.next();
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream(64 * 1024);
        try (MemoryCacheImageOutputStream stream = new MemoryCacheImageOutputStream(out)) {
            writer.setOutput(stream);
            ImageWriteParam param = writer.getDefaultWriteParam();
            param.setCompressionMode(ImageWriteParam.MODE_EXPLICIT);
            param.setCompressionQuality(0.82f);
            writer.write(null, new IIOImage(image, null, null), param);
        } finally {
            writer.dispose();
        }
        return out.toByteArray();
    }

    private static BufferedImage decode(Shot shot) throws IOException {
        BufferedImage image = ImageIO.read(new ByteArrayInputStream(shot.jpeg));
        if (image == null) {
            throw new IOException("unreadable replay frame");
        }
        return image;
    }

    private static void writeGif(List<Shot> shots, Path file) throws IOException {
        Shot first = shots.get(0);
        int w = Math.min(GIF_WIDTH, first.width);
        int h = Math.max(2, Math.round(first.height * (w / (float) first.width)));
        try (OutputStream out = new java.io.BufferedOutputStream(Files.newOutputStream(file))) {
            GifWriter gif = new GifWriter(out, w, h);
            long step = 1000 / GIF_FPS;
            long next = first.time;
            for (Shot shot : shots) {
                if (shot.time < next) {
                    continue;
                }
                next = shot.time + step;
                gif.frame(decode(shot), (int) (step / 10));
            }
            gif.finish();
        }
    }

    private static void writeAvi(List<Shot> shots, Path file) throws IOException {
        Shot first = shots.get(0);
        float seconds = Math.max(1, shots.get(shots.size() - 1).time - first.time) / 1000f;
        int fps = Math.max(1, Math.round((shots.size() - 1) / seconds));
        List<byte[]> frames = new ArrayList<>(shots.size());
        for (Shot shot : shots) {
            frames.add(shot.jpeg);
        }
        AviWriter.write(file, first.width, first.height, fps, frames);
    }

    private static void writeMp4(List<Shot> shots, Path file, String ffmpeg) throws IOException {
        Shot first = shots.get(0);
        float seconds = Math.max(1, shots.get(shots.size() - 1).time - first.time) / 1000f;
        String fps = String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", Math.max(1, (shots.size() - 1) / seconds));
        // Even sizes for yuv420p; the frames are fed as one MJPEG stream.
        ProcessBuilder builder = new ProcessBuilder(ffmpeg, "-y", "-loglevel", "error", "-f", "mjpeg",
                "-framerate", fps, "-i", "-", "-vf", "scale=trunc(iw/2)*2:trunc(ih/2)*2",
                "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "veryfast", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", "-movflags", "+faststart",
                file.toString());
        builder.redirectErrorStream(true);
        builder.redirectOutput(new File(System.getProperty("os.name", "").startsWith("Windows") ? "NUL" : "/dev/null"));
        Process process = builder.start();
        try (OutputStream in = process.getOutputStream()) {
            for (Shot shot : shots) {
                in.write(shot.jpeg);
            }
        }
        try {
            int code = process.waitFor();
            if (code != 0) {
                throw new IOException("ffmpeg failed with exit code " + code);
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new IOException("interrupted while ffmpeg ran", e);
        }
    }

    /** ffmpeg on PATH, if any. */
    private static String findFfmpeg() {
        String path = System.getenv("PATH");
        if (path == null) {
            return null;
        }
        boolean windows = System.getProperty("os.name", "").startsWith("Windows");
        for (String dir : path.split(File.pathSeparator)) {
            File candidate = new File(dir, windows ? "ffmpeg.exe" : "ffmpeg");
            if (candidate.isFile() && candidate.canExecute()) {
                return candidate.getAbsolutePath();
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Resources.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;

/** Reads files bundled in the agent jar. */
final class Resources {
    private Resources() {
    }

    static byte[] read(String name) throws IOException {
        try (InputStream in = Resources.class.getResourceAsStream(name)) {
            if (in == null) {
                throw new IOException("missing resource " + name);
            }
            ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            byte[] buffer = new byte[65536];
            for (int n; (n = in.read(buffer)) > 0; ) {
                out.write(buffer, 0, n);
            }
            return out.toByteArray();
        }
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Screenshots.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.DirectoryStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
import java.util.concurrent.ScheduledExecutorService;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Notices the screenshots the game saves (F2) by watching {@code <gameDir>/screenshots}, and keeps a
 * caption for each in {@code <dataDir>/screenshots.tsv}: file name, time, instance, dimension and
 * position, so the launcher's gallery can show where a shot was taken.
 */
final class Screenshots {
    final Path folder;
    private final Path captions;
    private final ScheduledExecutorService io;
    private final Executor render;
    private final Consumer<Throwable> onError;
    private final Set<String> known = new HashSet<>();
    /** The newest file names first, for the tools tab; render thread only. */
    final List<String> recent = new ArrayList<>();
    /** Called on the render thread with each new file name. */
    Consumer<String> onNew = name -> { };

    Screenshots(Path gameDir, Path dataDir, ScheduledExecutorService io, Executor render, Consumer<Throwable> onError) {
        this.folder = gameDir.resolve("screenshots");
        this.captions = dataDir.resolve("screenshots.tsv");
        this.io = io;
        this.render = render;
        this.onError = onError;
    }

    void start() {
        io.execute(() -> {
            try {
                known.addAll(list());
            } catch (IOException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
            }
            io.scheduleWithFixedDelay(this::poll, 1, 1, TimeUnit.SECONDS);
        });
    }

    private void poll() {
        try {
            for (String name : list()) {
                if (known.add(name)) {
                    render.execute(() -> {
                        recent.add(0, name);
                        if (recent.size() > 8) {
                            recent.remove(recent.size() - 1);
                        }
                        onNew.accept(name);
                    });
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
            onError.accept(e);
        }
    }

    private List<String> list() throws IOException {
        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();
        if (!Files.isDirectory(folder)) {
            return names;
        }
        try (DirectoryStream<Path> files = Files.newDirectoryStream(folder, "*.png")) {
            for (Path file : files) {
                names.add(file.getFileName().toString());
            }
        }
        names.sort(null);
        return names;
    }

    /** Appends one caption line; called on the render thread, written on the io thread. */
    void caption(String name, String instance, String dimension, String position) {
        String row = String.join("\t", name, Long.toString(System.currentTimeMillis()), clean(instance),
                clean(dimension), clean(position)) + "\n";
        io.execute(() -> {
            try {
                Files.createDirectories(captions.getParent());
                Files.write(captions, row.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8), StandardOpenOption.CREATE, StandardOpenOption.APPEND);
            } catch (IOException e) {
                onError.accept(e);
            }
        });
    }

    private static String clean(String value) {
        return value.replace('\t', ' ').replace('\n', ' ');
    }
}
```

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

/**
 * The SDL window backend (Minecraft 26.3+), kept behind an interface because it is compiled against
 * LWJGL 3.4 (the first with SDL bindings) and is only loaded when the game uses SDL.
 */
interface SdlBackend extends Platform {
    /** A hooked SDL call, answered as {@link Overlay#apply} does; {@code result} is set on exit. */
    Object call(String method, Object[] args, Object result, boolean exit);
}
```
