`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Stats.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.nio.file.DirectoryStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.Properties;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Lifetime counters across every game started with the overlay (play time, deaths, crashes...) and the
 * launcher's own achievements on top of them. The file lives in the shared data folder, so two games
 * may run at once: each save re-reads it and adds only this game's changes since the last save.
 *
 * <p>Counters change on the render thread; {@link #saveTask} snapshots them for the io thread.
 */
final class Stats {
    static final String[] COUNTERS = {"playMillis", "sessions", "deaths", "advancements", "screenshots", "crashes", "timers"};

    /** An achievement: unlocked when {@code counter} reaches {@code goal}; a null counter is checked by hand. */
    static final class Achievement {
        final String id;
        final String counter;
        final long goal;

        Achievement(String id, String counter, long goal) {
            this.id = id;
            this.counter = counter;
            this.goal = goal;
        }
    }

    static final long HOUR = 3_600_000L;
    static final Achievement[] ACHIEVEMENTS = {
        new Achievement("hour1", "playMillis", HOUR),
        new Achievement("hour10", "playMillis", 10 * HOUR),
        new Achievement("hour100", "playMillis", 100 * HOUR),
        new Achievement("sessions50", "sessions", 50),
        new Achievement("death1", "deaths", 1),
        new Achievement("death9", "deaths", 9),
        new Achievement("adv10", "advancements", 10),
        new Achievement("shot10", "screenshots", 10),
        new Achievement("crash1", "crashes", 1),
        new Achievement("crash10", "crashes", 10),
        new Achievement("timer1", "timers", 1),
        new Achievement("nightOwl", null, 0),
    };

    private final Path file;
    private final Path lock;
    private final Path crashReports;
    private final long started = System.currentTimeMillis();
    private final Properties totals = new Properties();
    /** This game's changes not yet written. */
    private final Properties pending = new Properties();
    final Set<String> unlocked = new LinkedHashSet<>();
    private long lastTick = started;
    /** Called on the render thread with each newly unlocked achievement id. */
    Consumer<String> onUnlock = id -> { };

    Stats(Path dataDir, Path gameDir) {
        file = dataDir.resolve("stats.properties");
        // One lock per game folder: a lock left behind means that game did not exit cleanly.
        lock = dataDir.resolve("session-" + Integer.toHexString(gameDir.toString().hashCode()) + ".lock");
        crashReports = gameDir.resolve("crash-reports");
    }

    /** Reads the file and records this session; on the io thread before anything else uses the stats. */
    void load() throws IOException {
        readInto(totals);
        for (String id : totals.getProperty("unlocked", "").split(",")) {
            if (!id.isEmpty()) {
                unlocked.add(id);
            }
        }
        add("sessions", 1);
        if (Files.exists(lock)) {
            add("crashes", 1);
        }
        Files.createDirectories(file.getParent());
        Files.write(lock, Long.toString(started).getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }

    long get(String counter) {
        return parse(totals.getProperty(counter)) + parse(pending.getProperty(counter));
    }

    void add(String counter, long amount) {
        pending.setProperty(counter, Long.toString(parse(pending.getProperty(counter)) + amount));
        check();
    }

    /** Once per frame: counts play time and checks the time-based achievements. */
    void tick(long now) {
        if (now - lastTick >= 1000) {
            add("playMillis", now - lastTick);
            lastTick = now;
            int hour = LocalTime.now().getHour();
            if (hour >= 3 && hour < 5) {
                unlock("nightOwl");
            }
        }
    }

    private void check() {
        for (Achievement a : ACHIEVEMENTS) {
            if (a.counter != null && !unlocked.contains(a.id) && get(a.counter) >= a.goal) {
                unlock(a.id);
            }
        }
    }

    private void unlock(String id) {
        if (unlocked.add(id)) {
            onUnlock.accept(id);
        }
    }

    /** A snapshot of this game's changes for the io thread; merges them into the file there. */
    Runnable saveTask() {
        Properties delta = new Properties();
        delta.putAll(pending);
        for (String key : pending.stringPropertyNames()) {
            totals.setProperty(key, Long.toString(parse(totals.getProperty(key)) + parse(pending.getProperty(key))));
        }
        pending.clear();
        String ids = String.join(",", unlocked);
        return () -> {
            try {
                merge(delta, ids);
            } catch (IOException e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("could not save " + file, e);
            }
        };
    }

    /**
     * On JVM exit: a crash report written during this session counts as a crash; then the lock goes.
     * Returns that crash report, or null after a normal exit.
     */
    Path saveOnExit() {
        Path report = null;
        try {
            report = crashReport();
            if (report != null) {
                add("crashes", 1);
            }
            saveTask().run();
            Files.deleteIfExists(lock);
        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException e) {
            System.err.println("[k0t1k-overlay] could not save stats: " + e);
        }
        return report;
    }

    /** The newest crash report written since this game started, or null. */
    private Path crashReport() throws IOException {
        if (!Files.isDirectory(crashReports)) {
            return null;
        }
        Path newest = null;
        long newestTime = started;
        try (DirectoryStream<Path> reports = Files.newDirectoryStream(crashReports, "crash-*.txt")) {
            for (Path report : reports) {
                long time = Files.getLastModifiedTime(report).toMillis();
                if (time >= newestTime) {
                    newest = report;
                    newestTime = time;
                }
            }
        }
        return newest;
    }

    private synchronized void merge(Properties delta, String ids) throws IOException {
        Properties current = new Properties();
        readInto(current);
        for (String key : delta.stringPropertyNames()) {
            current.setProperty(key, Long.toString(parse(current.getProperty(key)) + parse(delta.getProperty(key))));
        }
        Set<String> all = new LinkedHashSet<>();
        for (String id : (current.getProperty("unlocked", "") + "," + ids).split(",")) {
            if (!id.isEmpty()) {
                all.add(id);
            }
        }
        current.setProperty("unlocked", String.join(",", all));
        Files.createDirectories(file.getParent());
        Path temp = file.resolveSibling("stats.properties.tmp");
        try (OutputStream out = Files.newOutputStream(temp)) {
            current.store(out, "k0t1k overlay stats");
        }
        Files.move(temp, file, java.nio.file.StandardCopyOption.REPLACE_EXISTING);
    }

    private void readInto(Properties target) throws IOException {
        if (Files.exists(file)) {
            try (InputStream in = Files.newInputStream(file)) {
                target.load(in);
            }
        }
    }

    private static long parse(String value) {
        if (value == null) {
            return 0;
        }
        try {
            return Long.parseLong(value.trim());
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return 0;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/StbFont.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.nio.IntBuffer;

import org.lwjgl.stb.STBTTFontinfo;

import static org.lwjgl.stb.STBTruetype.*;

/** A font rasterised with stb_truetype, which LWJGL 3 (Minecraft 1.13 and newer) ships. */
final class StbFont extends Font {
    private final ByteBuffer data; // stb keeps pointers into it, so it must stay reachable
    private final STBTTFontinfo info = STBTTFontinfo.create();
    private final int ascent;
    private final int descent;
    private final int lineGap;
    private final IntBuffer a = ints();
    private final IntBuffer b = ints();
    private final IntBuffer c = ints();
    private final IntBuffer d = ints();

    StbFont(String resource, Atlas atlas) throws IOException {
        super(atlas);
        byte[] bytes = Resources.read(resource);
        data = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(bytes.length).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
        data.put(bytes).flip();
        if (!stbtt_InitFont(info, data)) {
            throw new IOException("unreadable font " + resource);
        }
        stbtt_GetFontVMetrics(info, a, b, c);
        ascent = a.get(0);
        descent = b.get(0);
        lineGap = c.get(0);
    }

    private static IntBuffer ints() {
        return ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(4).order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder()).asIntBuffer();
    }

    private float scale(float px) {
        return stbtt_ScaleForPixelHeight(info, px);
    }

    @Override
    float ascent(float px) {
        return ascent * scale(px);
    }

    @Override
    float lineHeight(float px) {
        return (ascent - descent + lineGap) * scale(px);
    }

    @Override
    boolean has(int codepoint) {
        return stbtt_FindGlyphIndex(info, codepoint) != 0;
    }

    @Override
    Glyph rasterise(int codepoint, float px) {
        float scale = scale(px);
        int index = stbtt_FindGlyphIndex(info, codepoint);
        stbtt_GetGlyphHMetrics(info, index, a, b);
        float advance = a.get(0) * scale;
        stbtt_GetGlyphBitmapBox(info, index, scale, scale, a, b, c, d);
        int x0 = a.get(0);
        int y0 = b.get(0);
        int w = c.get(0) - x0;
        int h = d.get(0) - y0;
        if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) {
            return new Glyph(advance, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
        }
        ByteBuffer pixels = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(w * h);
        stbtt_MakeGlyphBitmap(info, pixels, w, h, w, scale, scale, index);
        return place(advance, x0, y0, w, h, pixels);
    }

    @Override
    float kern(int left, int right, float px) {
        return stbtt_GetCodepointKernAdvance(info, left, right) * scale(px);
    }
}
```

```java
    private void add(String key, String en, String ru) {
        table.put(key, russian ? ru : en);
    }

    String get(String key) {
        String value = table.get(key);
        return value == null ? key : value;
    }

    String format(String key, Object... args) {
        return String.format(get(key), args);
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/TextEdit.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

/** Editable text with a caret and a selection ({@link #anchor} to {@link #caret}), in UTF-16 indices. */
final class TextEdit {
    final StringBuilder text = new StringBuilder();
    int caret;
    int anchor;
    /** Scroll offset kept by the field that draws this edit. */
    float scroll;
    boolean caretMoved;
    boolean dragging;

    TextEdit() {
    }

    TextEdit(String initial) {
        text.append(initial);
        caret = anchor = text.length();
    }

    String value() {
        return text.toString();
    }

    void set(String value) {
        text.setLength(0);
        text.append(value);
        caret = anchor = text.length();
        scroll = 0;
        caretMoved = true;
    }

    boolean hasSelection() {
        return caret != anchor;
    }

    String selected() {
        return text.substring(Math.min(caret, anchor), Math.max(caret, anchor));
    }

    void moveTo(int index, boolean select) {
        caret = Math.max(0, Math.min(text.length(), index));
        if (!select) {
            anchor = caret;
        }
        caretMoved = true;
    }

    void selectAll() {
        anchor = 0;
        caret = text.length();
        caretMoved = true;
    }

    void selectWord(int index) {
        anchor = wordStart(index);
        caret = wordEnd(index);
        caretMoved = true;
    }

    /** Replaces the selection (or inserts at the caret). */
    void insert(String value) {
        int start = Math.min(caret, anchor);
        text.replace(start, Math.max(caret, anchor), value);
        caret = anchor = start + value.length();
        caretMoved = true;
    }

    boolean backspace(boolean word) {
        if (hasSelection()) {
            insert("");
            return true;
        }
        if (caret == 0) {
            return false;
        }
        anchor = word ? wordStart(caret) : text.offsetByCodePoints(caret, -1);
        insert("");
        return true;
    }

    boolean delete(boolean word) {
        if (hasSelection()) {
            insert("");
            return true;
        }
        if (caret == text.length()) {
            return false;
        }
        anchor = word ? wordEnd(caret) : text.offsetByCodePoints(caret, 1);
        insert("");
        return true;
    }

    void left(boolean word, boolean select) {
        if (hasSelection() && !select) {
            moveTo(Math.min(caret, anchor), false);
        } else if (caret > 0) {
            moveTo(word ? wordStart(caret) : text.offsetByCodePoints(caret, -1), select);
        }
    }

    void right(boolean word, boolean select) {
        if (hasSelection() && !select) {
            moveTo(Math.max(caret, anchor), false);
        } else if (caret < text.length()) {
            moveTo(word ? wordEnd(caret) : text.offsetByCodePoints(caret, 1), select);
        }
    }

    int lineStart() {
        int i = caret;
        while (i > 0 && text.charAt(i - 1) != '\n') {
            i--;
        }
        return i;
    }

    int lineEnd() {
        int i = caret;
        while (i < text.length() && text.charAt(i) != '\n') {
            i++;
        }
        return i;
    }

    /** Start of the word left of {@code index}, skipping whitespace first. */
    private int wordStart(int index) {
        int i = index;
        while (i > 0 && Character.isWhitespace(text.charAt(i - 1))) {
            i--;
        }
        while (i > 0 && !Character.isWhitespace(text.charAt(i - 1))) {
            i--;
        }
        return i;
    }

    /** End of the word right of {@code index}, skipping whitespace first. */
    private int wordEnd(int index) {
        int i = index;
        while (i < text.length() && Character.isWhitespace(text.charAt(i))) {
            i++;
        }
        while (i < text.length() && !Character.isWhitespace(text.charAt(i))) {
            i++;
        }
        return i;
    }
}
```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Timers.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
import java.util.function.Consumer;

/**
 * Countdown timers and alarms ("check the farm in 20 minutes"). They are stored with their absolute
 * due time, so they survive a restart of the game; one that came due while the game was closed fires
 * on the next start. Lives on the render thread; files are written on the io thread.
 */
final class Timers {
    static final class Timer {
        final String label;
        final long due;
        final long length;

        Timer(String label, long due, long length) {
            this.label = label;
            this.due = due;
            this.length = length;
        }
    }

    final List<Timer> timers = new ArrayList<>();
    private final Path file;
    private final Executor io;
    private final Consumer<Throwable> onError;
    /** Called on the render thread when a timer fires. */
    Consumer<Timer> onFire = timer -> { };

    Timers(Path dataDir, Executor io, Consumer<Throwable> onError) {
        this.file = dataDir.resolve("timers.tsv");
        this.io = io;
        this.onError = onError;
    }

    /** Reads the saved timers; a small file, read once at start. */
    void load() {
        if (!Files.exists(file)) {
            return;
        }
        try {
            for (String row : Files.readAllLines(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8)) {
                String[] parts = row.split("\t", 3);
                if (parts.length == 3) {
                    timers.add(new Timer(parts[2], Long.parseLong(parts[0]), Long.parseLong(parts[1])));
                }
            }
        } catch (IOException | NumberFormatException e) {
            onError.accept(e);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Parses what the player typed: minutes ("20", "1.5") for a countdown, or a clock time ("14:30") for
     * an alarm, which is tomorrow when that time has passed today. Returns the due time, or -1.
     */
    static long parseDue(String text, long now) {
        String value = text.trim().replace(',', '.');
        if (value.isEmpty()) {
            return -1;
        }
        try {
            if (value.contains(":")) {
                String[] parts = value.split(":");
                LocalTime at = LocalTime.of(Integer.parseInt(parts[0].trim()), Integer.parseInt(parts[1].trim()));
                ZoneId zone = ZoneId.systemDefault();
                LocalDateTime today = LocalDateTime.now(zone).with(at).withSecond(0).withNano(0);
                long due = today.atZone(zone).toInstant().toEpochMilli();
                return due <= now ? today.plusDays(1).atZone(zone).toInstant().toEpochMilli() : due;
            }
            double minutes = Double.parseDouble(value);
            if (minutes <= 0 || minutes > 7 * 24 * 60) {
                return -1;
            }
            return now + Math.round(minutes * 60_000);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            return -1;
        }
    }

    void add(String label, long due, long now) {
        timers.add(new Timer(label, due, due - now));
        timers.sort((a, b) -> Long.compare(a.due, b.due));
        save();
    }

    void remove(Timer timer) {
        if (timers.remove(timer)) {
            save();
        }
    }

    /** Once per frame: fires the timers that came due. */
    void tick(long now) {
        boolean fired = false;
        while (!timers.isEmpty() && timers.get(0).due <= now) {
            onFire.accept(timers.remove(0));
            fired = true;
        }
        if (fired) {
            save();
        }
    }

    private void save() {
        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder();
        for (Timer timer : timers) {
            out.append(timer.due).append('\t').append(timer.length).append('\t')
                    .append(timer.label.replace('\t', ' ').replace('\n', ' ')).append('\n');
        }
        byte[] bytes = out.toString().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        io.execute(() -> {
            try {
                Files.createDirectories(file.getParent());
                Files.write(file, bytes);
            } catch (IOException e) {
                throw new IllegalStateException("could not save " + file, e);
            }
        });
    }

    /** "1:05:09" or "4:59". */
    static String remaining(long millis) {
        long s = Math.max(0, (millis + 999) / 1000);
        long h = s / 3600;
        long m = s / 60 % 60;
        return h > 0 ? String.format("%d:%02d:%02d", h, m, s % 60) : String.format("%d:%02d", m, s % 60);
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/ToolsView.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * The tools tab, things players otherwise work out on paper: Overworld/Nether coordinates, stacks and
 * shulker boxes, timers and alarms, and the latest screenshots.
 */
final class ToolsView {
    private static final int[] QUICK_MINUTES = {5, 10, 20, 30, 60};

    private final Strings strings;
    private final Overlay overlay;
    private final GameState game;
    private final Timers timers;
    private final Screenshots screenshots;

    private final TextEdit overX = new TextEdit();
    private final TextEdit overZ = new TextEdit();
    private final TextEdit netherX = new TextEdit();
    private final TextEdit netherZ = new TextEdit();

    private final TextEdit shulkers = new TextEdit();
    private final TextEdit stacks = new TextEdit();
    private final TextEdit items = new TextEdit();
    private int stackSize = 64;

    private final TextEdit timerLabel = new TextEdit();
    private final TextEdit timerTime = new TextEdit();

    ToolsView(Strings strings, Overlay overlay, GameState game, Timers timers, Screenshots screenshots) {
        this.strings = strings;
        this.overlay = overlay;
        this.game = game;
        this.timers = timers;
        this.screenshots = screenshots;
    }

    void draw(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        float gap = 14;
        float cw = (w - gap) / 2;
        float ch = (h - gap) / 2;
        coordinates(ui, x, y, cw, ch);
        stacks(ui, x + cw + gap, y, cw, ch);
        timers(ui, x, y + ch + gap, cw, ch);
        screenshots(ui, x + cw + gap, y + ch + gap, cw, ch);
    }

    private float card(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h, String title) {
        ui.panel(x, y, w, h, 10, Ui.BASE, Ui.LINE);
        ui.text(ui.bold, 14, x + 16, y + 14, Ui.FG, title);
        return y + 42;
    }

    // ---- Overworld <-> Nether ----

    private void coordinates(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        float cy = card(ui, x, y, w, h, strings.get("tools.coords"));
        float px = x + 16;
        float pw = w - 32;
        String where = !game.available ? strings.get("tools.noPosition")
                : !game.inWorld ? strings.get("tools.notInWorld")
                : strings.format("tools.position", dimensionLabel(game.dimension), game.blockPosition());
        float labelW = 96;
        float fw = (pw - labelW - 8) / 2;
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, px + labelW, cy, Ui.FAINT, "X");
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, px + labelW + fw + 8, cy, Ui.FAINT, "Z");
        cy += 16;
        ui.text(ui.bold, 13, px, cy + 7, Ui.OK, strings.get("tools.overworld"));
        boolean overChanged = ui.field(overX, px + labelW, cy, fw, 30, false, "0");
        overChanged |= ui.field(overZ, px + labelW + fw + 8, cy, fw, 30, false, "0");
        cy += 36;
        ui.text(ui.bold, 13, px, cy + 7, Ui.DANGER, strings.get("tools.nether"));
        boolean netherChanged = ui.field(netherX, px + labelW, cy, fw, 30, false, "0");
        netherChanged |= ui.field(netherZ, px + labelW + fw + 8, cy, fw, 30, false, "0");
        cy += 38;
        if (overChanged) {
            netherX.set(scaled(overX.value(), 1 / 8.0));
            netherZ.set(scaled(overZ.value(), 1 / 8.0));
        } else if (netherChanged) {
            overX.set(scaled(netherX.value(), 8));
            overZ.set(scaled(netherZ.value(), 8));
        }
        boolean canFill = game.available && game.inWorld;
        String fill = strings.get("tools.here");
        float bw = ui.buttonWidth(fill);
        if (canFill && ui.button(px, cy, bw, 30, fill, true)) {
            long bx = (long) Math.floor(game.x);
            long bz = (long) Math.floor(game.z);
            if ("the_nether".equals(game.dimension)) {
                netherX.set(Long.toString(bx));
                netherZ.set(Long.toString(bz));
                overX.set(Long.toString(bx * 8));
                overZ.set(Long.toString(bz * 8));
            } else {
                overX.set(Long.toString(bx));
                overZ.set(Long.toString(bz));
                netherX.set(scaled(overX.value(), 1 / 8.0));
                netherZ.set(scaled(overZ.value(), 1 / 8.0));
            }
        }
        float tx = canFill ? px + bw + 12 : px;
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, tx, cy + (30 - ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 12)) / 2, px + pw - tx, Ui.MUTED, where);
        if (canFill && ui.hover(tx, cy, px + pw - tx, 30) && ui.clickedIn(tx, cy, px + pw - tx, 30)) {
            ui.platform.setClipboard(game.blockPosition());
            overlay.toast(strings.get("chat.copied"), game.blockPosition());
        }
    }

    /** Multiplies a typed block coordinate, rounding down like the game; empty for anything else. */
    private static String scaled(String typed, double factor) {
        try {
            String value = typed.trim();
            return value.isEmpty() || "-".equals(value) ? "" : Long.toString((long) Math.floor(Long.parseLong(value) * factor));
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return "";
        }
    }

    private String dimensionLabel(String dimension) {
        switch (dimension) {
            case "overworld":
                return strings.get("tools.overworld");
            case "the_nether":
                return strings.get("tools.nether");
            case "the_end":
                return strings.get("tools.end");
            default:
                return dimension;
        }
    }

    // ---- stacks and shulker boxes ----

    private void stacks(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        float cy = card(ui, x, y, w, h, strings.get("tools.stacks"));
        float px = x + 16;
        float pw = w - 32;
        float sizeX = x + w - 16;
        for (int size : new int[] {1, 16, 64}) {
            String label = Integer.toString(size);
            float pwid = ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, label) + 22;
            sizeX -= pwid;
            if (ui.pill(sizeX, y + 9, pwid, 26, label, stackSize == size)) {
                stackSize = size;
            }
            sizeX -= 4;
        }
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, sizeX - ui.measure(ui.regular, 12, strings.get("tools.stackSize")) - 6,
                y + 9 + (26 - ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 12)) / 2, Ui.FAINT, strings.get("tools.stackSize"));

        float fw = (pw - 16) / 3;
        String[] labels = {strings.get("tools.shulkers"), strings.get("tools.stacksField"), strings.get("tools.items")};
        TextEdit[] fields = {shulkers, stacks, items};
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
            float fx = px + i * (fw + 8);
            ui.text(ui.regular, 12, fx, cy, Ui.FAINT, labels[i]);
            ui.field(fields[i], fx, cy + 16, fw, 30, false, "0");
        }
        cy += 56;

        long total = count(shulkers) * 27L * stackSize + count(stacks) * stackSize + count(items);
        long perShulker = 27L * stackSize;
        String breakdown = strings.format("tools.breakdown", total / perShulker, total % perShulker / stackSize, total % stackSize);
        ui.text(ui.bold, 18, px, cy, Ui.FG, strings.format("tools.total", total));
        cy += 26;
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 13, px, cy, pw, Ui.MUTED, breakdown);
        cy += 19;
        long chests = (total + 54L * stackSize - 1) / (54L * stackSize);
        long singles = (total + perShulker - 1) / perShulker;
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 13, px, cy, pw, Ui.FAINT, strings.format("tools.storage", singles, chests));
    }

    private static long count(TextEdit edit) {
        try {
            String value = edit.value().trim();
            return value.isEmpty() ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.min(1_000_000_000L, Long.parseLong(value)));
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    // ---- timers and alarms ----

    private void timers(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        float cy = card(ui, x, y, w, h, strings.get("tools.timers"));
        float px = x + 16;
        float pw = w - 32;
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        String start = strings.get("tools.start");
        float sw = ui.buttonWidth(start);
        float timeW = 110;
        float labelW = pw - timeW - sw - 16;
        ui.field(timerLabel, px, cy, labelW, 32, false, strings.get("tools.timerLabel"));
        ui.field(timerTime, px + labelW + 8, cy, timeW, 32, false, strings.get("tools.timerTime"));
        long due = Timers.parseDue(timerTime.value(), now);
        boolean submit = ui.button(px + pw - sw, cy, sw, 32, start, true);
        if ((ui.focus == timerLabel || ui.focus == timerTime) && pressed(ui, Keys.ENTER, Keys.KP_ENTER)) {
            submit = true;
        }
        if (submit) {
            if (due < 0) {
                overlay.toast(strings.get("tools.timers"), strings.get("tools.badTime"));
            } else {
                String label = timerLabel.value().trim();
                timers.add(label.isEmpty() ? strings.get("tools.timerDefault") : label, due, now);
                timerLabel.set("");
                timerTime.set("");
                ui.focus = null;
            }
        }
        cy += 40;
        float qx = px;
        for (int minutes : QUICK_MINUTES) {
            String label = strings.format("tools.minutes", minutes);
            float qw = ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, label) + 20;
            if (ui.pill(qx, cy, qw, 26, label, false)) {
                timerTime.set(Integer.toString(minutes));
            }
            qx += qw + 4;
        }
        cy += 34;

        if (timers.timers.isEmpty()) {
            ui.text(ui.regular, 13, px, cy + 4, Ui.FAINT, strings.get("tools.noTimers"));
            return;
        }
        float rowH = 30;
        float bottom = y + h - 10;
        for (Timers.Timer timer : new java.util.ArrayList<>(timers.timers)) {
            if (cy + rowH > bottom) {
                break;
            }
            long left = timer.due - now;
            float progress = timer.length <= 0 ? 1 : 1 - Math.max(0, left) / (float) timer.length;
            ui.round(px, cy, pw, rowH - 4, 6, Ui.RAISED);
            ui.round(px, cy + rowH - 6, pw * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, progress)), 2, 1, Ui.ACCENT);
            String time = Timers.remaining(left);
            float tw = ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, time);
            ui.textFit(ui.regular, 13, px + 10, cy + 5, pw - tw - 56, Ui.FG, timer.label);
            ui.text(ui.bold, 13, px + pw - tw - 36, cy + 5, Ui.ACCENT, time);
            boolean hot = ui.hover(px + pw - 28, cy, 28, rowH - 4);
            ui.text(ui.bold, 13, px + pw - 20, cy + 5, hot ? Ui.DANGER : Ui.FAINT, "×");
            if (hot && ui.clickedIn(px + pw - 28, cy, 28, rowH - 4)) {
                timers.remove(timer);
            }
            cy += rowH;
        }
    }

    private static boolean pressed(Ui ui, int... wanted) {
        for (int[] key : ui.keys) {
            for (int k : wanted) {
                if (key[0] == k) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ---- screenshots ----

    /** The replay buffer row: on/off, format, save. Returns the y below it. */
    private float replay(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w) {
        Options options = overlay.options();
        boolean on = ui.checkbox(x, y + 6, strings.get("replay.toggle"), options.replay);
        if (on != options.replay) {
            options.replay = on;
            overlay.saveSettings();
        }
        float fx = x + ui.measure(ui.regular, 13, strings.get("replay.toggle")) + 40;
        String[] formats = {"video", "gif"};
        for (String format : formats) {
            String label = strings.get("replay." + format);
            float pw = ui.measure(ui.bold, 13, label) + 20;
            if (ui.pill(fx, y + 1, pw, 26, label, format.equals(options.replayFormat))) {
                options.replayFormat = format;
                overlay.saveSettings();
            }
            fx += pw + 4;
        }
        String save = strings.get("replay.save");
        float sw = ui.buttonWidth(save);
        if (ui.button(x + w - sw, y, sw, 28, save, overlay.replayAvailable())) {
            overlay.saveReplay();
        }
        String hint = overlay.replayAvailable() ? strings.get("replay.hint")
                : options.replay ? strings.get("replay.glOnly") : strings.get("replay.off");
        ui.textFit(ui.regular, 12, x, y + 34, w, Ui.FAINT, hint);
        return y + 56;
    }

    private void screenshots(Ui ui, float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        card(ui, x, y, w, h, strings.get("tools.screenshots"));
        float px = x + 16;
        float pw = w - 32;
        String open = strings.get("tools.openFolder");
        float ow = ui.buttonWidth(open);
        if (ui.button(x + w - 16 - ow, y + 8, ow, 28, open, false)) {
            try {
                java.nio.file.Files.createDirectories(screenshots.folder);
                Links.folder(screenshots.folder);
            } catch (IOException e) {
                overlay.report(e);
                overlay.toast(strings.get("error"), e.getMessage());
            }
        }
        float cy = replay(ui, px, y + 44, pw);
        List<String> recent = screenshots.recent;
        if (recent.isEmpty()) {
            List<int[]> lines = ui.wrap(ui.regular, 13, strings.get("tools.noShots"), pw);
            String text = strings.get("tools.noShots");
            for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++) {
                ui.text(ui.regular, 13, px, cy + i * ui.lineHeight(ui.regular, 13), Ui.FAINT, text, lines.get(i)[0], lines.get(i)[1]);
            }
            return;
        }
        ui.text(ui.regular, 12, px, cy, Ui.FAINT, strings.get("tools.copyPath"));
        cy += 20;
        float rowH = 28;
        for (String name : recent) {
            if (cy + rowH > y + h - 8) {
                break;
            }
            boolean hot = ui.hover(px - 6, cy, pw + 12, rowH - 2);
            if (hot) {
                ui.round(px - 6, cy, pw + 12, rowH - 2, 5, Ui.RAISED);
            }
            ui.textFit(ui.regular, 13, px, cy + 5, pw, Ui.FG, name);
            if (hot && ui.clickedIn(px - 6, cy, pw + 12, rowH - 2)) {
                String path = screenshots.folder.resolve(name).toString();
                ui.platform.setClipboard(path);
                overlay.toast(strings.get("chat.copied"), path);
            }
            cy += rowH;
        }
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/Ui.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Immediate-mode widgets in logical units (framebuffer pixels divided by {@link #scale}). Input
 * gathered between frames is consumed by the widgets of the next frame.
 */
final class Ui {
    // Palette of the launcher's default dark theme (apps/app/src/style.css).
    static final int BASE = 0xff0b0910;
    static final int SURFACE = 0xff13101c;
    static final int RAISED = 0xff1b1728;
    static final int HOVER = 0xff251f37;
    static final int LINE = 0xff2b2542;
    static final int FG = 0xffece8f6;
    static final int MUTED = 0xffa199ba;
    static final int FAINT = 0xff6d6588;
    static final int ACCENT = 0xffa259f7;
    static final int OK = 0xff4ade80;
    static final int WARN = 0xfffbbf24;
    static final int DANGER = 0xfff87171;

    final Batch batch;
    final Font regular;
    final Font bold;
    Platform platform;
    float scale = 1;

    // Input of the current frame.
    float mouseX = -1;
    float mouseY = -1;
    boolean mouseDown;
    boolean clicked;
    boolean released;
    boolean doubleClicked;
    float scrollY;
    final List<int[]> keys = new ArrayList<>(); // {key, mods} for presses and repeats
    final StringBuilder chars = new StringBuilder();
    private long lastClickTime;

    /** The text field that receives keyboard input, or null. */
    Object focus;
    /** Set when a widget consumed the click, so clicking empty space can drop the focus. */
    private boolean clickHandled;

    Ui(Batch batch, Font regular, Font bold, Platform platform) {
        this.batch = batch;
        this.regular = regular;
        this.bold = bold;
        this.platform = platform;
    }

    // ---- input, fed by the overlay between frames ----

    void onMove(float x, float y) {
        mouseX = x;
        mouseY = y;
    }

    void onButton(int button, boolean press) {
        if (button != 0) {
            return;
        }
        if (press) {
            long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
            doubleClicked = now - lastClickTime < 350;
            lastClickTime = now;
            clicked = true;
            mouseDown = true;
        } else {
            released = true;
            mouseDown = false;
        }
    }

    void onScroll(double dy) {
        scrollY += (float) dy;
    }

    void onKey(int key, int mods) {
        keys.add(new int[] {key, mods});
    }

    void onChar(int codepoint) {
        chars.appendCodePoint(codepoint);
    }

    /** Clears per-frame input; called after the widgets of a frame ran. */
    void endFrame() {
        if (clicked && !clickHandled) {
            focus = null;
        }
        clicked = false;
        released = false;
        doubleClicked = false;
        clickHandled = false;
        scrollY = 0;
        keys.clear();
        chars.setLength(0);
    }

    /** Drops all input, e.g. when the overlay closes, so nothing leaks into the next opening. */
    void resetInput() {
        endFrame();
        mouseDown = false;
        focus = null;
    }

    boolean hover(float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        return mouseX >= x && mouseX < x + w && mouseY >= y && mouseY < y + h;
    }

    boolean clickedIn(float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (clicked && hover(x, y, w, h)) {
            clickHandled = true;
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }

    /** Takes the wheel movement over a region, in lines (positive = towards the top). */
    float takeScroll(float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        if (scrollY == 0 || !hover(x, y, w, h)) {
            return 0;
        }
        float value = scrollY;
        scrollY = 0;
        return value;
    }

    static boolean shortcut(int mods) {
        return (mods & (Keys.MOD_CONTROL | Keys.MOD_SUPER)) != 0;
    }

    // ---- drawing ----

    void rect(float x, float y, float w, float h, int color) {
        batch.rect(x * scale, y * scale, w * scale, h * scale, color);
    }

    void round(float x, float y, float w, float h, float r, int color) {
        batch.roundRect(x * scale, y * scale, w * scale, h * scale, r * scale, color);
    }

    /** A rounded box with a one unit border. */
    void panel(float x, float y, float w, float h, float r, int fill, int border) {
        round(x, y, w, h, r, border);
        round(x + 1, y + 1, w - 2, h - 2, r - 1, fill);
    }

    /** Draws the batch's current image (see {@link Batch#image}) into the rectangle. */
    void image(float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        batch.image(x * scale, y * scale, w * scale, h * scale, 0xffffffff);
    }

    void clip(float x, float y, float w, float h) {
        batch.pushClip(x * scale, y * scale, w * scale, h * scale);
    }

    void unclip() {
        batch.popClip();
    }

    float lineHeight(Font font, float size) {
        return font.lineHeight(size * scale) / scale;
    }

    private static int replacement(Font font, int cp) {
        return font.has(cp) ? cp : '?';
    }

    float measure(Font font, float size, CharSequence text, int start, int end) {
        float px = size * scale;
        float width = 0;
        int prev = -1;
        for (int i = start; i < end; ) {
            int cp = replacement(font, Character.codePointAt(text, i));
            i += Character.charCount(Character.codePointAt(text, i));
            if (prev >= 0) {
                width += font.kern(prev, cp, px);
            }
            width += font.glyph(cp, px).advance;
            prev = cp;
        }
        return width / scale;
    }

    float measure(Font font, float size, CharSequence text) {
        return measure(font, size, text, 0, text.length());
    }

    /** Draws one line with its top at {@code y}; returns its width. */
    float text(Font font, float size, float x, float y, int color, CharSequence text, int start, int end) {
        float px = size * scale;
        float pen = x * scale;
        float baseline = Math.round(y * scale + font.ascent(px));
        int prev = -1;
        for (int i = start; i < end; ) {
            int raw = Character.codePointAt(text, i);
            i += Character.charCount(raw);
            if (raw == '\n') {
                continue;
            }
            int cp = replacement(font, raw);
            if (prev >= 0) {
                pen += font.kern(prev, cp, px);
            }
            Font.Glyph g = font.glyph(cp, px);
            if (g.w > 0) {
                float gx = Math.round(pen) + g.x0;
                float gy = baseline + g.y0;
                batch.glyph(gx, gy, gx + g.w, gy + g.h, g.u0, g.v0, g.u1, g.v1, color);
            }
            pen += g.advance;
            prev = cp;
        }
        return (pen - x * scale) / scale;
    }

    float text(Font font, float size, float x, float y, int color, CharSequence text) {
        return text(font, size, x, y, color, text, 0, text.length());
    }

    /** Draws text cut with an ellipsis to fit {@code maxWidth}. */
    void textFit(Font font, float size, float x, float y, float maxWidth, int color, String text) {
        if (measure(font, size, text) <= maxWidth) {
            text(font, size, x, y, color, text);
            return;
        }
        float ellipsis = measure(font, size, "…");
        int end = text.length();
        while (end > 0 && measure(font, size, text, 0, end) + ellipsis > maxWidth) {
            end = text.offsetByCodePoints(end, -1);
        }
        text(font, size, x, y, color, text.substring(0, end) + "…");
    }

    /**
     * Breaks text into lines of at most {@code maxWidth}: at newlines, after the last space that fits,
     * or mid-word for words longer than a line. Returns {start, end} pairs; end excludes the break.
     */
    List<int[]> wrap(Font font, float size, CharSequence text, float maxWidth) {
        List<int[]> lines = new ArrayList<>();
        float px = size * scale;
        float limit = maxWidth * scale;
        int lineStart = 0;
        int lastSpace = -1;
        float width = 0;
        int prev = -1;
        int i = 0;
        while (i < text.length()) {
            int cp = Character.codePointAt(text, i);
            int next = i + Character.charCount(cp);
            if (cp == '\n') {
                lines.add(new int[] {lineStart, i});
                lineStart = next;
                lastSpace = -1;
                width = 0;
                prev = -1;
                i = next;
                continue;
            }
            int drawn = replacement(font, cp);
            float advance = font.glyph(drawn, px).advance + (prev >= 0 ? font.kern(prev, drawn, px) : 0);
            if (width + advance > limit && i > lineStart) {
                if (cp == ' ') {
                    // A space at the break point disappears into it.
                    lines.add(new int[] {lineStart, i});
                    lineStart = next;
                    i = next;
                } else if (lastSpace >= lineStart) {
                    lines.add(new int[] {lineStart, lastSpace});
                    lineStart = lastSpace + 1;
                    i = lineStart;
                } else {
                    lines.add(new int[] {lineStart, i});
                    lineStart = i;
                }
                lastSpace = -1;
                width = 0;
                prev = -1;
                continue;
            }
            if (cp == ' ') {
                lastSpace = i;
            }
            width += advance;
            prev = drawn;
            i = next;
        }
        lines.add(new int[] {lineStart, text.length()});
        return lines;
    }

    // ---- widgets ----

    /** A button; returns true when clicked. */
    boolean button(float x, float y, float w, float h, String label, boolean primary) {
        boolean hot = hover(x, y, w, h);
        int fill = primary ? (hot ? 0xffb678ff : ACCENT) : (hot ? HOVER : RAISED);
        panel(x, y, w, h, 7, fill, primary ? fill : LINE);
        float tw = measure(bold, 13, label);
        text(bold, 13, x + (w - tw) / 2, y + (h - lineHeight(bold, 13)) / 2, primary ? 0xffffffff : FG, label);
        return clickedIn(x, y, w, h);
    }

    /** Width a {@link #button} needs for its label. */
    float buttonWidth(String label) {
        return measure(bold, 13, label) + 24;
    }

    /** A tab or toggle: accent when active. */
    boolean pill(float x, float y, float w, float h, String label, boolean active) {
        boolean hot = hover(x, y, w, h);
        if (active) {
            round(x, y, w, h, h / 2, 0x33a259f7);
        } else if (hot) {
            round(x, y, w, h, h / 2, HOVER);
        }
        float tw = measure(bold, 13, label);
        text(bold, 13, x + (w - tw) / 2, y + (h - lineHeight(bold, 13)) / 2, active ? 0xffd4b0ff : MUTED, label);
        return clickedIn(x, y, w, h);
    }

    boolean checkbox(float x, float y, String label, boolean value) {
        float box = 16;
        float tw = measure(regular, 13, label);
        boolean hot = hover(x, y, box + 8 + tw, box);
        panel(x, y, box, box, 4, value ? ACCENT : (hot ? HOVER : RAISED), value ? ACCENT : LINE);
        if (value) {
            round(x + 4.5f, y + 4.5f, 7, 7, 2, 0xffffffff);
        }
        text(regular, 13, x + box + 8, y + (box - lineHeight(regular, 13)) / 2, FG, label);
        return clickedIn(x, y, box + 8 + tw, box) ? !value : value;
    }

    /**
     * A text field editing {@code edit}. Single-line fields scroll horizontally, multi-line ones wrap
     * and scroll vertically. Returns true when the text changed this frame.
     */
    boolean field(TextEdit edit, float x, float y, float w, float h, boolean multiline, String placeholder) {
        boolean focused = focus == edit;
        boolean hot = hover(x, y, w, h);
        panel(x, y, w, h, 7, focused ? BASE : RAISED, focused ? 0xff7c4fc0 : (hot ? 0xff3a3256 : LINE));

        float size = 14;
        float lh = lineHeight(regular, size);
        float padX = 10;
        float padY = multiline ? 8 : (h - lh) / 2;
        float innerW = w - padX * 2;
        float innerH = h - padY * 2;

        boolean changed = false;
        if (focused) {
            changed = handleKeys(edit, multiline, innerW);
        }

        CharSequence text = edit.text;
        List<int[]> lines = multiline ? wrap(regular, size, text, innerW) : single(text);

        if (multiline) {
            float content = lines.size() * lh;
            edit.scroll -= takeScroll(x, y, w, h) * lh * 3;
            if (edit.caretMoved) {
                float caretY = lineOf(lines, edit.caret) * lh;
                edit.scroll = Math.max(Math.min(edit.scroll, caretY), caretY + lh - innerH);
            }
            edit.scroll = Math.max(0, Math.min(edit.scroll, Math.max(0, content - innerH)));
        } else if (edit.caretMoved) {
            float caretX = measure(regular, size, text, 0, edit.caret);
            edit.scroll = Math.max(Math.min(edit.scroll, caretX), caretX - innerW + 2);
            edit.scroll = Math.max(0, edit.scroll);
        }
        edit.caretMoved = false;

        float ox = x + padX - (multiline ? 0 : edit.scroll);
        float oy = y + padY - (multiline ? edit.scroll : 0);

        if (hot && clicked) {
            clickHandled = true;
            focus = edit;
            int at = hit(lines, text, size, lh, ox, oy, mouseX, mouseY);
            if (doubleClicked) {
                edit.selectWord(at);
            } else {
                edit.moveTo(at, false);
            }
            edit.dragging = true;
        } else if (edit.dragging && mouseDown) {
            edit.moveTo(hit(lines, text, size, lh, ox, oy, mouseX, mouseY), true);
        }
        if (!mouseDown) {
            edit.dragging = false;
        }

        clip(x + 2, y + 2, w - 4, h - 4);
        if (text.length() == 0 && !placeholder.isEmpty()) {
            text(regular, size, x + padX, y + padY, FAINT, placeholder);
        }
        int selStart = Math.min(edit.anchor, edit.caret);
        int selEnd = Math.max(edit.anchor, edit.caret);
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++) {
            int[] line = lines.get(i);
            float ly = oy + i * lh;
            if (ly + lh < y || ly > y + h) {
                continue;
            }
            if (focused && selEnd > selStart && selEnd >= line[0] && selStart <= line[1]) {
                int a = Math.max(selStart, line[0]);
                int b = Math.min(selEnd, line[1]);
                float sx = ox + measure(regular, size, text, line[0], a);
                float sw = measure(regular, size, text, a, b) + (selEnd > line[1] && i < lines.size() - 1 ? 4 : 0);
                rect(sx, ly, sw, lh, 0x666d3fc0);
            }
            text(regular, size, ox, ly, FG, text, line[0], line[1]);
        }
        if (focused && (System.currentTimeMillis() / 530) % 2 == 0) {
            int li = lineOf(lines, edit.caret);
            int[] line = lines.get(li);
            float cx = ox + measure(regular, size, text, line[0], Math.min(edit.caret, line[1]));
            rect(cx, oy + li * lh + 1, 1.5f, lh - 2, ACCENT);
        }
        unclip();
        return changed;
    }

    private static List<int[]> single(CharSequence text) {
        List<int[]> lines = new ArrayList<>(1);
        lines.add(new int[] {0, text.length()});
        return lines;
    }

    private static int lineOf(List<int[]> lines, int index) {
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size() - 1; i++) {
            if (index < lines.get(i + 1)[0]) {
                return i;
            }
        }
        return lines.size() - 1;
    }

    private int hit(List<int[]> lines, CharSequence text, float size, float lh, float ox, float oy, float mx, float my) {
        int li = (int) Math.floor((my - oy) / lh);
        li = Math.max(0, Math.min(lines.size() - 1, li));
        return indexAtX(lines.get(li), text, size, mx - ox);
    }

    private int indexAtX(int[] line, CharSequence text, float size, float x) {
        float pen = 0;
        for (int i = line[0]; i < line[1]; ) {
            int next = i + Character.charCount(Character.codePointAt(text, i));
            float w = measure(regular, size, text, i, next);
            if (x < pen + w / 2) {
                return i;
            }
            pen += w;
            i = next;
        }
        return line[1];
    }

    private boolean handleKeys(TextEdit edit, boolean multiline, float innerW) {
        boolean changed = false;
        for (int[] k : keys) {
            int key = k[0];
            int mods = k[1];
            boolean shift = (mods & Keys.MOD_SHIFT) != 0;
            boolean word = shortcut(mods) || (mods & Keys.MOD_ALT) != 0;
            switch (key) {
                case Keys.LEFT:
                    edit.left(word, shift);
                    break;
                case Keys.RIGHT:
                    edit.right(word, shift);
                    break;
                case Keys.UP:
                case Keys.DOWN:
                    if (multiline) {
                        List<int[]> lines = wrap(regular, 14, edit.text, innerW);
                        int li = lineOf(lines, edit.caret) + (key == Keys.UP ? -1 : 1);
                        if (li < 0) {
                            edit.moveTo(0, shift);
                        } else if (li >= lines.size()) {
                            edit.moveTo(edit.text.length(), shift);
                        } else {
                            int[] cur = lines.get(lineOf(lines, edit.caret));
                            float cx = measure(regular, 14, edit.text, cur[0], Math.min(edit.caret, cur[1]));
                            edit.moveTo(indexAtX(lines.get(li), edit.text, 14, cx), shift);
                        }
                    }
                    break;
                case Keys.HOME:
                    edit.moveTo(shortcut(mods) || !multiline ? 0 : edit.lineStart(), shift);
                    break;
                case Keys.END:
                    edit.moveTo(shortcut(mods) || !multiline ? edit.text.length() : edit.lineEnd(), shift);
                    break;
                case Keys.BACKSPACE:
                    changed |= edit.backspace(word);
                    break;
                case Keys.DELETE:
                    changed |= edit.delete(word);
                    break;
                case Keys.ENTER:
                case Keys.KP_ENTER:
                    if (multiline) {
                        edit.insert("\n");
                        changed = true;
                    }
                    break;
                case Keys.TAB:
                    if (multiline) {
                        edit.insert("    ");
                        changed = true;
                    }
                    break;
                case Keys.A:
                    if (shortcut(mods)) {
                        edit.selectAll();
                    }
                    break;
                case Keys.C:
                case Keys.X:
                    if (shortcut(mods) && edit.hasSelection()) {
                        platform.setClipboard(edit.selected());
                        if (key == Keys.X) {
                            edit.insert("");
                            changed = true;
                        }
                    }
                    break;
                case Keys.V:
                    if (shortcut(mods)) {
                        String pasted = platform.clipboard();
                        if (pasted != null && !pasted.isEmpty()) {
                            pasted = pasted.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace('\r', '\n');
                            edit.insert(multiline ? pasted : pasted.replace('\n', ' '));
                            changed = true;
                        }
                    }
                    break;
                default:
                    break;
            }
        }
        if (chars.length() > 0) {
            edit.insert(chars.toString());
            changed = true;
        }
        return changed;
    }
}

```

`src/main/java/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/VulkanBackend.java`:

```java
package dev.k0t1k.overlay.impl;

/**
 * The Vulkan renderer, kept behind an interface because it is compiled against a newer LWJGL (3.3)
 * than the rest of the overlay (3.1, for Minecraft 1.13) and is only loaded when the game uses Vulkan.
 */
interface VulkanBackend {
    /**
     * A hooked Vulkan call, answered as {@link Overlay#apply} does. {@code result} is the return value
     * on exit and null before the call.
     */
    Object call(String method, Object[] args, Object result, boolean exit);

    /** Stops drawing after a failure; later presents pass through untouched. */
    void disable();
}

```

`src/main/resources/dev/k0t1k/overlay/impl/media.ps1`:

```ps1
# Bridge between the overlay and Windows' system media controls (what Win+media keys drive: Spotify,
# browsers, players). Prints one line per change: "S<TAB>state<TAB>title<TAB>artist<TAB>app", or "N"
# when nothing plays. Reads commands from stdin: play, next, prev.
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
[Console]::OutputEncoding = New-Object System.Text.UTF8Encoding($false)
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Runtime.WindowsRuntime
$asTask = ([System.WindowsRuntimeSystemExtensions].GetMethods() | Where-Object {
    $_.Name -eq 'AsTask' -and $_.GetParameters().Count -eq 1 -and $_.GetParameters()[0].ParameterType.Name -eq 'IAsyncOperation`1'
})[0]
function Await($op, [Type]$type) {
    $task = $asTask.MakeGenericMethod($type).Invoke($null, @($op))
    $task.Wait(-1) | Out-Null
    $task.Result
}
$null = [Windows.Media.Control.GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionManager, Windows.Media.Control, ContentType = WindowsRuntime]
$manager = Await ([Windows.Media.Control.GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionManager]::RequestAsync()) ([Windows.Media.Control.GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionManager])
$propsType = [Windows.Media.Control.GlobalSystemMediaTransportControlsSessionMediaProperties]

# Console.In reads synchronously even through ReadLineAsync; a StreamReader over the raw stream does not.
$stdin = New-Object System.IO.StreamReader([Console]::OpenStandardInput())
$pending = $stdin.ReadLineAsync()
$last = ''
while ($true) {
    $session = $manager.GetCurrentSession()
    if ($pending.IsCompleted) {
        $command = $pending.Result
        if ($null -eq $command) { break }
        if ($session) {
            switch ($command.Trim()) {
                'play' { $null = $session.TryTogglePlayPauseAsync() }
                'next' { $null = $session.TrySkipNextAsync() }
                'prev' { $null = $session.TrySkipPreviousAsync() }
            }
        }
        $pending = $stdin.ReadLineAsync()
        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 250
        continue
    }
    $line = 'N'
    if ($session) {
        try {
            $props = Await ($session.TryGetMediaPropertiesAsync()) $propsType
            $state = $session.GetPlaybackInfo().PlaybackStatus.ToString()
            $clean = { param($s) if ($s) { $s -replace "[`t`r`n]", ' ' } else { '' } }
            $line = "S`t$state`t$(& $clean $props.Title)`t$(& $clean $props.Artist)`t$(& $clean $session.SourceAppUserModelId)"
        } catch {
            $line = 'N'
        }
    }
    if ($line -ne $last) {
        [Console]::Out.WriteLine($line)
        [Console]::Out.Flush()
        $last = $line
    }
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700
}

```

`src/main/shaders/overlay.frag`:

```frag
#version 450
layout(push_constant) uniform Frame {
    vec2 viewport;
    float srgb;
} frame;
layout(set = 0, binding = 0) uniform sampler2D atlas;
layout(location = 0) in vec2 vUv;
layout(location = 1) in vec4 vColor;
layout(location = 2) in vec4 vParams;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 fragColor;
void main() {
    float coverage;
    if (vParams.w > 0.5) {
        coverage = texture(atlas, vUv).r;
    } else {
        vec2 q = abs(vUv) - vParams.xy + vParams.z;
        float d = length(max(q, 0.0)) + min(max(q.x, q.y), 0.0) - vParams.z;
        coverage = clamp(0.5 - d, 0.0, 1.0);
    }
    vec3 rgb = vColor.rgb;
    if (frame.srgb > 0.5) {
        rgb = mix(rgb / 12.92, pow((rgb + 0.055) / 1.055, vec3(2.4)), step(0.04045, rgb));
    }
    fragColor = vec4(rgb, vColor.a * coverage);
}

```

`src/main/shaders/overlay.vert`:

```vert
#version 450
// Vulkan twin of the GL shader in GlRenderer; see Batch for the vertex layout.
layout(push_constant) uniform Frame {
    vec2 viewport;
    float srgb; // 1 when the swapchain stores sRGB, so colours must be written linear
} frame;
layout(location = 0) in vec2 aPos;
layout(location = 1) in vec2 aUv;
layout(location = 2) in vec4 aParams;
layout(location = 3) in vec4 aColor;
layout(location = 0) out vec2 vUv;
layout(location = 1) out vec4 vColor;
layout(location = 2) out vec4 vParams;
void main() {
    vUv = aUv;
    vColor = aColor;
    vParams = aParams;
    // Vulkan's clip space has y pointing down, like the batch's pixels.
    gl_Position = vec4(aPos / frame.viewport * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
}
```
